type Row = Record<string, unknown>;
type RepairSeverity = 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
type RepairCategory = 'ui' | 'agent' | 'data' | 'ops' | 'market';
type RepairCategoryFilter = 'all' | RepairCategory;
type RepairApprovalStatus = 'drafted' | 'confirmed' | 'dismissed';
type RepairQueueFilter = 'all' | 'high' | 'needs_action' | 'drafted' | 'confirmed' | 'dismissed';
type RepairPreflightKey = 'fieldContract' | 'budget' | 'evidence' | 'failureState' | 'manualConfirmation';

interface RepairCard {
  id: string;
  category: RepairCategory;
  severity: RepairSeverity;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  evidence: string[];
  allowed: string[];
  blocked: string[];
  suggestedFix: string;
  owner: string;
  readiness: number;
}

interface RepairApproval {
  status: RepairApprovalStatus;
  updatedAt: string;
}

interface RepairExecutionRecord {
  recordId: string;
  proposalId: string;
  cardId: string;
  title: string;
  status: RepairApprovalStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  fieldContractSummary: string;
  budgetSummary: string;
  evidenceSummary: string;
  failureStateSummary: string;
  manualConfirmationSummary: string;
  nextWriteTargets: string[];
}

interface RepairExecutionExportPayload {
  version: 'repair_proposal.local.v0';
  record: RepairExecutionRecord;
  source: {
    category: RepairCategory;
    severity: RepairSeverity;
    owner: string;
    readiness: number;
    evidence: string[];
    allowed: string[];
    blocked: string[];
  };
  proposal: RepairDryRunProposal;
  writeTargets: Array<{
    target: string;
    mode: 'future';
    requiresApproval: boolean;
  }>;
  guardrails: string[];
}

interface RepairExecutionQueueItem {

    nextStep: '下一阶段可以把实现包接入人工 Review 队列，但仍需人工确认后执行。',
  },
  {
    id: 'business-audit',
    status: 'blocked',
    label: '业务审计落库',
    metric: 'blocked',
    evidence: '当前 audit/task/evidence 都只有 preview，没有真实 audit_action 或任务记录。',
    nextStep: '先定义 actor、role、source_hash、reason、before/after，再开真实 audit 写入。',
  },
  {
    id: 'production-action',
    status: 'blocked',
    label: '生产动作',
    metric: '0 execution',
    evidence: 'provider run、migration、deploy、权限变更、LLM/Gemini 批量任务全部保持关闭。',
    nextStep: '真实生产动作必须走 backup-first、单项执行、可回滚和验收日志。',
  },
];

function asRecord(value: unknown): Row {
  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Row : {};
}

function repairRejectScopeLabel(scope: RepairRejectScope): string {
  const labels: Record<RepairRejectScope, string> = {
    proposal: '提案保存',
    cancel: '取消保存',
    preflight: '预检保存',
    implementation: '实现包保存',
    handoff: '交接保存',
    future_write: '未来写入',
  };
  return labels[scope];
}

function repairPersistenceRejectedFromError(
  error: unknown,
  scope: RepairRejectScope,
  targetId: string,
  title: string,
): RepairPersistenceRejectedReceipt | null {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiResponseError)) return null;
  const detail = asRecord(error.detail);
  if (String(detail.mode || '') !== 'repair_persistence_rejected_v0') return null;
  const occurredAt = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    id: `${scope}:${targetId}:${occurredAt}`,
    scope,
    targetId,
    title,
    status: error.status,
    mode: String(detail.mode || 'repair_persistence_rejected_v0'),
    reason: String(detail.reason || error.message || 'Repair Center 当前禁止真实保存。'),
    nextAction: String(detail.nextAction || detail.next_action || '先完成备份、迁移、RBAC、审计写入和 rollback 审批。'),
    persist: Boolean(detail.persist),
    writeDb: Boolean(detail.writeDb ?? detail.write_db),
    occurredAt,
  };
}

function numberValue(value: unknown): number {
  const parsed = Number(value ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
}

function compact(value: number): string {
  if (Math.abs(value) >= 1_000_000) return `${(value / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '')}M`;
  if (Math.abs(value) >= 10_000) return `${(value / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '')}K`;
  return Math.round(value).toLocaleString('en-US');
}

function defaultRepairPreflightState(): RepairPreflightState {
  return repairPreflightDefinitions.reduce((acc, item) => {
    acc[item.key] = false;
    return acc;
  }, {} as RepairPreflightState);
}

function buildRepairPreflightPayload(state: RepairPreflightState): VkpiRepairPreflightStatePayload {
  return {
    fieldContract: Boolean(state.fieldContract),
    budget: Boolean(state.budget),
    evidence: Boolean(state.evidence),
    failureState: Boolean(state.failureState),
    manualConfirmation: Boolean(state.manualConfirmation),
  };
}

function repairPreflightItems(record: RepairExecutionRecord): RepairPreflightItem[] {
  return [
    { key: 'fieldContract', label: '字段契约', summary: record.fieldContractSummary || '字段来源、目标字段和空值策略待确认。' },
    { key: 'budget', label: '预算规则', summary: record.budgetSummary || '成本上限、调用频率和降级策略待确认。' },
    { key: 'evidence', label: '证据回链', summary: record.evidenceSummary || '证据来源、回链和新鲜度待确认。' },
    { key: 'failureState', label: '失败状态', summary: record.failureStateSummary || '失败、部分成功和不可用状态待确认。' },
    { key: 'manualConfirmation', label: '人工确认', summary: record.manualConfirmationSummary || '责任人、审批口径和执行边界待确认。' },
  ];
}

function repairPreflightComplete(state?: RepairPreflightState): boolean {
  return Boolean(state && repairPreflightDefinitions.every((item) => state[item.key]));
}

function repairPreflightDoneCount(state?: RepairPreflightState): number {
  return state ? repairPreflightDefinitions.filter((item) => state[item.key]).length : 0;
}

function isRepairApprovalStatus(value: unknown): value is RepairApprovalStatus {
  return value === 'drafted' || value === 'confirmed' || value === 'dismissed';
}

function asStringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map((item) => String(item || '')).filter(Boolean) : [];
}

function repairPersistencePreviewFrom(response?: RepairPersistencePreviewCarrier | null): VkpiRepairPersistencePreview | null {
  return response?.persistencePreview || response?.persistence_preview || null;
}

function repairPersistenceRows(preview?: VkpiRepairPersistencePreview | null) {
  return Array.isArray(preview?.rows) ? preview.rows : [];
}

function repairPreviewRowKey(row: Row, index: number): string {
  return `${String(row.table || 'table')}:${String(row.keyField || row.key_field || index)}`;
}

function shortHash(value: unknown): string {
  const text = String(value || '');
  return text.length > 14 ? `${text.slice(0, 10)}...${text.slice(-4)}` : text || '-';
}

function isRepairCategory(value: unknown): value is RepairCategory {
  return value === 'ui' || value === 'agent' || value === 'data' || value === 'ops' || value === 'market';
}

function isRepairSeverity(value: unknown): value is RepairSeverity {
  return value === 'high' || value === 'medium' || value === 'low';
}

function repairCardFromBackendProposal(proposal: VkpiRepairProposalRecord, sourceLabel = 'backend fixture'): RepairCard | null {
  const proposalId = String(proposal.proposalId || proposal.proposal_id || '').trim();
  const cardId = String(proposal.cardId || proposal.card_id || proposalId || '').trim();
  const title = String(proposal.title || '').trim();
  if (!cardId || !title) return null;
  const categoryRaw = proposal.category;
  const severityRaw = proposal.severity;
  const fieldContract = String(proposal.fieldContractSummary || proposal.field_contract_summary || '').trim();
  const budget = String(proposal.budgetSummary || proposal.budget_summary || '').trim();
  const evidenceSummary = String(proposal.evidenceSummary || proposal.evidence_summary || '').trim();
  const failureState = String(proposal.failureStateSummary || proposal.failure_state_summary || '').trim();
  const manualConfirmation = String(proposal.manualConfirmationSummary || proposal.manual_confirmation_summary || '').trim();
  const targetHints = asStringArray(proposal.nextWriteTargets || proposal.next_write_targets);
  const bodyParts = [fieldContract, evidenceSummary, failureState].filter(Boolean);
  return {
    id: `repair-api-${(proposalId || cardId).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').toLowerCase()}`,
    category: isRepairCategory(categoryRaw) ? categoryRaw : 'ops',
    severity: isRepairSeverity(severityRaw) ? severityRaw : 'medium',
    title,
    body: bodyParts.join(' ') || '后端只读 Repair proposal 已返回，当前仅用于 UI 接线和人工复核。',
    evidence: [
      `API: /api/admin/vkpi/repair/proposals`,
      `source: ${sourceLabel}`,
      fieldContract ? `字段契约: ${fieldContract}` : '',
      evidenceSummary ? `证据: ${evidenceSummary}` : '',
      targetHints.length ? `未来写入: ${targetHints.join(' / ')}` : '',
    ].filter(Boolean),
    allowed: [
      '读取后端 fixture',
      '本地提案',
      '五项预检',
      '人工确认',
    ],
    blocked: [
      budget || '外部 API 默认关闭',
      failureState || '失败状态必须显式展示',
      '自动执行修复',
      '生产部署',
    ],
    suggestedFix: manualConfirmation || '先把 proposal 接入审计/任务草稿前的只读流程，真实写入后置。',
    owner: String(proposal.owner || '待分配'),
    readiness: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, numberValue(proposal.readiness))),
  };
}

function repairCardsFromBackendResponse(response: VkpiRepairProposalsResponse | null): RepairCard[] {
  const proposals = Array.isArray(response?.proposals) ? response.proposals : [];
  const sourceLabel = String(response?.source || response?.mode || 'backend fixture');
  return proposals
    .map((proposal) => repairCardFromBackendProposal(proposal, sourceLabel))
    .filter((card): card is RepairCard => Boolean(card));
}

function sanitizeRepairExecutionRecord(value: unknown): RepairExecutionRecord | null {
  const row = asRecord(value);
  const cardId = String(row.cardId || '');
  const status = row.status;
  if (!cardId || !isRepairApprovalStatus(status)) return null;
  return {
    recordId: String(row.recordId || repairRecordId(cardId)),
    proposalId: String(row.proposalId || repairProposalId(cardId)),
    cardId,
    title: String(row.title || cardId),
    status,
    createdAt: String(row.createdAt || row.updatedAt || new Date().toISOString()),
    updatedAt: String(row.updatedAt || new Date().toISOString()),
    fieldContractSummary: String(row.fieldContractSummary || ''),
    budgetSummary: String(row.budgetSummary || ''),
    evidenceSummary: String(row.evidenceSummary || ''),
    failureStateSummary: String(row.failureStateSummary || ''),
    manualConfirmationSummary: String(row.manualConfirmationSummary || ''),
    nextWriteTargets: asStringArray(row.nextWriteTargets),
  };
}

function readRepairExecutionStorage(): RepairExecutionStoragePayload | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(REPAIR_EXECUTION_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = asRecord(JSON.parse(raw));
    if (parsed.version !== 'repair_execution_records.local.v0') return null;
    const storedRecords = asRecord(parsed.records);
    const records: Record<string, RepairExecutionRecord> = {};
    Object.values(storedRecords).forEach((value) => {
      const record = sanitizeRepairExecutionRecord(value);
      if (record) records[record.cardId] = record;
    });
    const count = Object.keys(records).length;
    return {
      version: 'repair_execution_records.local.v0',
      updatedAt: String(parsed.updatedAt || ''),
      count,
      records,
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function writeRepairExecutionStorage(records: Record<string, RepairExecutionRecord>): RepairExecutionStoragePayload | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const payload: RepairExecutionStoragePayload = {
      version: 'repair_execution_records.local.v0',
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      count: Object.keys(records).length,
      records,
    };
    window.localStorage.setItem(REPAIR_EXECUTION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
    return payload;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function clearRepairExecutionStorage() {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.localStorage.removeItem(REPAIR_EXECUTION_STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // localStorage can be blocked by browser privacy settings; clearing local UI state still works.
  }
}

function buildRepairApprovalsFromRecords(records: Record<string, RepairExecutionRecord>): Record<string, RepairApproval> {
  return Object.values(records).reduce((acc, record) => {
    acc[record.cardId] = { status: record.status, updatedAt: record.updatedAt };
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, RepairApproval>);
}

function buildRepairStatusOverridesFromRecords(records: Record<string, RepairExecutionRecord>): Record<string, IntelligenceCardStatus> {
  return Object.values(records).reduce((acc, record) => {
    if (record.status === 'confirmed') acc[record.cardId] = 'accepted';
    if (record.status === 'dismissed') acc[record.cardId] = 'rejected';
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, IntelligenceCardStatus>);
}

function severityLabel(value: RepairSeverity): string {
  if (value === 'high') return '高';
  if (value === 'medium') return '中';
  return '低';
}

function categoryLabel(value: RepairCategory): string {
  const labels: Record<RepairCategory, string> = {
    ui: '界面',
    agent: 'Agent',
    data: '数据',
    ops: '运维',
    market: '市场',
  };
  return labels[value];
}

function formatTime(value?: string): string {
  if (!value) return '-';
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;
  return date.toLocaleString('zh-CN', { month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
}

function approvalLabel(value?: RepairApproval): string {
  if (!value) return '未确认';
  if (value.status === 'drafted') return '已生成本地提案';
  if (value.status === 'confirmed') return '人工已确认';
  return '已忽略';
}

function repairProposalStatusFromApproval(approval?: RepairApproval): string {
  if (approval?.status === 'confirmed') return 'preflight_ready';
  return 'proposed';
}

function repairStatus(card: RepairCard, approval?: RepairApproval, statusOverride?: IntelligenceCardStatus): IntelligenceCardStatus {
  return statusOverride || (approval?.status === 'confirmed' ? 'accepted' : card.severity === 'high' ? 'blocked' : 'open');
}

function matchesQueueFilter(
  card: RepairCard,
  approval: RepairApproval | undefined,
  statusOverride: IntelligenceCardStatus | undefined,
  filter: RepairQueueFilter,
): boolean {
  const status = repairStatus(card, approval, statusOverride);
  if (filter === 'all') return true;
  if (filter === 'high') return card.severity === 'high';
  if (filter === 'drafted') return approval?.status === 'drafted';
  if (filter === 'confirmed') return approval?.status === 'confirmed' || status === 'accepted';
  if (filter === 'dismissed') return approval?.status === 'dismissed' || status === 'rejected';
  return !approval && (card.severity === 'high' || card.severity === 'medium' || status === 'blocked');
}

function likelyFileEvidence(items: string[]): string[] {
  return items.filter((item) => /(\.tsx|\.ts|\.css|\.py|\.md|\/v\d|\/api|dashboard\/|market-|agents\/)/i.test(item));
}

function fallbackFilesForCategory(category: RepairCategory): string[] {
  const files: Record<RepairCategory, string[]> = {
    ui: [
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/DashboardPremium.tsx',
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css',
    ],
    agent: [
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/IntelligenceCenterPage.tsx',
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx',
    ],
    data: [
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx',
      'frontend/src/services/vkpi.ui-api.ts',
    ],
    market: [
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/DashboardPremium.tsx',
      'frontend/src/services/vkpi.ui-api.ts',
    ],
    ops: [
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx',
      'frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/WorkspacePage.tsx',
    ],
  };
  return files[category];
}

function repairProposalId(cardId: string): string {
  return `DRY-${cardId.replace(/^repair-/, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').slice(0, 42).toUpperCase()}`;
}

function repairRecordId(cardId: string): string {
  return `LOCAL-${repairProposalId(cardId).replace(/^DRY-/, '')}`;
}

function buildRepairExecutionRecord(
  card: RepairCard,
  status: RepairApprovalStatus,
  previous?: RepairExecutionRecord,
): RepairExecutionRecord {
  const checklist = integrationChecklistForCard(card);
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    recordId: previous?.recordId || repairRecordId(card.id),
    proposalId: repairProposalId(card.id),
    cardId: card.id,
    title: card.title,
    status,
    createdAt: previous?.createdAt || now,
    updatedAt: now,
    fieldContractSummary: checklist.dataFields.slice(0, 6).join(' / '),
    budgetSummary: checklist.budgetRules.slice(0, 4).join(' / '),
    evidenceSummary: checklist.evidenceBacklinks.slice(0, 5).join(' / '),
    failureStateSummary: checklist.failureStates.slice(0, 5).join(' / '),
    manualConfirmationSummary: checklist.manualConfirmations.slice(0, 4).join(' / '),
    nextWriteTargets: [
      'future: vkpi_repair_proposals',
      'future: audit_action repair_proposal',
      'future: task draft / project action',
      'future: evidence refs',
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairExecutionExportPayload(
  card: RepairCard,
  record: RepairExecutionRecord,
  proposal: RepairDryRunProposal,
): RepairExecutionExportPayload {
  return {
    version: 'repair_proposal.local.v0',
    record,
    source: {
      category: card.category,
      severity: card.severity,
      owner: card.owner,
      readiness: card.readiness,
      evidence: card.evidence,
      allowed: card.allowed,
      blocked: card.blocked,
    },
    proposal,
    writeTargets: record.nextWriteTargets.map((target) => ({
      target,
      mode: 'future',
      requiresApproval: true,
    })),
    guardrails: [
      'local_only',
      'no_provider_run',
      'no_database_write',
      'no_migration',
      'no_deploy',
      'human_approval_required',
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairProposalCreatePayload(card: RepairCard): VkpiRepairProposalCreatePayload {
  const record = buildRepairExecutionRecord(card, 'drafted');
  return {
    version: 'repair_proposal.local.v0',
    cardId: card.id,
    title: card.title,
    category: card.category,
    severity: card.severity,
    owner: card.owner,
    readiness: card.readiness,
    fieldContractSummary: record.fieldContractSummary,
    budgetSummary: record.budgetSummary,
    evidenceSummary: record.evidenceSummary,
    failureStateSummary: record.failureStateSummary,
    manualConfirmationSummary: record.manualConfirmationSummary,
    nextWriteTargets: ['audit_action', 'task_draft', 'evidence_refs'],
  };
}

function buildRepairExecutionQueueExportPayload(items: RepairExecutionQueueItem[]): RepairExecutionQueueExportPayload {
  return {
    version: 'repair_proposal_queue.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    count: items.length,
    confirmedCount: items.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'confirmed').length,
    dismissedCount: items.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'dismissed').length,
    records: items.map((item) => item.payload),
    guardrails: [
      'local_only',
      'queue_preview_only',
      'no_provider_run',
      'no_database_write',
      'no_migration',
      'no_deploy',
      'human_approval_required',
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairImplementationPreview(
  card: RepairCard,
  record: RepairExecutionRecord,
  approval?: RepairApproval,
): RepairImplementationPreview {
  const detail = repairPackageFromCard(card, approval);
  const proposal = dryRunProposalFromCard(card, approval);
  return {
    packageId: `IMPL-${record.proposalId.replace(/^DRY-/, '')}`,
    statusLabel: record.status === 'confirmed' ? '人工确认后可设计实现' : '需要人工确认后再执行',
    candidateFiles: detail.targetFiles,
    patchOrder: [
      `备份/确认工作区：${record.proposalId}`,
      ...detail.executionSteps,
      '只提交本轮文件范围内的最小 patch。',
      '记录验收输出，不自动部署到生产。',
    ],
    validationCommands: [
      'npm run build',
      'git diff --check',
      ...detail.validation,
    ],
    rollbackBoundary: [
      ...proposal.rollback,
      '若涉及后端/数据库/provider，必须另开审批轮并先备份。',
      '本实现包当前只读预览，不自动执行任何回滚命令。',
    ],
    blockedActions: Array.from(new Set([
      ...detail.noGo,
      '自动执行 patch',
      '自动部署',
      '自动运行 provider / LLM / Gemini',
    ])),
  };
}

function repairImplementationReviewSummary(
  source: Pick<RepairCard, 'category' | 'severity' | 'owner' | 'readiness'>,
  preview: Pick<RepairImplementationPreview, 'candidateFiles' | 'rollbackBoundary'>,
  preflightPassed: boolean,
): RepairImplementationReviewSummary {
  const riskTone: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low' = source.severity === 'high' || source.readiness < 50
    ? 'high'
    : source.severity === 'medium' || source.readiness < 70
      ? 'medium'
      : 'low';
  const touchesBackend = preview.candidateFiles.some((file) => /backend|api|service|migration|alembic|sql|schema/i.test(file));
  const needsMigration = touchesBackend || source.category === 'data' || source.category === 'ops' || source.category === 'agent';
  const needsProduct = source.category === 'ui' || source.category === 'market';

  return {
    riskLabel: riskTone === 'high' ? '高风险' : riskTone === 'medium' ? '中风险' : '低风险',
    riskTone,
    migrationLabel: needsMigration ? '需要 migration / 写入契约 review' : '本轮不需要 migration',
    migrationNeeded: needsMigration,
    confirmationLabel: needsProduct ? '需要产品/设计确认' : '需要数据/后端确认',
    confirmationOwner: source.owner,
    preflightLabel: preflightPassed ? '5/5 已确认' : 'Preflight 未完成',
  };
}

function repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry): RepairControlledExecutionItem {
  const review = repairImplementationReviewSummary(entry, entry.payload.implementation, entry.preflightPassed);
  const hasMinimumEvidence = entry.candidateFiles.length > 0 && entry.validationCommands.length > 0 && entry.guardrails.length > 0;
  const sourceStatus = entry.payload.record.status;

  if (!entry.preflightPassed || !hasMinimumEvidence || entry.readiness < 45) {
    return {
      entry,
      review,
      status: 'needs_evidence',
      statusLabel: '需补证据',
      rationale: !entry.preflightPassed
        ? '执行前检查未全部确认，不能进入实现执行。'
        : '缺少影响文件、验收命令或 guardrail 证据。',
      nextGate: '补齐 preflight、影响文件、验收命令和证据回链。',
    };
  }

  if (review.riskTone === 'high' || review.migrationNeeded) {
    return {
      entry,
      review,
      status: 'blocked',
      statusLabel: '禁止执行',
      rationale: review.riskTone === 'high'
        ? '高风险实现包不能从 Repair Center 直接执行。'
        : '涉及后端/数据/Agent/运维范围，必须先做 migration 或写入契约 review。',
      nextGate: '另开受控轮次，先完成备份、migration review、rollback SQL 和人工审批。',
    };
  }

  if (sourceStatus === 'confirmed' && review.riskTone === 'low' && entry.readiness >= 70) {
    return {
      entry,
      review,
      status: 'confirmed',
      statusLabel: '已确认',
      rationale: '人工已确认且风险较低，可作为下一轮最小 patch 候选。',
      nextGate: '进入人工执行计划，但仍需 backup-first、diff review 和构建验收。',
    };
  }

  return {
    entry,
    review,
    status: 'pending_review',
    statusLabel: '待 review',
    rationale: '实现包已生成，但还需要人工确认风险、范围和验收方式。',
    nextGate: '由负责人确认影响文件、验收命令、回滚边界和是否需要产品/设计确认。',
  };
}

function buildRepairExecutionEvidencePackage(item: RepairControlledExecutionItem): RepairExecutionEvidencePackagePayload {
  const implementation = item.entry.payload.implementation;
  return {
    version: 'repair_execution_evidence_package.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    mode: 'preview_only',
    status: item.status,
    statusLabel: item.statusLabel,
    rationale: item.rationale,
    nextGate: item.nextGate,
    source: {
      logId: item.entry.logId,
      packageId: item.entry.packageId,
      proposalId: item.entry.proposalId,
      cardId: item.entry.cardId,
      title: item.entry.title,
      category: item.entry.category,
      severity: item.entry.severity,
      owner: item.entry.owner,
      readiness: item.entry.readiness,
    },
    review: item.review,
    impact: {
      candidateFiles: item.entry.candidateFiles,
      validationCommands: item.entry.validationCommands,
      rollbackBoundary: implementation.rollbackBoundary,
      guardrails: item.entry.guardrails,
    },
    noGo: [
      'no_code_execution_from_repair_center',
      'no_database_write',
      'no_task_enqueue',
      'no_provider_run',
      'no_llm_or_gemini_batch',
      'no_deploy',
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairMinimumExecutionGates(
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined,
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined,
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[],
): RepairMinimumExecutionGate[] {
  const controlledItems = entries.map((entry) => repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry));
  const confirmedCount = controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'confirmed').length;
  const blockedCount = controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'blocked').length;
  const needsEvidenceCount = controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'needs_evidence').length;
  const pendingCount = controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'pending_review').length;
  const closureScore = numberValue(closureStatus?.closureScore ?? closureStatus?.closure_score);
  const readinessScore = numberValue(readiness?.readinessScore ?? readiness?.readiness_score);
  const blockedGateCount = numberValue(readiness?.blockedCount ?? readiness?.blocked_count);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(
    (closureStatus?.writeAllowed ?? closureStatus?.write_allowed)
    || (readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed),
  );

  return [
    {
      id: 'candidate-package',
      status: confirmedCount > 0 ? 'ready' : entries.length ? 'review' : 'blocked',
      label: '候选实现包',
      metric: `${compact(confirmedCount)} confirmed / ${compact(entries.length)} total`,
      evidence: entries.length
        ? `已有 ${compact(entries.length)} 个 handoff 包，${compact(pendingCount)} 个待 review。`
        : '还没有复制实现包，无法进入执行轮。',
      nextGate: confirmedCount > 0 ? '只允许作为下一轮候选，不自动执行。' : '先完成本地提案、preflight、实现包和人工确认。',
    },
    {
      id: 'blocker-clean',
      status: blockedCount || needsEvidenceCount ? 'blocked' : pendingCount ? 'review' : 'ready',
      label: '队列阻塞项',
      metric: `${compact(blockedCount)} blocked / ${compact(needsEvidenceCount)} evidence`,
      evidence: blockedCount || needsEvidenceCount
        ? '队列里仍有禁止执行或需补证据的包。'
        : pendingCount
          ? '没有硬阻塞，但仍有包未完成人工 review。'
          : '当前候选队列没有阻塞项。',
      nextGate: blockedCount || needsEvidenceCount
        ? '逐条补证据或另开 migration / 写入契约 review。'
        : pendingCount
          ? '人工确认范围、风险、文件和验收命令。'
          : '保持单包、单轮、可回滚。',
    },
    {
      id: 'persistence-gate',
      status: writeAllowed && blockedGateCount === 0 ? 'ready' : 'blocked',
      label: '真实写入 Gate',
      metric: writeAllowed ? 'write allowed' : 'write blocked',
      evidence: writeAllowed
        ? `后端 gate 当前允许写入，阻塞 gate=${compact(blockedGateCount)}。`
        : `真实保存仍关闭，阻塞 gate=${compact(blockedGateCount)}。`,
      nextGate: writeAllowed && blockedGateCount === 0
        ? '执行前仍需备份和最小 diff review。'
        : '补齐 backup、migration、RBAC、audit、rollback 后再开真实保存。',
    },
    {
      id: 'closure-score',
      status: closureScore >= 90 ? 'ready' : closureScore >= 75 ? 'review' : 'blocked',
      label: '闭环成熟度',
      metric: closureStatus ? `${compact(closureScore)}%` : '-',
      evidence: closureStatus
        ? '闭环状态来自后端只读 closure endpoint。'
        : '未读取到后端 closure 状态。',
      nextGate: closureScore >= 90 ? '保持验收日志和回滚边界。' : '先补齐 preview-only 和 blocked 环节说明。',
    },
    {
      id: 'persistence-readiness',
      status: readinessScore >= 90 && blockedGateCount === 0 ? 'ready' : readinessScore >= 70 ? 'review' : 'blocked',
      label: '持久化成熟度',
      metric: readiness ? `${compact(readinessScore)}%` : '-',
      evidence: readiness
        ? '持久化状态来自后端 readiness endpoint。'
        : '未读取到持久化 readiness。',
      nextGate: readinessScore >= 90 && blockedGateCount === 0
        ? '允许进入写入审批讨论。'
        : '继续保持 no-write，先补 migration / rollback / audit 契约。',
    },
    {
      id: 'backup-rollback',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '备份与回滚',
      metric: 'not verified',
      evidence: '本页面没有读取到本轮新备份、回滚演练或生产执行日志。',
      nextGate: '任何真实执行前必须先备份、确认 rollback SQL/步骤，并记录验收输出。',
    },
    {
      id: 'side-effect-control',
      status: 'ready',
      label: '外部副作用控制',
      metric: '0 auto run',
      evidence: 'Repair Center 当前没有 provider、LLM、Gemini、deploy、sync 的自动执行入口。',
      nextGate: '继续保持外部 API 和生产动作独立审批。',
    },
  ];
}

function buildRepairPreExecutionRunbookSteps(
  gates: RepairMinimumExecutionGate[],
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[],
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[],
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined,
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined,
): RepairPreExecutionRunbookStep[] {
  const controlledItems = entries.map((entry) => repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry));
  const confirmedControlledCount = controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'confirmed').length;
  const blockedGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'blocked').length;
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(
    (closureStatus?.writeAllowed ?? closureStatus?.write_allowed)
    || (readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed),
  );
  const hasValidation = entries.some((entry) => entry.validationCommands.length > 0);

  return [
    {
      id: 'backup-snapshot',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '备份快照',
      owner: 'Ops / DBA',
      evidence: '页面没有读取到本轮 DB/R2/env 备份记录，也没有远程部署前快照。',
      acceptance: '真实执行前必须记录备份路径、时间、负责人和可恢复对象。',
    },
    {
      id: 'rollback-plan',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '回滚计划',
      owner: '执行负责人',
      evidence: '当前只有实现包里的 rollbackBoundary 草案，还没有实操回滚命令或 SQL。',
      acceptance: '每个候选包必须有可执行 rollback 步骤，且不依赖事后猜测。',
    },
    {
      id: 'diff-scope-review',
      status: entries.length ? 'required' : 'blocked',
      label: 'Diff 范围复核',
      owner: '前端 / 后端 owner',
      evidence: entries.length
        ? `已有 ${compact(entries.length)} 个实现包候选，仍需人工逐个确认影响文件。`
        : '还没有实现包候选，无法复核 diff 范围。',
      acceptance: '确认只做一个最小包，不混入 deploy、provider sync、LLM/Gemini 或 schema 写入。',
    },
    {
      id: 'write-contract-review',
      status: writeAllowed ? 'required' : 'blocked',
      label: '写入契约复核',
      owner: 'Backend / Data',
      evidence: writeAllowed ? '后端 gate 显示可讨论写入，但仍需 migration/RBAC/audit review。' : '真实写入 gate 仍关闭。',
      acceptance: 'migration、RBAC、audit_action、rollback SQL 和失败状态全部明确后才能开写。',
    },
    {
      id: 'validation-command-review',
      status: hasValidation ? 'required' : 'blocked',
      label: '验收命令',
      owner: '执行负责人',
      evidence: hasValidation ? '实现包已携带 validationCommands。' : '缺少可复制验收命令。',
      acceptance: '最少包含 build/test/diff-check，并记录失败时停止条件。',
    },
    {
      id: 'human-signoff',
      status: confirmedControlledCount > 0 ? 'required' : 'blocked',
      label: '人工签核',
      owner: '业务 owner + 技术 owner',
      evidence: confirmedControlledCount > 0
        ? `${compact(confirmedControlledCount)} 个候选包可进入人工签核。`
        : '没有 confirmed 候选包。',
      acceptance: '负责人确认目标、风险、验收输出和下一步位置后，才允许另开执行轮。',
    },
    {
      id: 'side-effect-freeze',
      status: blockedGateCount === 0 && executionQueueItems.length > 0 ? 'required' : 'ready',
      label: '外部副作用冻结',
      owner: 'Ops',
      evidence: 'Repair Center 没有 provider、LLM、Gemini、deploy、sync 的自动执行入口。',
      acceptance: '执行轮仍必须手工确认不触发外部 API，不恢复 timer，不跑批量采集。',
    },
  ];
}

function buildRepairPreExecutionStatePackage({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairPreExecutionStatePackagePayload {
  const gates = buildRepairMinimumExecutionGates(closureStatus, readiness, entries);
  const runbook = buildRepairPreExecutionRunbookSteps(gates, entries, executionQueueItems, closureStatus, readiness);
  const controlledItems = entries.map((entry) => repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry));
  const readyGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'ready').length;
  const reviewGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'review').length;
  const blockedGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'blocked').length;
  const decision: 'go_candidate' | 'no_go' = blockedGateCount === 0 && reviewGateCount === 0 ? 'go_candidate' : 'no_go';
  const closureScore = numberValue(closureStatus?.closureScore ?? closureStatus?.closure_score);
  const persistenceReadinessScore = numberValue(readiness?.readinessScore ?? readiness?.readiness_score);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(
    (closureStatus?.writeAllowed ?? closureStatus?.write_allowed)
    || (readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed),
  );

  return {
    version: 'repair_pre_execution_state.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_pre_execution',
    decision,
    summary: {
      apiMode: hasApiToken ? 'authenticated_readonly' : 'local_only',
      repairCandidateCount,
      localProposalCount: executionQueueItems.length,
      handoffPackageCount: entries.length,
      closureScore,
      persistenceReadinessScore,
      writeAllowed,
      readyGateCount,
      reviewGateCount,
      blockedGateCount,
    },
    gates,
    runbook,
    controlledQueue: {
      count: controlledItems.length,
      confirmedCount: controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'confirmed').length,
      blockedCount: controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'blocked').length,
      needsEvidenceCount: controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'needs_evidence').length,
      pendingReviewCount: controlledItems.filter((item) => item.status === 'pending_review').length,
      items: controlledItems.map((item) => ({
        logId: item.entry.logId,
        packageId: item.entry.packageId,
        title: item.entry.title,
        status: item.status,
        rationale: item.rationale,
        nextGate: item.nextGate,
        candidateFiles: item.entry.candidateFiles,
        validationCommands: item.entry.validationCommands,
      })),
    },
    executionQueue: {
      count: executionQueueItems.length,
      confirmedCount: executionQueueItems.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'confirmed').length,
      records: executionQueueItems.map((item) => ({
        recordId: item.record.recordId,
        proposalId: item.record.proposalId,
        cardId: item.record.cardId,
        title: item.record.title,
        status: item.record.status,
        updatedAt: item.record.updatedAt,
      })),
    },
    noGo: [
      'no_database_write',
      'no_provider_sync',
      'no_llm_or_gemini_run',
      'no_deploy',
      'no_timer_resume',
      'no_migration_without_backup_and_rollback',
    ],
    nextStep: decision === 'go_candidate'
      ? '另开人工执行轮，先备份，再做最小 diff review。'
      : '继续补齐 blocked/review gate，不进入真实执行轮。',
  };
}

function repairV0ClosureRounds(): RepairV0ClosureRound[] {
  return [
    {
      round: 'R83',
      status: 'done',
      title: '验收锚点',
      output: '页面级 acceptance anchors',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r83-acceptance-checkpoints.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R84',
      status: 'done',
      title: '人工 Review 交接',
      output: 'implementation review summary + handoff card',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r84-human-review-handoff.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R85',
      status: 'done',
      title: '受控执行队列',
      output: 'controlled queue, no auto execution',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r85-controlled-execution-queue.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R86',
      status: 'done',
      title: '执行证据包',
      output: 'copyable evidence package JSON',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r86-pre-execution-evidence-package.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R87',
      status: 'done',
      title: '最小执行准入',
      output: 'GO/NO-GO readiness gates',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r87-minimum-execution-readiness.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R88',
      status: 'done',
      title: '执行前 Runbook',
      output: 'pre-execution state package',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r88-pre-execution-runbook.md',
    },
    {
      round: 'R89',
      status: 'active',
      title: 'v0 闭环报告',
      output: 'read-only closure report package',
      evidence: 'docs/vkpi/repair-center-r89-v0-closure-report.md',
    },
  ];
}

function buildRepairV0ClosureReport({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairV0ClosureReportPayload {
  const preExecutionState = buildRepairPreExecutionStatePackage({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const rounds = repairV0ClosureRounds();
  const artifacts: RepairV0ClosureReportPayload['artifacts'] = [
    {
      id: 'acceptance-anchors',
      label: '验收锚点',
      purpose: '让当前阶段每个能力都有可见验收输出。',
      status: 'ready',
    },
    {
      id: 'implementation-handoff-log',
      label: '实现包交接历史',
      purpose: '记录复制过的实现包，供后续人工执行轮复核。',
      status: entries.length ? 'ready' : 'preview_only',
    },
    {
      id: 'execution-evidence-package',
      label: '执行证据包',
      purpose: '将单个候选包的影响文件、验收命令和 guardrail 打包。',
      status: entries.length ? 'ready' : 'preview_only',
    },
    {
      id: 'minimum-readiness',
      label: '最小执行准入',
      purpose: '汇总 GO/NO-GO gate，阻止无备份或无回滚的执行。',
      status: preExecutionState.decision === 'go_candidate' ? 'ready' : 'blocked',
    },
    {
      id: 'pre-execution-runbook',
      label: '执行前 Runbook',
      purpose: '导出当前状态包，供真实执行轮前人工确认。',
      status: 'preview_only',
    },
  ];
  const openBlockers = [
    ...preExecutionState.gates
      .filter((gate) => gate.status !== 'ready')
      .map((gate) => `${gate.label}: ${gate.nextGate}`),
    ...preExecutionState.runbook
      .filter((step) => step.status === 'blocked')
      .map((step) => `${step.label}: ${step.acceptance}`),
  ];
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...preExecutionState.noGo,
    'no_auto_patch_from_repair_center',
    'no_real_execution_without_backup',
    'no_real_execution_without_rollback',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_center_v0_closure.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_closure_report',
    decision: openBlockers.length ? 'needs_more_evidence' : 'closed_no_write',
    summary: {
      completedRoundCount: rounds.filter((round) => round.status === 'done').length,
      artifactCount: artifacts.length,
      openGateCount: openBlockers.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
      localProposalCount: executionQueueItems.length,
      handoffPackageCount: entries.length,
    },
    rounds,
    preExecutionState,
    artifacts,
    openBlockers,
    noGo,
    nextStep: openBlockers.length
      ? '保持 preview-only，补齐备份、回滚和写入契约后再开真实执行轮。'
      : '可以作为 GO-CANDIDATE 交给人工执行轮，但 Repair Center 本身仍不执行。',
  };
}

function buildRepairV0AcceptanceGapReport({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairV0AcceptanceGapPayload {
  const closureReport = buildRepairV0ClosureReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const controlledItems = entries.map((entry) => repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry));
  const goCandidates = controlledItems
    .filter((item) => item.status === 'confirmed')
    .map((item): RepairV0AcceptanceGapItem => ({
      id: `go-${item.entry.logId}`,
      lane: 'go_candidate',
      title: item.entry.title,
      statusLabel: item.statusLabel,
      owner: item.entry.owner,
      evidence: `${compact(item.entry.candidateFiles.length)} files / ${compact(item.entry.validationCommands.length)} validations`,
      nextAction: item.nextGate,
    }));
  const contractRequired = controlledItems
    .filter((item) => item.review.migrationNeeded || item.status === 'blocked')
    .map((item): RepairV0AcceptanceGapItem => ({
      id: `contract-${item.entry.logId}`,
      lane: 'contract_required',
      title: item.entry.title,
      statusLabel: item.review.migrationLabel,
      owner: item.review.confirmationOwner,
      evidence: item.rationale,
      nextAction: '单独开 backend persistence / migration / audit contract review，不从 Repair Center 直接执行。',
    }));
  const noWrite = [
    ...closureReport.preExecutionState.gates
      .filter((gate) => gate.status !== 'ready')
      .map((gate): RepairV0AcceptanceGapItem => ({
        id: `gate-${gate.id}`,
        lane: 'no_write',
        title: gate.label,
        statusLabel: gate.status,
        owner: 'Repair gate',
        evidence: gate.evidence,
        nextAction: gate.nextGate,
      })),
    ...closureReport.preExecutionState.runbook
      .filter((step) => step.status === 'blocked')
      .map((step): RepairV0AcceptanceGapItem => ({
        id: `runbook-${step.id}`,
        lane: 'no_write',
        title: step.label,
        statusLabel: step.status,
        owner: step.owner,
        evidence: step.evidence,
        nextAction: step.acceptance,
      })),
  ];
  const decision: 'go_candidate' | 'no_write' = goCandidates.length > 0 && noWrite.length === 0 && contractRequired.length === 0
    ? 'go_candidate'
    : 'no_write';

  return {
    version: 'repair_center_v0_acceptance_gap.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_acceptance_gap',
    decision,
    summary: {
      goCandidateCount: goCandidates.length,
      noWriteCount: noWrite.length,
      contractRequiredCount: contractRequired.length,
      openBlockerCount: closureReport.openBlockers.length,
      noGoCount: closureReport.noGo.length,
    },
    lanes: {
      goCandidates,
      noWrite,
      contractRequired,
    },
    closureReport,
    noGo: closureReport.noGo,
    nextStep: decision === 'go_candidate'
      ? '可以挑一个 confirmed 低风险候选另开真实执行轮；执行前仍要先备份和复核 diff。'
      : '继续保持 no-write；先处理备份、回滚、写入契约和 blocked gate。',
  };
}

function repairBackendContractFields(): RepairBackendContractField[] {
  const required = (section: RepairBackendContractSectionKey, field: string, requiredFor: string, evidence: string, acceptance: string): RepairBackendContractField => ({
    id: `${section}-${field}`,
    section,
    field,
    status: 'required',
    requiredFor,
    evidence,
    acceptance,
  });

  return [
    required('migration', 'migration_id', '版本化 schema 变更', '需要能追踪 alembic / SQL migration 编号。', '每个写入路径必须绑定唯一 migration id 或明确 no-migration reason。'),
    required('migration', 'target_table', '写入目标', 'Repair Center 目前只输出 preview package，没有真实目标表确认。', '列出 audit/task/evidence/proposal 对应表和字段。'),
    required('migration', 'schema_diff', '字段契约', '未来写入需要字段类型、nullable、默认值和索引说明。', 'schema diff 经过 review，且能被 dry-run 校验。'),
    required('migration', 'rollback_sql', '可回滚写入', '当前只有 rollbackBoundary 文案，没有 SQL 或恢复命令。', '提供 rollback SQL 或恢复步骤，且标注执行负责人。'),
    required('rbac', 'permission_key', '权限入口', '真实保存需要明确谁能创建/确认/取消修复项。', '定义 permission key，并在 UI/API 两侧返回拒绝状态。'),
    required('rbac', 'allowed_roles', '角色边界', '不能把 Repair Center 写入能力默认开放给所有员工。', '列出允许角色、拒绝角色和只读角色。'),
    required('rbac', 'denial_state', '失败状态', '无权限时不能静默失败或显示成功。', 'API 返回可解释 denial code，UI 显示 no-write/forbidden。'),
    required('audit', 'audit_action', '可追责操作', '真实写入必须记录 actor、action、target 和 request id。', '每个 write endpoint 有 audit_action 名称和 payload contract。'),
    required('audit', 'before_after', '变更证据', '执行修复可能改变任务、证据或提案状态。', 'audit log 保存 before/after 或 diff summary。'),
    required('audit', 'correlation_id', '跨表追踪', 'handoff、task、evidence 和 proposal 需要同一个追踪 id。', '生成 correlation_id 并贯穿 response 和 audit row。'),
    required('rollback', 'backup_ref', '备份优先', '页面没有读取到本轮 DB/R2/env 备份记录。', '执行前必须记录备份路径、时间、负责人和恢复范围。'),
    required('rollback', 'restore_command', '恢复动作', '仅有回滚文案无法支撑生产恢复。', '给出恢复命令或手动恢复步骤，并写明停止条件。'),
    required('rollback', 'verification_query', '恢复验收', '回滚后需要确认数据状态。', '提供 SQL/API 验收查询，证明恢复成功。'),
    required('failure_state', 'gate_status', '写入 gate', '当前 gate 仍默认 blocked。', '每个 endpoint 返回 gate_open / write_allowed / persisted。'),
    required('failure_state', 'rejected_reason', '失败原因', '被阻止时需要可解释 reason。', '返回 reason、next_action、persist=false、write_db=false。'),
    required('failure_state', 'retry_policy', '重试边界', '写失败不能自动重复造成副作用。', '定义是否可重试、重试次数和人工介入条件。'),
    required('failure_state', 'user_visible_state', '前端状态', '员工不能看到假成功。', 'UI 显示 blocked / preview-only / needs-review，而不是 saved。'),
  ];
}

function groupBackendContractFields(fields: RepairBackendContractField[]): Record<RepairBackendContractSectionKey, RepairBackendContractField[]> {
  return fields.reduce((acc, field) => {
    acc[field.section].push(field);
    return acc;
  }, {
    migration: [],
    rbac: [],
    audit: [],
    rollback: [],
    failure_state: [],
  } as Record<RepairBackendContractSectionKey, RepairBackendContractField[]>);
}

function buildRepairBackendPersistenceContract({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairBackendPersistenceContractPayload {
  const sourceGapReport = buildRepairV0AcceptanceGapReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const fields = repairBackendContractFields();
  const sections = groupBackendContractFields(fields);
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...sourceGapReport.noGo,
    'no_backend_write_without_contract',
    'no_migration_without_rollback_sql',
    'no_write_without_audit_action',
    'no_write_without_rbac_denial_state',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_backend_persistence_contract.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_contract',
    writeMode: 'blocked',
    summary: {
      sectionCount: Object.keys(sections).length,
      fieldCount: fields.length,
      requiredFieldCount: fields.filter((field) => field.status === 'required').length,
      blockedFieldCount: fields.filter((field) => field.status === 'blocked').length,
      contractTargetCount: sourceGapReport.lanes.contractRequired.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    sections,
    contractTargets: sourceGapReport.lanes.contractRequired,
    sourceGapReport,
    noGo,
    nextStep: '保持 Repair Center no-write；单独开后端持久化契约 review，补齐 migration、RBAC、audit、rollback、failure-state 后再讨论真实保存。',
  };
}

function repairExecutionApprovalSteps(contract: RepairBackendPersistenceContractPayload): RepairExecutionApprovalStep[] {
  return [
    {
      id: 'ops-backup',
      owner: 'ops',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '备份确认',
      confirms: 'DB/R2/env 已完成执行前备份，并记录恢复范围。',
      evidenceRequired: 'backup_ref、备份路径、时间、负责人、恢复对象。',
      validationCommand: '记录备份清单后再跑 smoke；没有备份不允许任何写入。',
      stopIfMissing: '没有 backup_ref 或备份负责人。',
    },
    {
      id: 'backend-migration',
      owner: 'backend',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: 'Migration / Schema 确认',
      confirms: `已审阅 ${compact(contract.summary.fieldCount)} 个后端契约字段，migration id、目标表和 rollback SQL 明确。`,
      evidenceRequired: 'migration_id、target_table、schema_diff、rollback_sql。',
      validationCommand: 'alembic current && alembic heads && backend schema guard tests',
      stopIfMissing: 'schema diff、rollback SQL 或 migration review 缺失。',
    },
    {
      id: 'security-rbac',
      owner: 'security',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: 'RBAC / Denial 确认',
      confirms: '权限 key、允许角色、拒绝角色和前端拒绝态已定义。',
      evidenceRequired: 'permission_key、allowed_roles、denial_state。',
      validationCommand: 'pytest tests/test_vkpi_repair_* -q',
      stopIfMissing: '无权限时无法证明 write_db=false 或用户可见拒绝态。',
    },
    {
      id: 'backend-audit',
      owner: 'backend',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: 'Audit / Correlation 确认',
      confirms: '每个真实写入动作都有 audit_action、before/after 和 correlation_id。',
      evidenceRequired: 'audit_action、before_after、correlation_id。',
      validationCommand: 'pytest tests/test_vkpi_repair_endpoint.py tests/test_vkpi_repair_repository.py -q',
      stopIfMissing: '无法追踪 actor/action/target/request id。',
    },
    {
      id: 'frontend-state',
      owner: 'frontend',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '前端状态确认',
      confirms: 'UI 不显示假成功；blocked、preview-only、needs-review 都能被用户看见。',
      evidenceRequired: 'gate_status、rejected_reason、user_visible_state。',
      validationCommand: 'npm --prefix frontend run build',
      stopIfMissing: '写入失败时前端仍可能显示 saved 或 done。',
    },
    {
      id: 'business-signoff',
      owner: 'business',
      status: 'blocked',
      label: '业务签核',
      confirms: '业务 owner 知道本轮只执行一个最小包，且接受回滚边界。',
      evidenceRequired: '确认人、确认时间、目标、验收输出、停止条件。',
      validationCommand: '复制 approval package JSON 进入人工执行轮，不在 Repair Center 直接执行。',
      stopIfMissing: '没有明确业务 owner 或验收输出。',
    },
  ];
}

function buildRepairExecutionApprovalPackage({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairExecutionApprovalPackagePayload {
  const backendContract = buildRepairBackendPersistenceContract({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const steps = repairExecutionApprovalSteps(backendContract);
  const validationCommands = Array.from(new Set(steps.map((step) => step.validationCommand)));
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...backendContract.noGo,
    'no_real_execution_before_all_signoffs',
    'no_repair_center_direct_write',
    'no_unreviewed_rollback_boundary',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_execution_approval_package.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_approval',
    decision: 'blocked_until_signed',
    summary: {
      approverCount: new Set(steps.map((step) => step.owner)).size,
      stepCount: steps.length,
      requiredStepCount: steps.filter((step) => step.status === 'blocked' || step.status === 'required').length,
      validationCommandCount: validationCommands.length,
      contractFieldCount: backendContract.summary.fieldCount,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    steps,
    backendContract,
    noGo,
    nextStep: '把此 approval package 交给独立真实执行轮；全部签核和备份完成前，Repair Center 继续 no-write。',
  };
}

function buildRepairFinalHandoffManifest({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairFinalHandoffManifestPayload {
  const approvalPackage = buildRepairExecutionApprovalPackage({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const backendContract = approvalPackage.backendContract;
  const acceptanceGap = backendContract.sourceGapReport;
  const closureReport = acceptanceGap.closureReport;
  const rounds = repairFinalHandoffRounds();
  const artifacts: RepairFinalHandoffManifestPayload['artifacts'] = [
    {
      id: 'acceptance_checkpoints',
      label: '页面级验收锚点',
      source: 'R83 / RepairAcceptanceCheckpointPanel',
      purpose: '确认 Repair Center 当前只做 preview、copy、handoff 和 dry-run。',
      status: 'ready',
    },
    {
      id: 'human_review_handoff',
      label: '人工 Review 交接',
      source: 'R84 / implementation handoff log',
      purpose: '把实现包交给人审，不从 UI 直接执行。',
      status: entries.length ? 'ready' : 'preview_only',
    },
    {
      id: 'controlled_queue',
      label: '受控执行队列',
      source: 'R85 / controlled execution queue',
      purpose: '给候选项排序和标注，但不写任务、不跑命令。',
      status: executionQueueItems.length ? 'ready' : 'preview_only',
    },
    {
      id: 'pre_execution_runbook',
      label: '执行前 Runbook',
      source: 'R88 / pre-execution package',
      purpose: '说明真实执行前的备份、验收、停止条件。',
      status: 'ready',
    },
    {
      id: 'v0_closure',
      label: 'V0 闭环报告',
      source: 'R89 / closure report',
      purpose: '汇总已完成产物和 no-write 结论。',
      status: 'ready',
    },
    {
      id: 'acceptance_gaps',
      label: 'V0 缺口分流',
      source: 'R90 / acceptance gaps',
      purpose: '把下一轮工作分成可交接、不可写、必须补契约。',
      status: 'ready',
    },
    {
      id: 'backend_contract',
      label: '后端持久化契约',
      source: 'R91 / backend persistence contract',
      purpose: '真实写入前必须补齐字段合同。',
      status: 'blocked',
    },
    {
      id: 'execution_approval',
      label: '真实执行审批',
      source: 'R92 / execution approval package',
      purpose: '全部签核完成前禁止进入写入轮。',
      status: 'blocked',
    },
  ];
  const validationCommands = Array.from(new Set([
    'npm --prefix frontend run build',
    'git diff --check -- frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r93-final-handoff-manifest.md',
    'rg -n "RepairFinalHandoffManifestPanel|buildRepairFinalHandoffManifest|repair_center_v0_handoff_manifest|Repair Center v0 最终交接 Manifest|FINAL HANDOFF / NO-WRITE|复制最终 Manifest JSON" frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r93-final-handoff-manifest.md',
    ...approvalPackage.steps.map((step) => step.validationCommand),
  ]));
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...approvalPackage.noGo,
    'no_manifest_as_execution_command',
    'no_auto_apply_from_manifest',
    'no_r93_database_write',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_center_v0_handoff_manifest.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_manifest',
    decision: 'blocked_by_missing_signoff',
    summary: {
      roundCount: rounds.length,
      artifactCount: artifacts.length,
      handoffPackageCount: entries.length,
      queueItemCount: executionQueueItems.length,
      approvalStepCount: approvalPackage.summary.stepCount,
      contractFieldCount: backendContract.summary.fieldCount,
      validationCommandCount: validationCommands.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    rounds,
    artifacts,
    validationCommands,
    packages: {
      closureReport,
      acceptanceGap,
      backendContract,
      approvalPackage,
    },
    noGo,
    nextStep: '用此 Manifest 做真实执行轮的索引；签核、备份、schema review、RBAC、audit、rollback 完成前，仍禁止在 Repair Center 里执行真实修复。',
  };
}

function buildRepairPreviewCloseoutReport({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairPreviewCloseoutReportPayload {
  const finalManifest = buildRepairFinalHandoffManifest({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const completed: RepairPreviewCloseoutItem[] = finalManifest.rounds
    .filter((round) => round.status === 'ready')
    .map((round) => ({
      id: `completed_${round.round.toLowerCase()}`,
      lane: 'completed',
      title: `${round.round} · ${round.title}`,
      statusLabel: 'Preview complete',
      evidence: round.artifact,
      nextAction: '保留为真实执行轮的只读依据，不重复实现。',
    }));
  const handoffReady: RepairPreviewCloseoutItem[] = [
    {
      id: 'final_manifest',
      lane: 'handoff_ready',
      title: '最终交接 Manifest',
      statusLabel: finalManifest.decision,
      evidence: `${compact(finalManifest.summary.roundCount)} 轮 · ${compact(finalManifest.summary.artifactCount)} 个产物 · ${compact(finalManifest.summary.validationCommandCount)} 条验收命令`,
      nextAction: '复制 Manifest 给独立真实执行轮；不要在 Repair Center 内直接执行。',
    },
    {
      id: 'pre_execution_index',
      lane: 'handoff_ready',
      title: '执行前索引',
      statusLabel: 'Ready for review',
      evidence: '包含 closure、gap、backend contract、approval package 四个嵌套包。',
      nextAction: '先完成 backup-first 和签核，再拆第一个最小写入切片。',
    },
  ];
  const blocked: RepairPreviewCloseoutItem[] = [
    ...finalManifest.artifacts
      .filter((artifact) => artifact.status === 'blocked')
      .map((artifact) => ({
        id: `blocked_${artifact.id}`,
        lane: 'blocked' as const,
        title: artifact.label,
        statusLabel: artifact.source,
        evidence: artifact.purpose,
        nextAction: artifact.id === 'backend_contract'
          ? '补 migration、RBAC、audit、rollback、failure state 后再允许写入设计。'
          : 'Ops/Backend/Security/Frontend/Business 全部签核后再进入真实执行轮。',
      })),
    {
      id: 'write_execution_gate',
      lane: 'blocked',
      title: '真实写入仍阻塞',
      statusLabel: 'No-write remains active',
      evidence: 'Repair Center 当前只允许 preview、copy、dry-run、handoff。',
      nextAction: '下一轮如果要开写，必须单独做 backup、schema review、RBAC、audit、rollback 和业务签核。',
    },
  ];
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...finalManifest.noGo,
    'no_preview_closeout_as_execution_command',
    'no_r94_database_write',
    'no_r94_provider_or_llm_run',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_center_preview_closeout.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_closeout',
    decision: 'preview_line_closed_no_write',
    summary: {
      completedCount: completed.length,
      handoffReadyCount: handoffReady.length,
      blockedCount: blocked.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
      finalManifestRoundCount: finalManifest.summary.roundCount,
      finalManifestArtifactCount: finalManifest.summary.artifactCount,
    },
    lanes: {
      completed,
      handoffReady,
      blocked,
    },
    finalManifest,
    noGo,
    nextStep: 'Repair Center preview/no-write 线到此收口。下一步若进入真实执行，必须另开最小写入切片，并先完成备份、schema、RBAC、audit、rollback、业务签核。',
  };
}

function buildRepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidate({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidatePayload {
  const closeoutReport = buildRepairPreviewCloseoutReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const backendContract = closeoutReport.finalManifest.packages.backendContract;
  const primarySlice: RepairExecutionSliceCandidate = {
    id: 'repair_proposal_draft_persistence',
    title: '保存一条人工确认的修复提案草稿',
    status: 'candidate',
    writeScope: '只新增 repair proposal draft 和对应 audit/correlation 记录；不执行修复、不修改业务表、不触发外部任务。',
    whyFirst: '这是从 preview 进入真实写入的最小切片：价值是保存人审结果，风险不触碰生产数据或 provider。',
    targetTables: ['vkpi_repair_proposals', 'vkpi_repair_audit_events'],
    requiredBackendWork: '新增 migration、repository create_draft、POST endpoint、RBAC permission、audit_action、failure-state response。',
    rollbackPlan: '按 proposal_id 删除 draft 和 audit event；migration rollback 只移除新增表/索引，不影响现有业务表。',
    acceptance: '无权限返回 denied；有权限时只写一条 draft，response 显示 write_db=true 且 executed=false。',
  };
  const alternateSlices: RepairExecutionSliceCandidate[] = [
    {
      id: 'repair_task_draft_persistence',
      title: '保存任务草稿',
      status: 'blocked',
      writeScope: '只保存 task draft，不分派、不提醒、不进入员工任务流。',
      whyFirst: '可作为第二切片；但 task 语义更靠近工作流，先级低于 proposal draft。',
      targetTables: ['vkpi_repair_task_drafts'],
      requiredBackendWork: '需要任务归属、状态机、取消语义和 audit 口径先定稿。',
      rollbackPlan: '按 draft_id 删除草稿和 audit event。',
      acceptance: '只生成 draft，不出现在正式任务列表。',
    },
    {
      id: 'repair_evidence_ref_persistence',
      title: '保存证据引用',
      status: 'blocked',
      writeScope: '只保存 evidence ref，不抓取、不上传、不改媒体。',
      whyFirst: '证据引用有价值，但依赖 canonical id 和证据归属口径。',
      targetTables: ['vkpi_repair_evidence_refs'],
      requiredBackendWork: '需要 evidence id、source url、hash、owner 和 retention 口径。',
      rollbackPlan: '按 evidence_ref_id 删除引用和 audit event。',
      acceptance: '引用可查询，原始证据不被复制或改写。',
    },
  ];
  const prerequisites: RepairExecutionSlicePrerequisite[] = [
    {
      id: 'backup_ref',
      owner: 'ops',
      label: '执行前备份',
      evidenceRequired: 'backup_ref、备份路径、恢复范围、负责人。',
      stopIfMissing: '没有备份引用，不允许创建 migration 或写入 endpoint。',
    },
    {
      id: 'schema_review',
      owner: 'backend',
      label: 'Schema / Migration Review',
      evidenceRequired: 'migration id、表结构、索引、rollback SQL、dry-run 输出。',
      stopIfMissing: '没有 migration review 和 rollback SQL。',
    },
    {
      id: 'rbac_denial',
      owner: 'security',
      label: 'RBAC / Denial Review',
      evidenceRequired: 'permission key、允许角色、拒绝角色、401/403 前端态。',
      stopIfMissing: '无法证明未授权用户不能写 draft。',
    },
    {
      id: 'audit_correlation',
      owner: 'backend',
      label: 'Audit / Correlation Review',
      evidenceRequired: 'audit_action、before/after、correlation_id、request id。',
      stopIfMissing: '无法追踪谁在何时保存了什么提案。',
    },
    {
      id: 'frontend_failure_state',
      owner: 'frontend',
      label: '前端失败态 Review',
      evidenceRequired: '保存中、保存失败、权限拒绝、回滚提示四种 UI 状态。',
      stopIfMissing: '写入失败时用户可能看到假成功。',
    },
    {
      id: 'business_signoff',
      owner: 'business',
      label: '业务签核',
      evidenceRequired: '确认人、目标、验收输出、停止条件。',
      stopIfMissing: '没有业务 owner 接受第一切片边界。',
    },
  ];
  const validationCommands = Array.from(new Set([
    'npm --prefix frontend run build',
    'pytest tests/test_vkpi_repair_endpoint.py tests/test_vkpi_repair_repository.py tests/test_vkpi_repair_schemas.py -q',
    'alembic current && alembic heads',
    'git diff --check -- frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r95-first-execution-slice-candidate.md',
    'rg -n "RepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidatePanel|buildRepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidate|repair_first_execution_slice_candidate|第一个真实执行最小切片候选|SLICE CANDIDATE / WRITE NOT ENABLED|复制切片候选 JSON" frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r95-first-execution-slice-candidate.md',
  ]));
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...closeoutReport.noGo,
    'no_first_slice_without_backup_ref',
    'no_first_slice_without_schema_review',
    'no_first_slice_without_rbac_denial_state',
    'no_first_slice_without_audit_correlation',
    'no_first_slice_without_business_signoff',
    'no_provider_or_llm_in_first_slice',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_first_execution_slice_candidate.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_slice_candidate',
    writeMode: 'not_enabled',
    decision: 'blocked_until_backup_and_signoff',
    summary: {
      sliceCount: 1 + alternateSlices.length,
      prerequisiteCount: prerequisites.length,
      validationCommandCount: validationCommands.length,
      contractFieldCount: backendContract.summary.fieldCount,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    primarySlice,
    alternateSlices,
    prerequisites,
    validationCommands,
    closeoutReport,
    noGo,
    nextStep: '若要进入真实执行，先完成备份和六项签核；第一切片只允许保存 proposal draft，不执行修复、不触发 provider、不调用 LLM/Gemini。',
  };
}

function buildRepairSchemaMigrationDesign({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairSchemaMigrationDesignPayload {
  const sourceSlice = buildRepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidate({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const tables: RepairSchemaTableDesign[] = [
    {
      tableName: 'vkpi_repair_proposals',
      purpose: '保存人工确认过的修复提案草稿，不执行修复。',
      writePolicy: 'insert-only for first slice; no update/delete endpoint in R96 design.',
      columns: [
        { name: 'id', dataType: 'bigserial primary key', nullable: false, source: 'database', purpose: '内部主键。' },
        { name: 'proposal_id', dataType: 'varchar(120) unique', nullable: false, source: 'frontend proposal package', purpose: '幂等键，防止重复保存同一提案。' },
        { name: 'card_id', dataType: 'varchar(160)', nullable: false, source: 'RepairCard.id', purpose: '关联 Repair Center 卡片。' },
        { name: 'title', dataType: 'text', nullable: false, source: 'RepairCard.title', purpose: '人读标题。' },
        { name: 'category', dataType: 'varchar(80)', nullable: false, source: 'RepairCard.category', purpose: '分类过滤。' },
        { name: 'severity', dataType: 'varchar(40)', nullable: false, source: 'RepairCard.severity', purpose: '优先级。' },
        { name: 'status', dataType: "varchar(40) default 'draft'", nullable: false, source: 'server default', purpose: '第一切片只允许 draft。' },
        { name: 'payload_json', dataType: 'jsonb', nullable: false, source: 'implementation package', purpose: '保留完整提案草稿。' },
        { name: 'created_by', dataType: 'varchar(120)', nullable: false, source: 'auth context', purpose: '审计 actor。' },
        { name: 'correlation_id', dataType: 'varchar(120)', nullable: false, source: 'request context', purpose: '串联 audit 和 response。' },
        { name: 'created_at', dataType: 'timestamptz default now()', nullable: false, source: 'database', purpose: '创建时间。' },
      ],
      indexes: [
        'unique index on proposal_id',
        'btree index on status, created_at desc',
        'btree index on category, severity',
        'btree index on correlation_id',
      ],
      rollback: 'drop indexes then drop vkpi_repair_proposals if no dependent write slice has shipped.',
    },
    {
      tableName: 'vkpi_repair_audit_events',
      purpose: '记录第一切片的 actor/action/target/correlation，不替代全局 audit 表。',
      writePolicy: 'append-only; every proposal draft insert must have one audit row.',
      columns: [
        { name: 'id', dataType: 'bigserial primary key', nullable: false, source: 'database', purpose: '内部主键。' },
        { name: 'proposal_id', dataType: 'varchar(120)', nullable: false, source: 'vkpi_repair_proposals.proposal_id', purpose: '关联草稿。' },
        { name: 'audit_action', dataType: "varchar(120) default 'repair.proposal_draft.create'", nullable: false, source: 'server constant', purpose: '审计动作名。' },
        { name: 'actor_id', dataType: 'varchar(120)', nullable: false, source: 'auth context', purpose: '操作者。' },
        { name: 'target_type', dataType: "varchar(80) default 'repair_proposal'", nullable: false, source: 'server constant', purpose: '目标类型。' },
        { name: 'target_id', dataType: 'varchar(160)', nullable: false, source: 'proposal_id', purpose: '目标 id。' },
        { name: 'before_json', dataType: 'jsonb', nullable: true, source: 'server', purpose: 'create 时为空。' },
        { name: 'after_json', dataType: 'jsonb', nullable: false, source: 'saved row summary', purpose: '保存后的最小摘要。' },
        { name: 'correlation_id', dataType: 'varchar(120)', nullable: false, source: 'request context', purpose: '串联请求。' },
        { name: 'created_at', dataType: 'timestamptz default now()', nullable: false, source: 'database', purpose: '审计时间。' },
      ],
      indexes: [
        'btree index on proposal_id',
        'btree index on actor_id, created_at desc',
        'btree index on audit_action, created_at desc',
        'btree index on correlation_id',
      ],
      rollback: 'drop indexes then drop vkpi_repair_audit_events before dropping proposal table.',
    },
  ];
  const rollbackSteps = [
    'confirm no later write slice depends on vkpi_repair_proposals or vkpi_repair_audit_events',
    'drop vkpi_repair_audit_events indexes',
    'drop vkpi_repair_audit_events',
    'drop vkpi_repair_proposals indexes',
    'drop vkpi_repair_proposals',
    'run alembic downgrade smoke in staging before production',
  ];
  const validationCommands = Array.from(new Set([
    'npm --prefix frontend run build',
    'alembic current && alembic heads',
    'pytest tests/test_vkpi_repair_schemas.py tests/test_vkpi_repair_repository.py -q',
    'git diff --check -- frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r96-schema-migration-design.md',
    'rg -n "RepairSchemaMigrationDesignPanel|buildRepairSchemaMigrationDesign|repair_schema_migration_design|后端 Schema / Migration 设计包|DESIGN ONLY / NO MIGRATION|复制 Schema 设计 JSON" frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r96-schema-migration-design.md',
  ]));
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...sourceSlice.noGo,
    'no_r96_alembic_revision',
    'no_r96_database_write',
    'no_schema_without_backup_ref',
    'no_schema_without_rollback_sql_review',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_schema_migration_design.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'read_only_schema_design',
    writeMode: 'migration_not_created',
    decision: 'design_only_blocked_until_review',
    summary: {
      tableCount: tables.length,
      columnCount: tables.reduce((total, table) => total + table.columns.length, 0),
      indexCount: tables.reduce((total, table) => total + table.indexes.length, 0),
      rollbackStepCount: rollbackSteps.length,
      validationCommandCount: validationCommands.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    sourceSlice,
    tables,
    rollbackSteps,
    validationCommands,
    noGo,
    nextStep: 'R96 只完成 schema/migration 设计。下一轮如要创建 migration，必须先有 backup_ref、rollback SQL review、RBAC/audit review 和业务签核。',
  };
}

function buildRepairMigrationReviewPacket({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  hasApiToken,
  repairCandidateCount,
}: {
  closureStatus: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null | undefined;
  readiness: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null | undefined;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  repairCandidateCount: number;
}): RepairMigrationReviewPacketPayload {
  const sourceDesign = buildRepairSchemaMigrationDesign({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const upSql: RepairMigrationReviewSqlStatement[] = [
    {
      step: 1,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'create repair proposal draft table',
      sql: "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vkpi_repair_proposals (id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY, proposal_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE, card_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL, title TEXT NOT NULL, category VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL, severity VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL, status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft', payload_json JSONB NOT NULL, created_by VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL, correlation_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW());",
      reviewNote: '第一切片只保存人工确认后的 draft proposal，不执行修复动作。',
    },
    {
      step: 2,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair proposal review queue',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_status_created ON vkpi_repair_proposals (status, created_at DESC);',
      reviewNote: '支撑 Repair Center 审核队列按状态和时间读取。',
    },
    {
      step: 3,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair proposal category severity',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_category_severity ON vkpi_repair_proposals (category, severity);',
      reviewNote: '支撑按问题类型和优先级过滤。',
    },
    {
      step: 4,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair proposal correlation',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_correlation ON vkpi_repair_proposals (correlation_id);',
      reviewNote: '支撑排查一次请求链路。',
    },
    {
      step: 5,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'create repair audit event table',
      sql: "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vkpi_repair_audit_events (id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY, proposal_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL REFERENCES vkpi_repair_proposals(proposal_id), audit_action VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'repair.proposal_draft.create', actor_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL, target_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'repair_proposal', target_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL, before_json JSONB, after_json JSONB NOT NULL, correlation_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW());",
      reviewNote: '每一次 proposal draft 保存必须写一条审计事件。',
    },
    {
      step: 6,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair audit proposal',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_proposal ON vkpi_repair_audit_events (proposal_id);',
      reviewNote: '支撑从提案回看审计事件。',
    },
    {
      step: 7,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair audit actor',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_actor_created ON vkpi_repair_audit_events (actor_id, created_at DESC);',
      reviewNote: '支撑按员工/操作者追溯。',
    },
    {
      step: 8,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair audit action',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_action_created ON vkpi_repair_audit_events (audit_action, created_at DESC);',
      reviewNote: '支撑按动作类型审计。',
    },
    {
      step: 9,
      statementKind: 'up',
      name: 'index repair audit correlation',
      sql: 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_correlation ON vkpi_repair_audit_events (correlation_id);',
      reviewNote: '支撑一次请求内 proposal/audit 串联。',
    },
  ];
  const rollbackSql: RepairMigrationReviewSqlStatement[] = [
    {
      step: 1,
      statementKind: 'rollback',
      name: 'drop repair audit indexes',
      sql: 'DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_correlation; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_action_created; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_actor_created; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_audit_events_proposal;',
      reviewNote: '先移除 audit 侧索引，避免 drop table 顺序误判。',
    },
    {
      step: 2,
      statementKind: 'rollback',
      name: 'drop repair audit table',
      sql: 'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS vkpi_repair_audit_events;',
      reviewNote: 'audit 表依赖 proposal_id，需要先于 proposals 删除。',
    },
    {
      step: 3,
      statementKind: 'rollback',
      name: 'drop repair proposal indexes',
      sql: 'DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_correlation; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_category_severity; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_vkpi_repair_proposals_status_created;',
      reviewNote: 'proposal_id unique 由 table constraint 管理，drop table 时一并删除。',
    },
    {
      step: 4,
      statementKind: 'rollback',
      name: 'drop repair proposal table',
      sql: 'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS vkpi_repair_proposals;',
      reviewNote: '只有确认没有后续真实执行切片依赖时才允许 rollback。',
    },
  ];
  const reviewGates: RepairMigrationReviewGate[] = [
    { gate: 'backup_ref', owner: 'ops', requiredEvidence: '生产 DB 备份路径、时间戳、restore smoke 说明。', stopIfMissing: true },
    { gate: 'rollback_sql_review', owner: 'backend', requiredEvidence: 'rollback SQL 已由第二人 review，且顺序覆盖 FK/索引。', stopIfMissing: true },
    { gate: 'rbac_audit_review', owner: 'security', requiredEvidence: 'created_by/actor_id/correlation_id 的来源已确认。', stopIfMissing: true },
    { gate: 'business_signoff', owner: 'business', requiredEvidence: '确认第一切片只保存草稿，不自动执行修复。', stopIfMissing: true },
    { gate: 'staging_migration_smoke', owner: 'backend', requiredEvidence: 'staging 跑 migration + rollback + heads/current 检查。', stopIfMissing: true },
  ];
  const validationCommands = Array.from(new Set([
    ...sourceDesign.validationCommands,
    'alembic upgrade head --sql > /tmp/vkpi_repair_migration_up.sql',
    'alembic downgrade -1 --sql > /tmp/vkpi_repair_migration_down.sql',
    'pytest tests/test_vkpi_repair_schemas.py tests/test_vkpi_repair_repository.py -q',
    'git diff --check -- migrations backend/app frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx docs/vkpi/repair-center-r97-migration-review-packet.md',
    'rg -n "RepairMigrationReviewPacketPanel|buildRepairMigrationReviewPacket|repair_migration_review_packet|Migration Review Packet|复制 Migration Review JSON" frontend/src/components/vkpi/pages/RepairCenterPage.tsx frontend/src/components/vkpi/VkpiDashboard.css docs/vkpi/repair-center-r97-migration-review-packet.md',
  ]));
  const noGo = Array.from(new Set([
    ...sourceDesign.noGo,
    'no_r97_migration_file_created',
    'no_r97_database_write',
    'no_migration_without_backup_ref',
    'no_migration_without_staging_smoke',
    'no_migration_if_existing_heads_not_clean',
  ]));

  return {
    version: 'repair_migration_review_packet.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    platform: 'viltroxtest',
    mode: 'review_only_migration_skeleton',
    writeMode: 'migration_file_not_created',
    decision: 'blocked_until_backup_review_signoff',
    summary: {
      upStatementCount: upSql.length,
      rollbackStatementCount: rollbackSql.length,
      reviewGateCount: reviewGates.length,
      validationCommandCount: validationCommands.length,
      noGoCount: noGo.length,
    },
    proposedRevision: {
      suggestedFilename: 'migrations/0xx_vkpi_repair_proposal_drafts.sql',
