      suggestedRevisionLabel: 'vkpi_repair_proposal_drafts',
      migrationType: 'additive',
      backupRequired: true,
      rollbackRequired: true,
    },
    sourceDesign,
    upSql,
    rollbackSql,
    reviewGates,
    validationCommands,
    noGo,
    nextStep: 'R97 只提供 migration review skeleton。创建真实 migration 文件、运行 alembic 或写库前，必须先拿到 backup_ref、rollback SQL review、RBAC/audit review、staging smoke 和业务签核。',
  };
}

function buildRepairImplementationExportPayload(
  card: RepairCard,
  record: RepairExecutionRecord,
  preflightState: RepairPreflightState,
  approval?: RepairApproval,
): RepairImplementationExportPayload {
  return {
    version: 'repair_implementation_package.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    implementation: buildRepairImplementationPreview(card, record, approval),
    record,
    source: {
      cardId: card.id,
      title: card.title,
      category: card.category,
      severity: card.severity,
      owner: card.owner,
      readiness: card.readiness,
    },
    preflight: {
      passed: repairPreflightComplete(preflightState),
      checked: preflightState,
    },
    handoffPurpose: 'developer_execution_review',
    guardrails: [
      'developer_handoff_only',
      'backup_first_before_real_edits',
      'no_provider_run',
      'no_database_write_without_migration_review',
      'no_deploy',
      'manual_review_required',
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairImplementationPackagePayload(
  card: RepairCard,
  record: RepairExecutionRecord,
  preflightState: RepairPreflightState,
  approval?: RepairApproval,
): VkpiRepairImplementationPackagePayload {
  const preview = buildRepairImplementationPreview(card, record, approval);
  return {
    cardId: card.id,
    title: card.title,
    category: card.category,
    severity: card.severity,
    owner: card.owner,
    readiness: card.readiness,
    statusLabel: preview.statusLabel,
    candidateFiles: preview.candidateFiles,
    patchOrder: preview.patchOrder,
    validationCommands: preview.validationCommands,
    rollbackBoundary: preview.rollbackBoundary,
    blockedActions: preview.blockedActions,
    preflight: buildRepairPreflightPayload(preflightState),
  };
}

function repairHandoffLogId(packageId: string, copiedAt: string): string {
  return `HANDOFF-${packageId.replace(/^IMPL-/, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').slice(0, 36).toUpperCase()}-${copiedAt.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 14)}`;
}

function buildRepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry(payload: RepairImplementationExportPayload): RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry {
  const copiedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    logId: repairHandoffLogId(payload.implementation.packageId, copiedAt),
    packageId: payload.implementation.packageId,
    proposalId: payload.record.proposalId,
    cardId: payload.source.cardId,
    title: payload.source.title,
    copiedAt,
    category: payload.source.category,
    severity: payload.source.severity,
    owner: payload.source.owner,
    readiness: payload.source.readiness,
    preflightPassed: payload.preflight.passed,
    candidateFiles: payload.implementation.candidateFiles,
    validationCommands: payload.implementation.validationCommands,
    guardrails: payload.guardrails,
    payload,
  };
}

function sanitizeRepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry(value: unknown): RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry | null {
  const row = asRecord(value);
  const payload = asRecord(row.payload) as unknown as RepairImplementationExportPayload;
  const source = asRecord(row.source || payload.source);
  const implementation = asRecord(row.implementation || payload.implementation);
  const packageId = String(row.packageId || implementation.packageId || '');
  const cardId = String(row.cardId || source.cardId || '');
  if (!packageId || !cardId || payload.version !== 'repair_implementation_package.local.v0') return null;
  const copiedAt = String(row.copiedAt || payload.generatedAt || new Date().toISOString());
  return {
    logId: String(row.logId || repairHandoffLogId(packageId, copiedAt)),
    packageId,
    proposalId: String(row.proposalId || asRecord(payload.record).proposalId || ''),
    cardId,
    title: String(row.title || source.title || cardId),
    copiedAt,
    category: (['ui', 'agent', 'data', 'ops', 'market'].includes(String(row.category || source.category)) ? String(row.category || source.category) : 'ops') as RepairCategory,
    severity: (['high', 'medium', 'low'].includes(String(row.severity || source.severity)) ? String(row.severity || source.severity) : 'medium') as RepairSeverity,
    owner: String(row.owner || source.owner || '待分配'),
    readiness: numberValue(row.readiness || source.readiness),
    preflightPassed: Boolean(row.preflightPassed || asRecord(payload.preflight).passed),
    candidateFiles: asStringArray(row.candidateFiles || implementation.candidateFiles),
    validationCommands: asStringArray(row.validationCommands || implementation.validationCommands),
    guardrails: asStringArray(row.guardrails || payload.guardrails),
    payload,
  };
}

function readRepairImplementationHandoffLog(): RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(REPAIR_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF_LOG_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = asRecord(JSON.parse(raw));
    if (parsed.version !== 'repair_implementation_handoff_log.local.v0') return [];
    return (Array.isArray(parsed.entries) ? parsed.entries : [])
      .map((item) => sanitizeRepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry(item))
      .filter((item): item is RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry => Boolean(item));
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeRepairImplementationHandoffLog(entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[]): RepairImplementationHandoffLogPayload | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const payload: RepairImplementationHandoffLogPayload = {
      version: 'repair_implementation_handoff_log.local.v0',
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      count: entries.length,
      entries,
    };
    window.localStorage.setItem(REPAIR_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF_LOG_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
    return payload;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function clearRepairImplementationHandoffLog() {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.localStorage.removeItem(REPAIR_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF_LOG_KEY);
  } catch {
    // localStorage can be blocked by browser privacy settings; clearing local UI state still works.
  }
}

function buildRepairHandoffFutureWritePreview(entry: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry): RepairHandoffFutureWritePreview {
  const record = entry.payload.record;
  return {
    auditAction: [
      { field: 'event_category', value: 'repair' },
      { field: 'action', value: 'implementation_handoff_copied' },
      { field: 'target_type', value: 'repair_implementation_package' },
      { field: 'target_id', value: entry.packageId },
      { field: 'detail', value: entry.title },
      { field: 'metadata.proposal_id', value: record.proposalId },
      { field: 'metadata.preflight_passed', value: String(entry.preflightPassed) },
      { field: 'metadata.guardrails', value: entry.guardrails.join(' / ') },
    ],
    developerTask: [
      { field: 'task_type', value: 'developer_execution_review' },
      { field: 'title', value: `实现包复核：${entry.title}` },
      { field: 'priority', value: entry.severity === 'high' ? 'high' : 'normal' },
      { field: 'owner', value: entry.owner },
      { field: 'source_package_id', value: entry.packageId },
      { field: 'candidate_files', value: entry.candidateFiles.join(' / ') || '-' },
      { field: 'validation_commands', value: entry.validationCommands.slice(0, 4).join(' / ') || '-' },
      { field: 'status', value: 'draft_only' },
    ],
    evidenceRefs: [
      { field: 'repair_card_id', value: entry.cardId },
      { field: 'proposal_id', value: record.proposalId },
      { field: 'record_id', value: record.recordId },
      { field: 'package_id', value: entry.packageId },
      { field: 'copied_at', value: entry.copiedAt },
      { field: 'candidate_file_refs', value: entry.candidateFiles.join(' / ') || '-' },
      { field: 'preflight_summary', value: entry.preflightPassed ? 'passed' : 'blocked' },
      { field: 'future_table', value: 'future: evidence_refs / repair_proposals / task draft' },
    ],
  };
}

function buildRepairFutureWriteMappingExportPayload(entry: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry): RepairFutureWriteMappingExportPayload {
  return {
    version: 'repair_future_write_mapping.local.v0',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    sourceLogId: entry.logId,
    sourcePackageId: entry.packageId,
    mapping: buildRepairHandoffFutureWritePreview(entry),
    writeMode: 'preview_only',
    guardrails: [
      'preview_only',
      'no_audit_write',
      'no_task_create',
      'no_evidence_write',
      'human_review_required',
    ],
  };
}

function repairSourceKey(card: RepairCard): string {
  const raw = card.id.replace(/^repair-roadmap-/, '').replace(/^repair-/, '');
  const haystack = `${raw} ${card.title} ${card.body} ${card.evidence.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase();
  if (haystack.includes('google') || haystack.includes('trends') || haystack.includes('news')) return 'google-trends-news';
  if (haystack.includes('reddit')) return 'reddit-market';
  if (haystack.includes('gemini') || haystack.includes('claude') || haystack.includes('gpt') || haystack.includes('llm') || haystack.includes('视频理解')) return 'gemini-llm-summary';
  if (haystack.includes('competitor') || haystack.includes('竞品') || haystack.includes('sigma') || haystack.includes('tamron')) return 'competitor-radar';
  if (haystack.includes('kol-video') || haystack.includes('kol 视频') || haystack.includes('qualified kol')) return 'kol-video-library';
  if (haystack.includes('owned-video') || haystack.includes('自有官方') || haystack.includes('官方视频')) return 'owned-video-library';
  if (haystack.includes('platform-advice')) return 'platform-advice';
  return 'generic';
}

function integrationChecklistForCard(card: RepairCard): RepairIntegrationChecklist {
  const key = repairSourceKey(card);
  if (key === 'google-trends-news') {
    return {
      title: 'Google Trends / News 接入检查',
      dataFields: ['query', 'region', 'time_window', 'interest_score', 'related_topics', 'source_url', 'captured_at', 'query_hash'],
      budgetRules: ['每日查询白名单', '同 query 24h 缓存', '失败不重试超过 1 次', 'provider_calls / cost_ledger 必须落账'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['source_url', 'captured_at', 'query_hash', 'region', 'raw_payload_ref'],
      failureStates: ['not_configured', 'quota_limited', 'no_results', 'partial_region', 'source_unavailable'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认关键词白名单', '确认国家/地区列表', '确认负面关键词', '确认是否允许进入每日摘要'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'reddit-market') {
    return {
      title: 'Reddit / 社区趋势接入检查',
      dataFields: ['subreddit', 'post_url', 'post_id', 'author', 'score', 'comments_declared', 'comments_cached', 'captured_at', 'sentiment_tag'],
      budgetRules: ['只跑白名单 subreddit', '评论正文 cap 必须可见', 'OAuth 或 best-effort 状态要暴露', '不承诺全量抓取'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['post_url', 'subreddit', 'comment_sample_ref', 'capture_run_id', 'cap_status'],
      failureStates: ['auth_missing', 'rate_limited', 'private_or_deleted', 'comments_cap_reached', 'no_relevant_posts'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认 subreddit 白名单', '确认评论 cap', '确认敏感社区不自动回复', '确认只作为机会/风险信号'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'gemini-llm-summary') {
    return {
      title: 'Gemini 视频理解 + GPT/Claude 总结接入检查',
      dataFields: ['video_url', 'platform', 'post_id', 'duration_seconds', 'frames_sampled', 'transcript_status', 'sku_mentions', 'production_quality', 'provider_call_id', 'cost_usd'],
      budgetRules: ['只允许单 KOL / 单视频小口', '每次调用前检查预算', '视频 hash 命中直接复用', 'LLM 只总结已有证据'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['video_url', 'post_id', 'provider_call_id', 'cost_ledger_id', 'evidence_refs'],
      failureStates: ['budget_blocked', 'unsupported_media', 'audio_only', 'provider_error', 'evidence_missing'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认视频选择理由', '确认预算上限', '确认是否可用于平台建议', '确认不生成无证据事实'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'competitor-radar') {
    return {
      title: '竞品雷达接入检查',
      dataFields: ['brand', 'competitor_sku', 'signal_type', 'launch_date', 'source_url', 'kol_overlap', 'risk_tier', 'captured_at', 'review_status'],
      budgetRules: ['优先 RSS / 官网 / 已有信号', '不抓私密或登录墙数据', '重复 signal 合并', '人工复核前不改推荐排序'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['source_url', 'brand', 'signal_uid', 'kol_overlap_refs', 'review_status'],
      failureStates: ['source_unavailable', 'duplicate_signal', 'ambiguous_brand', 'stale_signal', 'needs_human_review'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认竞品品牌表', '确认 risk_tier 规则', '确认 KOL 重合口径', '确认是否进入老板首页风险'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'kol-video-library') {
    return {
      title: 'KOL 视频库接入检查',
      dataFields: ['kol_pool_id', 'platform', 'post_id', 'video_url', 'published_at', 'views', 'engagement_rate', 'viltrox_signal', 'collaboration_status'],
      budgetRules: ['只刷 qualified subset', 'max_posts 必须受控', 'cold KOL 搜索时 stale-while-revalidate', '不回到 1021/1023 全量每日刷新'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['kol_pool_id', 'post_id', 'profile_url', 'media_cache_ref', 'refresh_tier_reason'],
      failureStates: ['cold_kol_skipped', 'media_unavailable', 'cache_failed', 'no_recent_signal', 'platform_quota_limited'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认 qualified 条件', '确认最近 Viltrox / 合作口径', '确认是否进入每日刷新', '确认失败 KOL 可追踪'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'owned-video-library') {
    return {
      title: '自有官方视频库接入检查',
      dataFields: ['official_account_id', 'platform', 'post_id', 'video_url', 'thumbnail_url', 'published_at', 'views', 'likes', 'comments_cached', 'baseline_protected'],
      budgetRules: ['官方账号优先全缓存', '视频缓存失败必须显示坏源', '评论 cap/status 必须同步', '不得用 latest 30 污染 baseline'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['official_account_id', 'post_id', 'media_cache_ref', 'metric_snapshot_id', 'comment_contract'],
      failureStates: ['views_unavailable', 'bad_source', 'cache_miss', 'comment_cap_reached', 'baseline_protected'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认官方账号列表', '确认媒体缓存策略', '确认 baseline 解释文案', '确认坏视频源显示 open original'],
    };
  }
  if (key === 'platform-advice') {
    return {
      title: '平台建议矩阵接入检查',
      dataFields: ['platform', 'best_for', 'signal_need', 'query', 'evidence_refs', 'recommendation_text', 'freshness_hours', 'confidence'],
      budgetRules: ['先复用已有内容/趋势信号', '外部趋势源接入前必须预算 ledger', '平台建议不能自动生成任务', '无证据建议必须灰显'],
      evidenceBacklinks: ['source_card_id', 'platform', 'discover_focus_query', 'evidence_refs', 'accepted_feedback_ref'],
      failureStates: ['missing_evidence', 'stale_signal', 'budget_blocked', 'platform_not_supported', 'needs_human_review'],
      manualConfirmations: ['确认平台优先级', '确认推荐可解释', '确认员工接受/拒绝回流', '确认是否展示给老板首页'],
    };
  }
  return {
    title: '通用接入检查',
    dataFields: ['source_id', 'source_url', 'captured_at', 'evidence_refs', 'status', 'owner'],
    budgetRules: ['先定义预算', '先定义失败状态', '先定义人工确认点', '不自动执行外部调用'],
    evidenceBacklinks: ['source_url', 'evidence_refs', 'run_id', 'created_at'],
    failureStates: ['not_configured', 'no_results', 'partial', 'failed', 'needs_human_review'],
    manualConfirmations: ['确认数据字段', '确认预算规则', '确认验收方式', '确认回滚方式'],
  };
}

function repairPackageFromCard(card: RepairCard, approval?: RepairApproval): RepairPackageDetail {
  const targetFiles = Array.from(new Set([...likelyFileEvidence(card.evidence), ...fallbackFilesForCategory(card.category)])).slice(0, 5);
  const checklist = integrationChecklistForCard(card);
  const executionSteps = [
    `确认缺口：${card.title}`,
    `限定范围：只处理 ${categoryLabel(card.category)} / ${card.owner} 负责的 UI 或只读诊断面`,
    `先确认字段契约：${checklist.dataFields.slice(0, 4).join(' / ')}`,
    `先生成本地提案：${card.suggestedFix}`,
    approval?.status === 'confirmed' ? '人工已确认，可进入下一轮受控执行' : '等待人工确认后，下一轮再进入真实代码修改或后端接入',
  ];
  const risks = [
    card.severity === 'high' ? '高优先级修复可能影响老板首页或关键判断入口，必须保留旧入口兜底。' : '当前风险可控，但仍需避免扩大到同步、抓取或生产配置。',
    `准备度 ${card.readiness}%：低于 70% 时只允许补证据、补文案、补入口，不允许自动化执行。`,
    '所有外部 API、LLM、Gemini、provider 调用必须单独走预算和审计。',
  ];
  const validation = [
    '前端 build 必须通过。',
    'git diff --check 必须通过。',
    '页面能显示中文空态、证据、允许范围、禁止范围和验收结果。',
    '不新增自动同步、不新增批量抓取、不改变线上配置。',
  ];
  return {
    scope: `${categoryLabel(card.category)}修复 · ${severityLabel(card.severity)}优先级 · ${approvalLabel(approval)}`,
    targetFiles,
    executionSteps,
    risks,
    validation,
    noGo: card.blocked,
    checklist,
  };
}

function dryRunProposalFromCard(card: RepairCard, approval?: RepairApproval): RepairDryRunProposal {
  const detail = repairPackageFromCard(card, approval);
  const hasDraft = approval?.status === 'drafted' || approval?.status === 'confirmed';
  return {
    proposalId: repairProposalId(card.id),
    statusLabel: hasDraft ? approvalLabel(approval) : '预览草案，尚未生成',
    objective: `${card.title}：${card.body}`,
    scope: [
      detail.scope,
      `负责人：${card.owner}`,
      `准备度：${card.readiness}%`,
      `只允许范围：${card.allowed.join(' / ')}`,
    ],
    patchPlan: [
      '保留现有入口和旧数据兜底，不删除当前可用功能。',
      ...detail.targetFiles.slice(0, 3).map((file) => `限制修改范围：${file}`),
      `先落字段契约：${detail.checklist.dataFields.slice(0, 5).join(' / ')}`,
      `失败状态必须前端可见：${detail.checklist.failureStates.slice(0, 4).join(' / ')}`,
      card.suggestedFix,
    ],
    acceptance: [
      ...detail.validation,
      `证据回链至少包含：${detail.checklist.evidenceBacklinks.slice(0, 4).join(' / ')}`,
      `人工确认项已展示：${detail.checklist.manualConfirmations.slice(0, 3).join(' / ')}`,
      hasDraft ? '草案已进入本地队列，可由人工确认是否进入下一轮执行。' : '点击“生成本地提案”后，只记录页面本地状态，不写库、不执行 patch。',
    ],
    rollback: [
      '如果下一轮执行后页面异常，优先回退本轮相关前端文件。',
      '如果涉及 API 接入失败，保持只读空态和旧入口可用。',
      '任何线上配置、同步策略或预算策略变更都必须另开受控轮次。',
    ],
    decisionGate: approval?.status === 'confirmed'
      ? '人工已确认：下一轮仍需备份、diff review、构建通过后才允许继续。'
      : '未人工确认：只能停留在 dry-run proposal，不允许自动执行。',
  };
}

function RepairPersistencePreviewBlock({
  preview,
  title = '未来落库预览',
}: {
  preview?: VkpiRepairPersistencePreview | null;
  title?: string;
}) {
  const rows = repairPersistenceRows(preview);
  if (!rows.length) return null;
  const persisted = Boolean(preview?.persisted);
  const writeDb = Boolean(asRecord(preview?.policy).writeDb);

  return (
    <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-preview" aria-label={title}>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-preview__head">
        <div>
          <b>{title}</b>
          <span>{String(preview?.mode || 'repair_persistence_preview_no_write_v0')} · rows={rows.length}</span>
        </div>
        <em>{persisted || writeDb ? '异常：写入打开' : 'no-write'}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-preview__rows">
        {rows.map((rawRow, index) => {
          const row = asRecord(rawRow);
          const columns = asRecord(row.columns);
          const columnEntries = Object.entries(columns).slice(0, 8);
          return (
            <section key={repairPreviewRowKey(row, index)}>
              <div>
                <strong>{String(row.table || '-')}</strong>
                <span>{String(row.operation || '-')} · {String(row.keyField || row.key_field || '-')}: {String(row.keyValue || row.key_value || '-')}</span>
              </div>
              <small>hash {shortHash(row.payloadSha256 || row.payload_sha256)}</small>
              <em>{row.enabled ? 'enabled' : String(row.disabledReason || row.disabled_reason || 'disabled')}</em>
              <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-preview__columns">
                {columnEntries.map(([field, value]) => (
                  <span key={field}><b>{field}</b>{String(value ?? '-')}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function RepairPersistenceReadinessPanel({
  readiness,
}: {
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
}) {
  const gates = Array.isArray(readiness?.gates) ? readiness.gates : [];
  const score = numberValue(readiness?.readinessScore ?? readiness?.readiness_score);
  const readyCount = numberValue(readiness?.readyCount ?? readiness?.ready_count);
  const blockedCount = numberValue(readiness?.blockedCount ?? readiness?.blocked_count);
  const gateCount = numberValue(readiness?.gateCount ?? readiness?.gate_count);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-write-gate" aria-label="真实保存准入检查">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-write-gate__head">
        <div>
          <span>Persistence readiness</span>
          <b>真实保存准入检查</b>
          <p>{readiness ? '后端只读 gate 已返回：用于解释为什么当前仍不能真实保存。' : '未读取到后端 gate；保持 preview-only。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{writeAllowed ? 'write allowed' : `blocked · ${score}%`}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-write-gate__stats">
        <span><b>{compact(gateCount)}</b><small>Gate</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(readyCount)}</b><small>Ready</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedCount)}</b><small>Blocked</small></span>
        <span><b>{writeAllowed ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>允许写库</small></span>
      </div>
      {gates.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-write-gate__gates">
          {gates.map((gate) => {
            const evidence = Array.isArray(gate.evidence) ? gate.evidence : [];
            return (
              <article className={gate.ready ? 'is-ready' : 'is-blocked'} key={String(gate.key || gate.label)}>
                <div>
                  <strong>{String(gate.label || gate.key || '-')}</strong>
                  <em>{String(gate.status || '-')} · {compact(numberValue(gate.score))}%</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(gate.detail || '-')}</p>
                {evidence.length ? <small>{evidence.slice(0, 3).join(' · ')}</small> : null}
                <span>{String(gate.nextAction || gate.next_action || '-')}</span>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-write-gate__empty">没有 gate 明细。当前页面不会显示任何真实保存按钮。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPersistenceBoundaryPanel({
  boundary,
}: {
  boundary?: VkpiRepairPersistenceBoundaryResponse | null;
}) {
  const tables = Array.isArray(boundary?.tables) ? boundary.tables : [];
  const endpoints = Array.isArray(boundary?.endpoints) ? boundary.endpoints : [];
  const blockedBy = Array.isArray(boundary?.blockedBy || boundary?.blocked_by)
    ? (boundary?.blockedBy || boundary?.blocked_by || [])
    : [];
  const tableCount = numberValue(boundary?.tableCount ?? boundary?.table_count ?? tables.length);
  const endpointCount = numberValue(boundary?.endpointCount ?? boundary?.endpoint_count ?? endpoints.length);
  const firstSlice = String(boundary?.firstWriteSlice || boundary?.first_write_slice || 'repair_proposal_draft_persistence');
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(boundary?.writeAllowed ?? boundary?.write_allowed);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-persistence-boundary" aria-label="后端真实保存边界">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-boundary__head">
        <div>
          <span>Backend persistence boundary</span>
          <b>后端真实保存边界</b>
          <p>{boundary ? '后端已返回最小写入边界：哪些表、哪些 endpoint、哪些 gate 仍然阻止 persist=true。' : '未读取到后端边界合同；当前仍按 no-write 处理。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{writeAllowed ? 'write allowed' : 'persist=true blocked'}</em>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-boundary__summary">
        <span><b>{compact(tableCount)}</b><small>目标表</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(endpointCount)}</b><small>未来写入 endpoint</small></span>
        <span><b>{writeAllowed ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>允许写库</small></span>
        <span><b>{firstSlice}</b><small>第一切片</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-boundary__grid">
        <article>
          <strong>表边界</strong>
          {tables.length ? tables.map((table) => {
            const required = Array.isArray(table.requiredBeforeWrite || table.required_before_write)
              ? (table.requiredBeforeWrite || table.required_before_write || [])
              : [];
            return (
              <section key={String(table.tableName || table.table_name)}>
                <div>
                  <b>{String(table.tableName || table.table_name || '-')}</b>
                  <em>{table.firstWriteSlice || table.first_write_slice ? 'first slice' : 'later'}</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(table.runtimePolicy || table.runtime_policy || '-')}</p>
                {required.length ? <small>{required.slice(0, 3).join(' · ')}</small> : null}
              </section>
            );
          }) : <p>没有表边界数据。</p>}
        </article>

        <article>
          <strong>Endpoint 边界</strong>
          {endpoints.length ? endpoints.map((endpoint) => {
            const blocked = Array.isArray(endpoint.blockedBy || endpoint.blocked_by)
              ? (endpoint.blockedBy || endpoint.blocked_by || [])
              : [];
            return (
              <section key={`${String(endpoint.method)}:${String(endpoint.route)}`}>
                <div>
                  <b>{String(endpoint.method || '-')} {String(endpoint.route || '-')}</b>
                  <em>{endpoint.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'blocked'}</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(endpoint.writeTarget || endpoint.write_target || endpoint.currentMode || endpoint.current_mode || '-')}</p>
                {blocked.length ? <small>{blocked.slice(0, 4).join(' · ')}</small> : null}
              </section>
            );
          }) : <p>没有 endpoint 边界数据。</p>}
        </article>
      </div>

      {blockedBy.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-persistence-boundary__blocked">
          <strong>当前阻断</strong>
          {blockedBy.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPersistenceApprovalEnvelopePanel({
  envelope,
  message,
  hasApiToken,
  loading,
  onDryRun,
}: {
  envelope?: VkpiRepairPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeResponse | null;
  message?: string;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  onDryRun: () => void;
}) {
  const gates = Array.isArray(envelope?.gates) ? envelope.gates : [];
  const blockedBy = Array.isArray(envelope?.blockedBy || envelope?.blocked_by)
    ? (envelope?.blockedBy || envelope?.blocked_by || [])
    : [];
  const gateCount = numberValue(envelope?.gateCount ?? envelope?.gate_count ?? gates.length);
  const providedCount = numberValue(envelope?.providedCount ?? envelope?.provided_count);
  const missingCount = numberValue(envelope?.missingCount ?? envelope?.missing_count);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(envelope?.writeAllowed ?? envelope?.write_allowed);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope" aria-label="persist=true 审批 envelope dry-run">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__head">
        <div>
          <span>Persist approval envelope</span>
          <b>persist=true 审批 Envelope</b>
          <p>这里仅校验备份、迁移、rollback、RBAC/audit、业务签核和 staging smoke 引用；即使全部填齐，当前也不会打开写库。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__actions">
          <em>{writeAllowed ? 'write allowed' : 'DRY-RUN / WRITE BLOCKED'}</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onDryRun} disabled={!hasApiToken || loading}>
            {loading ? '校验中...' : '校验空审批 Envelope'}
          </button>
          {message ? <small role="status">{message}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(gateCount)}</b><small>审批项</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(providedCount)}</b><small>已提供</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(missingCount)}</b><small>缺失</small></span>
        <span><b>{writeAllowed ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>允许写库</small></span>
      </div>

      {gates.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__gates">
          {gates.map((gate) => (
            <article className={gate.provided ? 'is-provided' : 'is-missing'} key={String(gate.key || gate.label)}>
              <div>
                <strong>{String(gate.label || gate.key || '-')}</strong>
                <em>{String(gate.status || '-')}</em>
              </div>
              <p>{String(gate.detail || '-')}</p>
              <span>{String(gate.nextAction || gate.next_action || '-')}</span>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__empty">
          {hasApiToken ? '点击按钮后只做 dry-run 校验，不会保存审批、不写库。' : '未登录 token，不能请求后端 dry-run；页面仍保持只读。'}
        </p>
      )}

      {blockedBy.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-envelope__blocked">
          <strong>阻断原因</strong>
          {blockedBy.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairMigrationPreflightManifestPanel({
  manifest,
}: {
  manifest?: VkpiRepairMigrationPreflightManifestResponse | null;
}) {
  const artifacts = Array.isArray(manifest?.artifacts) ? manifest.artifacts : [];
  const steps = Array.isArray(manifest?.steps) ? manifest.steps : [];
  const blockedBy = Array.isArray(manifest?.blockedBy || manifest?.blocked_by)
    ? (manifest?.blockedBy || manifest?.blocked_by || [])
    : [];
  const artifactCount = numberValue(manifest?.artifactCount ?? manifest?.artifact_count ?? artifacts.length);
  const missingCount = numberValue(manifest?.missingCount ?? manifest?.missing_count);
  const stepCount = numberValue(manifest?.stepCount ?? manifest?.step_count ?? steps.length);
  const migrationAllowed = Boolean(manifest?.migrationAllowed ?? manifest?.migration_allowed);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-migration-preflight" aria-label="Migration preflight manifest">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-preflight__head">
        <div>
          <span>Migration preflight manifest</span>
          <b>Migration 前置 Manifest</b>
          <p>创建真实 migration 前，必须先把备份、rollback review、RBAC/audit、业务签核和 staging smoke 证据收齐；当前不会生成 migration 文件。</p>
        </div>
        <em>{migrationAllowed ? 'migration allowed' : 'MIGRATION BLOCKED'}</em>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-preflight__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(artifactCount)}</b><small>证据项</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(missingCount)}</b><small>缺失</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(stepCount)}</b><small>前置步骤</small></span>
        <span><b>{migrationAllowed ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>允许 migration</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-preflight__grid">
        <article>
          <strong>必备证据</strong>
          {artifacts.length ? artifacts.map((artifact) => (
            <section key={String(artifact.key || artifact.label)}>
              <div>
                <b>{String(artifact.label || artifact.key || '-')}</b>
                <em>{String(artifact.status || '-')}</em>
              </div>
              <p>{String(artifact.detail || '-')}</p>
              <small>{String(artifact.expectedRefFormat || artifact.expected_ref_format || '-')}</small>
            </section>
          )) : <p>没有 manifest 证据项；保持 blocked。</p>}
        </article>

        <article>
          <strong>前置步骤</strong>
          {steps.length ? steps.map((step) => (
            <section key={`${String(step.phase)}:${String(step.step)}`}>
              <div>
                <b>{compact(numberValue(step.step))}. {String(step.title || '-')}</b>
                <em>{String(step.phase || '-')}</em>
              </div>
              <code>{String(step.command || '-')}</code>
              <small>{String(step.expectedOutput || step.expected_output || '-')}</small>
            </section>
          )) : <p>没有前置步骤；保持 blocked。</p>}
        </article>
      </div>

      {blockedBy.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-preflight__blocked">
          <strong>阻断原因</strong>
          {blockedBy.slice(0, 10).map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairMigrationDraftPackagePanel({
  draftPackage,
}: {
  draftPackage?: VkpiRepairMigrationDraftPackageResponse | null;
}) {
  const upSql = Array.isArray(draftPackage?.upSql || draftPackage?.up_sql)
    ? (draftPackage?.upSql || draftPackage?.up_sql || [])
    : [];
  const downSql = Array.isArray(draftPackage?.downSql || draftPackage?.down_sql)
    ? (draftPackage?.downSql || draftPackage?.down_sql || [])
    : [];
  const reviewCommands = Array.isArray(draftPackage?.reviewCommands || draftPackage?.review_commands)
    ? (draftPackage?.reviewCommands || draftPackage?.review_commands || [])
    : [];
  const blockedBy = Array.isArray(draftPackage?.blockedBy || draftPackage?.blocked_by)
    ? (draftPackage?.blockedBy || draftPackage?.blocked_by || [])
    : [];
  const fileCreated = Boolean(draftPackage?.fileCreated ?? draftPackage?.file_created);
  const migrationAllowed = Boolean(draftPackage?.migrationAllowed ?? draftPackage?.migration_allowed);
  const suggestedFilename = String(draftPackage?.suggestedFilename || draftPackage?.suggested_filename || 'migrations/0xx_vkpi_repair_proposal_drafts.sql');
  const revisionSlug = String(draftPackage?.revisionSlug || draftPackage?.revision_slug || 'vkpi_repair_proposal_drafts');

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft" aria-label="Migration draft package">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__head">
        <div>
          <span>Migration draft package</span>
          <b>Migration 文件草案包</b>
          <p>这里展示建议文件名、up/down SQL 草案和 review 命令；当前不会创建 migration 文件，也不会运行 Alembic。</p>
        </div>
        <em>{fileCreated || migrationAllowed ? 'unexpected enabled' : 'DRAFT ONLY / NO FILE'}</em>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__revision">
        <strong>{suggestedFilename}</strong>
        <span>{revisionSlug} · {String(draftPackage?.migrationKind || draftPackage?.migration_kind || 'additive')}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(numberValue(draftPackage?.upSqlCount ?? draftPackage?.up_sql_count ?? upSql.length))}</b><small>Up SQL</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(numberValue(draftPackage?.downSqlCount ?? draftPackage?.down_sql_count ?? downSql.length))}</b><small>Down SQL</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(numberValue(draftPackage?.reviewCommandCount ?? draftPackage?.review_command_count ?? reviewCommands.length))}</b><small>Review 命令</small></span>
        <span><b>{fileCreated ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>文件已创建</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__sql">
        <article>
          <strong>Up SQL 草案</strong>
          {upSql.slice(0, 4).map((statement) => (
            <code key={`${String(statement.step)}:${String(statement.name)}`}>{compact(numberValue(statement.step))}. {String(statement.sql || '-')}</code>
          ))}
        </article>
        <article>
          <strong>Down SQL 草案</strong>
          {downSql.map((statement) => (
            <code key={`${String(statement.step)}:${String(statement.name)}`}>{compact(numberValue(statement.step))}. {String(statement.sql || '-')}</code>
          ))}
        </article>
      </div>

      {reviewCommands.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__commands">
          <strong>Review 命令</strong>
          {reviewCommands.map((command) => <code key={command}>{command}</code>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {blockedBy.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-draft__blocked">
          <strong>阻断原因</strong>
          {blockedBy.slice(0, 10).map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPersistenceModeBanner({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  hasApiToken,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
}) {
  const closureScore = numberValue(closureStatus?.closureScore ?? closureStatus?.closure_score);
  const readinessScore = numberValue(readiness?.readinessScore ?? readiness?.readiness_score);
  const blockedCount = numberValue(readiness?.blockedCount ?? readiness?.blocked_count);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(
    (closureStatus?.writeAllowed ?? closureStatus?.write_allowed)
    || (readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed),
  );
  const required = Array.isArray(readiness?.requiredBeforePersist || readiness?.required_before_persist)
    ? (readiness?.requiredBeforePersist || readiness?.required_before_persist || []).slice(0, 6)
    : [
      'fresh_db_backup',
      'migration_review',
      'rbac_actor_gate',
      'business_audit_write',
      'rollback_sql',
    ];

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-persist-mode" aria-label="Repair Center 保存模式">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persist-mode__main">
        <span>当前保存模式</span>
        <b>预览模式：未写库，未执行，未入队</b>
        <p>
          页面所有修复动作只生成 dry-run 回执、落库预览和本地 JSON。前端请求显式使用
          <code>persist=false</code>
          ，后端遇到
          <code>persist=true</code>
          会返回 409，并保持
          <code>writeDb=false</code>
          。
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persist-mode__meters">
        <span><strong>{writeAllowed ? '异常' : '安全'}</strong><small>真实写库</small></span>
        <span><strong>{closureStatus ? `${compact(closureScore)}%` : '-'}</strong><small>闭环分</small></span>
        <span><strong>{readiness ? `${compact(readinessScore)}%` : '-'}</strong><small>持久化分</small></span>
        <span><strong>{compact(blockedCount)}</strong><small>阻塞 Gate</small></span>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persist-mode__flow" aria-label="保存链路状态">
        <span className="is-done"><i>1</i>本地提案</span>
        <span className="is-done"><i>2</i>后端 dry-run</span>
        <span className="is-done"><i>3</i>落库预览</span>
        <span className="is-blocked"><i>4</i>真实保存阻止</span>
        <span className="is-blocked"><i>5</i>执行/部署关闭</span>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-persist-mode__requirements">
        <b>打开真实保存前必须补齐</b>
        {required.map((item) => <span key={String(item)}>{String(item)}</span>)}
        <em>{hasApiToken ? '已登录可读 gate，但仍不能写库' : '未登录时只展示本地安全边界'}</em>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPersistenceRejectPanel({
  receipts,
  onClear,
}: {
  receipts: RepairPersistenceRejectedReceipt[];
  onClear: () => void;
}) {
  if (!receipts.length) return null;
  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-reject-panel" aria-label="真实保存拒绝回执">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-reject-panel__head">
        <div>
          <span>Persistence reject receipts</span>
          <b>真实保存已被后端阻止</b>
          <p>这些回执来自后端 409，不是普通失败。含义是：请求试图打开真实保存，但当前 gate 未批准，系统保持未写库。</p>
        </div>
        <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onClear}>清除回执</button>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-reject-panel__list">
        {receipts.slice(0, 5).map((receipt) => (
          <article key={receipt.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{repairRejectScopeLabel(receipt.scope)} · {receipt.title}</strong>
              <em>HTTP {receipt.status}</em>
            </div>
            <p>{receipt.reason}</p>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-reject-panel__facts">
              <span><b>mode</b>{receipt.mode}</span>
              <span><b>target</b>{receipt.targetId}</span>
              <span><b>persist</b>{String(receipt.persist)}</span>
              <span><b>writeDb</b>{String(receipt.writeDb)}</span>
            </div>
            <small>{receipt.nextAction}</small>
            <i>{formatTime(receipt.occurredAt)}</i>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairEndpointContractPanel() {
  const endpointCount = repairEndpointContracts.length;
  const blockedCount = repairEndpointContracts.reduce((total, item) => total + item.blockedActions.length, 0);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract" aria-label="Repair Center 接口契约表">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__head">
        <div>
          <span>Endpoint contract</span>
          <b>接口契约表</b>
          <p>
            6 个按钮接口全部显式使用
            <code>persist=false</code>
            ：当前只返回 dry-run、preview 和回执；真实写库、执行、入队仍被阻止。
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__stats">
          <span><strong>{compact(endpointCount)}</strong><small>接口</small></span>
          <span><strong>0</strong><small>写库</small></span>
          <span><strong>0</strong><small>执行</small></span>
          <span><strong>{compact(blockedCount)}</strong><small>阻止项</small></span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__grid">
        {repairEndpointContracts.map((item) => (
          <article key={item.id}>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__route">
              <em>{item.method}</em>
              <code>{item.endpoint}</code>
            </div>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__meta">
              <span><b>触发</b>{item.trigger}</span>
              <span><b>当前模式</b>{item.currentMode}</span>
              <span><b>未来写入</b>{item.futureTarget}</span>
              <span><b>当前输出</b>{item.output}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-endpoint-contract__blocked">
              <b>当前阻止</b>
              {item.blockedActions.map((action) => <span key={action}>{action}</span>)}
            </div>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairAcceptanceCheckpointPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
}) {
  const closureScore = numberValue(closureStatus?.closureScore ?? closureStatus?.closure_score);
  const readinessScore = numberValue(readiness?.readinessScore ?? readiness?.readiness_score);
  const blockedGateCount = numberValue(readiness?.blockedCount ?? readiness?.blocked_count);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(
    (closureStatus?.writeAllowed ?? closureStatus?.write_allowed)
    || (readiness?.writeAllowed ?? readiness?.write_allowed),
  );
  const readyCount = repairAcceptanceAnchors.filter((item) => item.status === 'ready').length;
  const previewCount = repairAcceptanceAnchors.filter((item) => item.status === 'preview').length;
  const blockedCount = repairAcceptanceAnchors.filter((item) => item.status === 'blocked').length;

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-acceptance" aria-label="Repair Center 页面级验收锚点">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-acceptance__head">
        <div>
          <span>Acceptance checkpoint</span>
          <b>页面级验收锚点</b>
          <p>
            这一块只用于验收当前阶段的自动化修复能力：能看到、能复制、能预览，但不能写库、不能执行、不能入队。
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-acceptance__score">
          <strong>{writeAllowed ? '异常' : 'NO-WRITE'}</strong>
          <small>{writeAllowed ? '真实保存 gate 已打开，需要立刻复查' : '当前验收口径：只读安全'}</small>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-acceptance__metrics">
        <span><b>{closureStatus ? `${compact(closureScore)}%` : '-'}</b><small>闭环分</small></span>
        <span><b>{readiness ? `${compact(readinessScore)}%` : '-'}</b><small>持久化分</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(repairEndpointContracts.length)}</b><small>接口契约</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedGateCount)}</b><small>阻塞 Gate</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(readyCount)}</b><small>Ready</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(previewCount)}</b><small>Preview</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedCount)}</b><small>Blocked</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-acceptance__grid">
        {repairAcceptanceAnchors.map((item) => (
          <article className={`is-${item.status}`} key={item.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{item.label}</strong>
              <em>{item.status}</em>
            </div>
            <b>{item.metric}</b>
            <p>{item.evidence}</p>
            <small>{item.nextStep}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairMinimumExecutionReadinessPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
}) {
  const gates = buildRepairMinimumExecutionGates(closureStatus, readiness, entries);
  const readyCount = gates.filter((item) => item.status === 'ready').length;
  const reviewCount = gates.filter((item) => item.status === 'review').length;
  const blockedCount = gates.filter((item) => item.status === 'blocked').length;
  const canEnterExecutionRound = blockedCount === 0 && reviewCount === 0;

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-minimum-execution" aria-label="最小执行准入总览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-minimum-execution__head">
        <div>
          <span>Minimum execution readiness</span>
          <b>最小执行准入总览</b>
          <p>这不是执行开关，只是把进入真正执行轮之前必须满足的条件集中展示。当前默认应保持 NO-GO。</p>
        </div>
        <div className={`vkpi-repair-minimum-execution__decision${canEnterExecutionRound ? ' is-go' : ' is-no-go'}`}>
          <strong>{canEnterExecutionRound ? 'GO-CANDIDATE' : 'NO-GO'}</strong>
          <small>{canEnterExecutionRound ? '仍需人工启动独立执行轮' : '不能进入真实执行轮'}</small>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-minimum-execution__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(gates.length)}</b><small>准入项</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(readyCount)}</b><small>Ready</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(reviewCount)}</b><small>Review</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedCount)}</b><small>Blocked</small></span>
        <span><b>0</b><small>自动执行</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-minimum-execution__gates">
        {gates.map((gate) => (
          <article className={`is-${gate.status}`} key={gate.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{gate.label}</strong>
              <em>{gate.status}</em>
            </div>
            <b>{gate.metric}</b>
            <p>{gate.evidence}</p>
            <small>{gate.nextGate}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPreExecutionRunbookPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyPackage,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyPackage: () => void;
}) {
  const gates = buildRepairMinimumExecutionGates(closureStatus, readiness, entries);
  const steps = buildRepairPreExecutionRunbookSteps(gates, entries, executionQueueItems, closureStatus, readiness);
  const readyStepCount = steps.filter((step) => step.status === 'ready').length;
  const requiredStepCount = steps.filter((step) => step.status === 'required').length;
  const blockedStepCount = steps.filter((step) => step.status === 'blocked').length;
  const blockedGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'blocked').length;
  const reviewGateCount = gates.filter((gate) => gate.status === 'review').length;
  const decision = blockedGateCount === 0 && reviewGateCount === 0 ? 'GO-CANDIDATE' : 'NO-GO';

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-runbook" aria-label="执行前 runbook checklist">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-runbook__head">
        <div>
          <span>Pre-execution runbook</span>
          <b>执行前 Runbook Checklist</b>
          <p>把真正执行前要确认的备份、回滚、diff、写入契约、验收命令和人工签核集中成状态包。当前页面只负责导出，不负责执行。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-runbook__actions">
          <em>{decision}</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyPackage}>复制当前状态包 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-runbook__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(steps.length)}</b><small>Runbook 项</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(readyStepCount)}</b><small>Ready</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(requiredStepCount)}</b><small>需人工确认</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedStepCount)}</b><small>Blocked</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(entries.length)}</b><small>实现包</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(executionQueueItems.length)}</b><small>本地提案</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-runbook__steps">
        {steps.map((step, index) => (
          <article className={`is-${step.status}`} key={step.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{index + 1}. {step.label}</strong>
              <em>{step.status}</em>
            </div>
            <b>{step.owner}</b>
            <p>{step.evidence}</p>
            <small>{step.acceptance}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-runbook__package">
        <span><strong>平台</strong>viltroxtest</span>
        <span><strong>读取模式</strong>{hasApiToken ? '已登录只读 API' : '本地只读 fallback'}</span>
        <span><strong>问题候选</strong>{compact(repairCandidateCount)}</span>
        <span><strong>禁止动作</strong>DB 写入 / provider sync / LLM / Gemini / deploy / timer resume</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairV0ClosureReportPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyReport,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyReport: () => void;
}) {
  const report = buildRepairV0ClosureReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const decisionLabel = report.decision === 'closed_no_write' ? 'V0 CLOSED / NO-WRITE' : 'V0 PREVIEW / NO-GO';
  const latestRounds = report.rounds.slice(-4);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure" aria-label="Repair Center v0 闭环总览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__head">
        <div>
          <span>Repair Center v0 closure</span>
          <b>Repair Center v0 闭环总览</b>
          <p>把 R83-R89 的只读自动化修复能力收束成一张交接卡：已完成什么、还能复制什么、哪些 gate 仍禁止真实执行。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__actions">
          <em>{decisionLabel}</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyReport}>复制 v0 闭环报告 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.completedRoundCount)}</b><small>已完成轮次</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.artifactCount)}</b><small>交接产物</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.openGateCount)}</b><small>未关 Gate</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>禁止动作</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.handoffPackageCount)}</b><small>实现包</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.localProposalCount)}</b><small>本地提案</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__rounds">
        {latestRounds.map((round) => (
          <article className={`is-${round.status}`} key={round.round}>
            <div>
              <strong>{round.round} · {round.title}</strong>
              <em>{round.status}</em>
            </div>
            <b>{round.output}</b>
            <small>{round.evidence}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__artifacts">
        {report.artifacts.map((artifact) => (
          <span className={`is-${artifact.status}`} key={artifact.id}>
            <strong>{artifact.label}</strong>
            {artifact.purpose}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-closure__blockers">
        <strong>真实执行前仍需关闭</strong>
        {report.openBlockers.length ? report.openBlockers.slice(0, 6).map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>) : <span>当前没有 open blocker，但仍需人工启动独立执行轮。</span>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairV0AcceptanceGapPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyReport,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyReport: () => void;
}) {
  const report = buildRepairV0AcceptanceGapReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const decisionLabel = report.decision === 'go_candidate' ? 'GO-CANDIDATE' : 'NO-WRITE';
  const lanes: Array<{
    key: keyof RepairV0AcceptanceGapPayload['lanes'];
    label: string;
    tone: RepairV0AcceptanceGapLane;
    empty: string;
  }> = [
    { key: 'goCandidates', label: '可进人工执行轮', tone: 'go_candidate', empty: '暂无 confirmed 低风险候选。' },
    { key: 'noWrite', label: '必须继续 no-write', tone: 'no_write', empty: '暂无 no-write blocker。' },
    { key: 'contractRequired', label: '后端契约单独拆', tone: 'contract_required', empty: '暂无需要独立拆分的持久化契约。' },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps" aria-label="Repair Center v0 总验收与缺口">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps__head">
        <div>
          <span>V0 acceptance gaps</span>
          <b>Repair Center v0 总验收 / 缺口清单</b>
          <p>把当前状态拆成三条处理线：能否进入人工真实执行轮、哪些必须继续 no-write、哪些要另开后端持久化契约。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps__actions">
          <em>{decisionLabel}</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyReport}>复制 v0 缺口 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.goCandidateCount)}</b><small>执行候选</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.noWriteCount)}</b><small>No-write</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.contractRequiredCount)}</b><small>后端契约</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.openBlockerCount)}</b><small>Open blocker</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>禁止动作</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps__lanes">
        {lanes.map((lane) => {
          const items = report.lanes[lane.key];
          return (
            <article className={`is-${lane.tone}`} key={lane.key}>
              <div>
                <strong>{lane.label}</strong>
                <em>{compact(items.length)}</em>
              </div>
              {items.length ? items.slice(0, 4).map((item) => (
                <section key={item.id}>
                  <b>{item.title}</b>
                  <span>{item.owner} · {item.statusLabel}</span>
                  <p>{item.evidence}</p>
                  <small>{item.nextAction}</small>
                </section>
              )) : <p>{lane.empty}</p>}
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-v0-gaps__next">
        <strong>下一步</strong>
        <span>{report.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairBackendPersistenceContractPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyContract,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyContract: () => void;
}) {
  const contract = buildRepairBackendPersistenceContract({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const sections: Array<{ key: RepairBackendContractSectionKey; label: string }> = [
    { key: 'migration', label: 'Migration' },
    { key: 'rbac', label: 'RBAC' },
    { key: 'audit', label: 'Audit' },
    { key: 'rollback', label: 'Rollback' },
    { key: 'failure_state', label: 'Failure State' },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract" aria-label="后端持久化契约">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract__head">
        <div>
          <span>Backend persistence contract</span>
          <b>后端持久化契约包</b>
          <p>这张卡只定义未来真实写入必须满足的字段合同；当前 writeMode 固定 blocked，不创建 migration、不写 audit、不建任务。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract__actions">
          <em>CONTRACT ONLY / NO-WRITE</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyContract}>复制后端契约 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(contract.summary.sectionCount)}</b><small>Section</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(contract.summary.fieldCount)}</b><small>字段合同</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(contract.summary.requiredFieldCount)}</b><small>Required</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(contract.summary.contractTargetCount)}</b><small>拆分目标</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(contract.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract__sections">
        {sections.map((section) => {
          const fields = contract.sections[section.key];
          return (
            <article key={section.key}>
              <div>
                <strong>{section.label}</strong>
                <em>{compact(fields.length)}</em>
              </div>
              {fields.slice(0, 4).map((field) => (
                <section key={field.id}>
                  <b>{field.field}</b>
                  <span>{field.requiredFor}</span>
                  <p>{field.evidence}</p>
                  <small>{field.acceptance}</small>
                </section>
              ))}
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-backend-contract__targets">
        <strong>需要单独拆分的后端契约目标</strong>
        {contract.contractTargets.length ? contract.contractTargets.slice(0, 5).map((target) => (
          <span key={target.id}>{target.title} · {target.nextAction}</span>
        )) : <span>当前没有具体实现包被归入后端契约目标，但字段合同仍是未来开写前的硬门槛。</span>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairExecutionApprovalPackagePanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyApproval,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyApproval: () => void;
}) {
  const approval = buildRepairExecutionApprovalPackage({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const ownerLabel: Record<RepairExecutionApprovalOwner, string> = {
    ops: 'Ops',
    backend: 'Backend',
    frontend: 'Frontend',
    security: 'Security',
    business: 'Business',
  };

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-approval-package" aria-label="真实执行轮审批清单">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-package__head">
        <div>
          <span>Execution approval package</span>
          <b>真实执行轮审批清单</b>
          <p>这张卡说明谁要确认、确认什么、缺什么就停止。它只导出审批包，不把 Repair Center 变成执行入口。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-package__actions">
          <em>BLOCKED UNTIL SIGNED</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyApproval}>复制审批包 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-package__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(approval.summary.approverCount)}</b><small>确认方</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(approval.summary.stepCount)}</b><small>签核项</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(approval.summary.validationCommandCount)}</b><small>验收命令</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(approval.summary.contractFieldCount)}</b><small>契约字段</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(approval.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-package__steps">
        {approval.steps.map((step) => (
          <article className={`is-${step.owner}`} key={step.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{step.label}</strong>
              <em>{ownerLabel[step.owner]}</em>
            </div>
            <p>{step.confirms}</p>
            <span><b>证据</b>{step.evidenceRequired}</span>
            <span><b>验收</b>{step.validationCommand}</span>
            <small>{step.stopIfMissing}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-approval-package__next">
        <strong>执行边界</strong>
        <span>{approval.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairFinalHandoffManifestPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyManifest,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyManifest: () => void;
}) {
  const manifest = buildRepairFinalHandoffManifest({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const statusLabel: Record<RepairFinalHandoffRoundStatus, string> = {
    ready: 'READY',
    blocked: 'BLOCKED',
    handoff: 'HANDOFF',
  };

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest" aria-label="Repair Center v0 最终交接 Manifest">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__head">
        <div>
          <span>Final handoff manifest</span>
          <b>Repair Center v0 最终交接 Manifest</b>
          <p>把 R83-R92 的产物、验收命令、签核条件和禁止动作统一成一份索引。它用于下一轮人工执行准备，不从本页触发真实修复。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__actions">
          <em>FINAL HANDOFF / NO-WRITE</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--primary" type="button" onClick={onCopyManifest}>复制最终 Manifest JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.roundCount)}</b><small>轮次</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.artifactCount)}</b><small>产物</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.approvalStepCount)}</b><small>签核</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.contractFieldCount)}</b><small>契约字段</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.validationCommandCount)}</b><small>验收命令</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(manifest.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__rounds">
        {manifest.rounds.map((round) => (
          <article className={`is-${round.status}`} key={round.round}>
            <div>
              <strong>{round.round}</strong>
              <em>{statusLabel[round.status]}</em>
            </div>
            <b>{round.title}</b>
            <span>{round.artifact}</span>
            <small>{round.use}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__artifacts">
        <strong>交接产物索引</strong>
        {manifest.artifacts.map((artifact) => (
          <span className={`is-${artifact.status}`} key={artifact.id}>
            <b>{artifact.label}</b>
            <em>{artifact.source}</em>
            {artifact.purpose}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-final-manifest__next">
        <strong>下一步</strong>
        <span>{manifest.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPreviewCloseoutReportPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyReport,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyReport: () => void;
}) {
  const report = buildRepairPreviewCloseoutReport({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const lanes: Array<{
    key: keyof RepairPreviewCloseoutReportPayload['lanes'];
    label: string;
    tone: RepairPreviewCloseoutLaneKey;
  }> = [
    { key: 'completed', label: '已收口', tone: 'completed' },
    { key: 'handoffReady', label: '可交接', tone: 'handoff_ready' },
    { key: 'blocked', label: '仍阻塞', tone: 'blocked' },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout" aria-label="Repair Center preview 线最终收口">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout__head">
        <div>
          <span>Preview closeout</span>
          <b>Repair Center preview 线最终收口</b>
          <p>这张表只回答当前做到哪、哪些已经可以归档、哪些仍不能开写。它是 R83-R94 的收口报告，不是执行按钮。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout__actions">
          <em>PREVIEW CLOSED / NO-WRITE</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyReport}>复制收口报告 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.completedCount)}</b><small>已收口</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.handoffReadyCount)}</b><small>可交接</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.blockedCount)}</b><small>仍阻塞</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.finalManifestRoundCount)}</b><small>Manifest 轮次</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(report.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout__lanes">
        {lanes.map((lane) => {
          const items = report.lanes[lane.key];
          return (
            <article className={`is-${lane.tone}`} key={lane.key}>
              <div>
                <strong>{lane.label}</strong>
                <em>{compact(items.length)}</em>
              </div>
              {items.slice(0, 6).map((item) => (
                <section key={item.id}>
                  <b>{item.title}</b>
                  <span>{item.statusLabel}</span>
                  <p>{item.evidence}</p>
                  <small>{item.nextAction}</small>
                </section>
              ))}
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-preview-closeout__next">
        <strong>当前位置 / 下一步</strong>
        <span>{report.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidatePanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyCandidate,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyCandidate: () => void;
}) {
  const candidate = buildRepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidate({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });
  const ownerLabel: Record<RepairExecutionApprovalOwner, string> = {
    ops: 'Ops',
    backend: 'Backend',
    frontend: 'Frontend',
    security: 'Security',
    business: 'Business',
  };

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-first-slice" aria-label="第一个真实执行最小切片候选">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__head">
        <div>
          <span>First execution slice</span>
          <b>第一个真实执行最小切片候选</b>
          <p>候选只定义第一步该写什么和缺什么签核；当前 writeMode 仍是 not_enabled，不从 Repair Center 开真实保存。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__actions">
          <em>SLICE CANDIDATE / WRITE NOT ENABLED</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--primary" type="button" onClick={onCopyCandidate}>复制切片候选 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(candidate.summary.sliceCount)}</b><small>候选切片</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(candidate.summary.prerequisiteCount)}</b><small>前置签核</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(candidate.summary.contractFieldCount)}</b><small>契约字段</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(candidate.summary.validationCommandCount)}</b><small>验收命令</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(candidate.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__primary">
        <div>
          <strong>{candidate.primarySlice.title}</strong>
          <em>{candidate.decision}</em>
        </div>
        <p>{candidate.primarySlice.writeScope}</p>
        <span><b>为什么先做</b>{candidate.primarySlice.whyFirst}</span>
        <span><b>后端工作</b>{candidate.primarySlice.requiredBackendWork}</span>
        <span><b>Rollback</b>{candidate.primarySlice.rollbackPlan}</span>
        <small>{candidate.primarySlice.acceptance}</small>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__prereqs">
        {candidate.prerequisites.map((item) => (
          <article className={`is-${item.owner}`} key={item.id}>
            <div>
              <strong>{item.label}</strong>
              <em>{ownerLabel[item.owner]}</em>
            </div>
            <span>{item.evidenceRequired}</span>
            <small>{item.stopIfMissing}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__alternates">
        <strong>备选切片，暂不先做</strong>
        {candidate.alternateSlices.map((slice) => (
          <span key={slice.id}>
            <b>{slice.title}</b>
            {slice.whyFirst}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-first-slice__next">
        <strong>下一步</strong>
        <span>{candidate.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairSchemaMigrationDesignPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyDesign,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;

}) {
  const design = buildRepairSchemaMigrationDesign({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-schema-design" aria-label="后端 Schema / Migration 设计包">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__head">
        <div>

          <b>后端 Schema / Migration 设计包</b>
          <p>这里只把第一切片需要的表、字段、索引和 rollback 设计清楚；当前不会创建 Alembic revision，也不会连接数据库。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__actions">
          <em>DESIGN ONLY / NO MIGRATION</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyDesign}>复制 Schema 设计 JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(design.summary.tableCount)}</b><small>表</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(design.summary.columnCount)}</b><small>字段</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(design.summary.indexCount)}</b><small>索引</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(design.summary.rollbackStepCount)}</b><small>Rollback</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(design.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__tables">
        {design.tables.map((table) => (
          <article key={table.tableName}>
            <div>
              <strong>{table.tableName}</strong>
              <em>{compact(table.columns.length)} fields</em>
            </div>
            <p>{table.purpose}</p>
            <span>{table.writePolicy}</span>
            <section>
              {table.columns.slice(0, 6).map((column) => (
                <small key={column.name}>
                  <b>{column.name}</b>
                  {column.dataType} · {column.nullable ? 'nullable' : 'required'}
                </small>
              ))}
            </section>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__rollback">
        <strong>Rollback 设计</strong>
        {design.rollbackSteps.map((step) => <span key={step}>{step}</span>)}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-schema-design__next">
        <strong>下一步</strong>
        <span>{design.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairMigrationReviewPacketPanel({
  closureStatus,
  readiness,
  entries,
  executionQueueItems,
  repairCandidateCount,
  hasApiToken,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyPacket,
}: {
  closureStatus?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
  readiness?: VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null;
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  executionQueueItems: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  repairCandidateCount: number;
  hasApiToken: boolean;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyPacket: () => void;
}) {
  const packet = buildRepairMigrationReviewPacket({
    closureStatus,
    readiness,
    entries,
    executionQueueItems,
    hasApiToken,
    repairCandidateCount,
  });

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-migration-review" aria-label="Migration Review Packet">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__head">
        <div>
          <span>Migration Review Packet</span>
          <b>迁移审核包 / SQL 草案</b>
          <p>这里提前把 up SQL、rollback SQL、review gates 和验证命令收齐；当前仍不会创建 migration 文件，也不会跑 alembic。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__actions">
          <em>REVIEW ONLY / FILE NOT CREATED</em>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyPacket}>复制 Migration Review JSON</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__revision">
        <strong>{packet.proposedRevision.suggestedFilename}</strong>
        <span>{packet.proposedRevision.suggestedRevisionLabel} · {packet.proposedRevision.migrationType}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__metrics">
        <span><b>{compact(packet.summary.upStatementCount)}</b><small>Up SQL</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(packet.summary.rollbackStatementCount)}</b><small>Rollback SQL</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(packet.summary.reviewGateCount)}</b><small>Review gates</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(packet.summary.validationCommandCount)}</b><small>验证命令</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(packet.summary.noGoCount)}</b><small>No-go</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__sql">
        <article>
          <strong>Up SQL 草案</strong>
          {packet.upSql.slice(0, 4).map((statement) => (
            <code key={statement.name}>{statement.step}. {statement.sql}</code>
          ))}
        </article>
        <article>
          <strong>Rollback SQL 草案</strong>
          {packet.rollbackSql.map((statement) => (
            <code key={statement.name}>{statement.step}. {statement.sql}</code>
          ))}
        </article>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__gates">
        {packet.reviewGates.map((gate) => (
          <span key={gate.gate}>
            <b>{gate.gate}</b>
            {gate.owner} · {gate.requiredEvidence}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-migration-review__next">
        <strong>下一步</strong>
        <span>{packet.nextStep}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairClosureStatusPanel({
  status,
}: {
  status?: VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null;
}) {
  const steps = Array.isArray(status?.steps) ? status.steps : [];
  const score = numberValue(status?.closureScore ?? status?.closure_score);
  const readyCount = numberValue(status?.readyCount ?? status?.ready_count);
  const previewOnlyCount = numberValue(status?.previewOnlyCount ?? status?.preview_only_count);
  const blockedCount = numberValue(status?.blockedCount ?? status?.blocked_count);
  const stepCount = numberValue(status?.stepCount ?? status?.step_count ?? steps.length);
  const writeAllowed = Boolean(status?.writeAllowed ?? status?.write_allowed);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-closure-status" aria-label="修复中心闭环状态">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-closure-status__head">
        <div>
          <span>Closure status</span>
          <b>修复闭环状态</b>
          <p>{status ? '后端只读状态已返回：当前控制链路已接通到 preview-ready，真实写入仍被 gate 阻断。' : '未读取到闭环状态；页面继续使用只读兜底。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{writeAllowed ? 'write allowed' : `preview ${compact(score)}%`}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-closure-status__stats">
        <span><b>{compact(stepCount)}</b><small>环节</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(readyCount)}</b><small>Ready</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(previewOnlyCount)}</b><small>Preview</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(blockedCount)}</b><small>Blocked</small></span>
      </div>
      {steps.length ? (
