        <div className="vkpi-repair-closure-status__steps">
          {steps.map((step) => {
            const evidence = Array.isArray(step.evidence) ? step.evidence.slice(0, 3) : [];
            const statusKey = String(step.status || 'blocked');
            return (
              <article className={`is-${statusKey.replace(/_/g, '-')}`} key={String(step.key || step.label)}>
                <div>
                  <strong>{String(step.label || step.key || '-')}</strong>
                  <em>{statusKey} · {compact(numberValue(step.score))}%</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(step.detail || '-')}</p>
                <small>{String(step.endpoint || '-')}</small>
                {evidence.length ? <span>{evidence.join(' · ')}</span> : null}
                <i>{String(step.nextAction || step.next_action || '-')}</i>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-closure-status__empty">暂无闭环步骤。当前页面不会打开真实写入。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairAuditWritePreviewPanel({
  preview,
}: {
  preview?: VkpiRepairAuditWritePreviewResponse | null;
}) {
  const events = Array.isArray(preview?.events) ? preview.events : [];
  const eventCount = numberValue(preview?.eventCount ?? preview?.event_count ?? events.length);
  const auditWritten = Boolean(preview?.auditWritten ?? preview?.audit_written);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-audit-preview" aria-label="审计写入预览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-audit-preview__head">
        <div>
          <span>Audit write preview</span>
          <b>审计写入预览</b>
          <p>{preview ? '未来真实保存时，每个 mutation 应写入的 business audit 事件。当前只读，不写 audit 表。' : '未读取到审计预览；真实保存继续关闭。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{auditWritten ? '异常：已写入' : `${compact(eventCount)} events · no-write`}</em>
      </div>
      {events.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-audit-preview__events">
          {events.map((event) => {
            const action = String(event.actionType || event.action_type || '-');
            const target = `${String(event.targetType || event.target_type || '-')}:${String(event.targetId || event.target_id || '-')}`;
            const metadata = asRecord(event.metadata);
            const metadataEntries = Object.entries(metadata).slice(0, 6);
            return (
              <article key={action}>
                <div>
                  <strong>{action}</strong>
                  <em>{event.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(event.detail || '-')}</p>
                <small>{String(event.table || 'vkpi_business_audit_logs')} · {target}</small>
                <div>
                  {metadataEntries.map(([key, value]) => (
                    <span key={key}><b>{key}</b>{String(value ?? '-')}</span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-audit-preview__empty">暂无事件明细。当前页面不会写 audit 表。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairTaskDraftPreviewPanel({
  preview,
}: {
  preview?: VkpiRepairTaskDraftPreviewResponse | null;
}) {
  const drafts = Array.isArray(preview?.drafts) ? preview.drafts : [];
  const draftCount = numberValue(preview?.draftCount ?? preview?.draft_count ?? drafts.length);
  const taskEnqueued = Boolean(preview?.taskEnqueued ?? preview?.task_enqueued);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-task-preview" aria-label="任务草稿预览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-task-preview__head">
        <div>
          <span>Task draft preview</span>
          <b>任务草稿预览</b>
          <p>{preview ? '未来真实保存后会生成的开发 / 运营任务草稿。当前只展示，不进队列、不派单。' : '未读取到任务草稿预览；Repair Center 继续禁止自动派任务。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{taskEnqueued ? '异常：已入队' : `${compact(draftCount)} drafts · no queue`}</em>
      </div>
      {drafts.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-task-preview__cards">
          {drafts.map((draft) => {
            const draftId = String(draft.draftId || draft.draft_id || draft.title || draft.taskType || draft.task_type || '-');
            const taskType = String(draft.taskType || draft.task_type || '-');
            const source = `${String(draft.sourceType || draft.source_type || '-')}:${String(draft.sourceId || draft.source_id || '-')}`;
            const acceptance = Array.isArray(draft.acceptance) ? draft.acceptance.slice(0, 4) : [];
            const commands = Array.isArray(draft.validationCommands || draft.validation_commands)
              ? (draft.validationCommands || draft.validation_commands || []).slice(0, 3)
              : [];
            return (
              <article key={draftId}>
                <div>
                  <strong>{String(draft.title || '-')}</strong>
                  <em>{String(draft.priority || 'normal')} · {String(draft.status || 'draft_only')}</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(draft.objective || '-')}</p>
                <small>{taskType} · {String(draft.owner || '-')} · {source}</small>
                {acceptance.length ? (
                  <div className="vkpi-repair-task-preview__acceptance">
                    {acceptance.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
                  </div>
                ) : null}
                {commands.length ? (
                  <div className="vkpi-repair-task-preview__commands">
                    {commands.map((item) => <code key={item}>{item}</code>)}
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-task-preview__empty">暂无任务草稿。当前页面不会创建真实任务。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairEvidenceRefPreviewPanel({
  preview,
}: {
  preview?: VkpiRepairEvidenceRefPreviewResponse | null;
}) {
  const refs = Array.isArray(preview?.refs) ? preview.refs : [];
  const refCount = numberValue(preview?.refCount ?? preview?.ref_count ?? refs.length);
  const evidenceWritten = Boolean(preview?.evidenceWritten ?? preview?.evidence_written);

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-evidence-preview" aria-label="证据引用预览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-evidence-preview__head">
        <div>
          <span>Evidence refs preview</span>
          <b>证据引用预览</b>
          <p>{preview ? '未来真实保存时，应把修复卡片、提案、实现包、交接映射和写入 gate 关联到 evidence refs。当前只展示，不写 evidence。' : '未读取到证据引用预览；Evidence 写入继续关闭。'}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{evidenceWritten ? '异常：已写入' : `${compact(refCount)} refs · no-write`}</em>
      </div>
      {refs.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-evidence-preview__refs">
          {refs.map((ref) => {
            const refId = String(ref.refId || ref.ref_id || ref.title || ref.refType || ref.ref_type || '-');
            const refType = String(ref.refType || ref.ref_type || '-');
            const source = `${String(ref.sourceType || ref.source_type || '-')}:${String(ref.sourceId || ref.source_id || '-')}`;
            const entity = `${String(ref.entityType || ref.entity_type || '-')}:${String(ref.entityId || ref.entity_id || '-')}`;
            const fields = Array.isArray(ref.fields) ? ref.fields.slice(0, 7) : [];
            return (
              <article key={refId}>
                <div>
                  <strong>{String(ref.title || '-')}</strong>
                  <em>{ref.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}</em>
                </div>
                <p>{String(ref.summary || '-')}</p>
                <small>{refType} · {source} · {entity}</small>
                {fields.length ? (
                  <div>
                    {fields.map((field) => (
                      <span key={`${refId}-${field.field}`}><b>{String(field.field || '-')}</b>{String(field.value || '-')}</span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-evidence-preview__empty">暂无证据引用明细。当前页面不会写 evidence 表。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function readinessNumber(value: string): number {
  const parsed = Number(String(value || '').replace('%', ''));
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, parsed)) : 0;
}

function severityFromFocus(value: RepairRoadmapFocusPayload): RepairSeverity {
  if (value.statusTone === 'planned') return 'medium';
  if (value.statusTone === 'partial') return 'medium';
  return 'low';
}

function categoryFromFocus(value: RepairRoadmapFocusPayload): RepairCategory {
  if (value.key.includes('competitor') || value.key.includes('google') || value.key.includes('reddit')) return 'market';
  if (value.key.includes('gemini') || value.key.includes('summary') || value.key.includes('agent')) return 'agent';
  if (value.key.includes('learning')) return 'data';
  return 'ui';
}

function repairCardFromRoadmapFocus(value: RepairRoadmapFocusPayload): RepairCard {
  return {
    id: `repair-roadmap-${value.key}`,
    category: categoryFromFocus(value),
    severity: severityFromFocus(value),
    title: `接入前检查：${value.title}`,
    body: `${value.output} 当前状态：${value.statusLabel}。`,
    evidence: [
      `来源：${value.source}`,
      `准备度：${value.readiness}`,
      `数据依赖：${value.dataNeeded}`,
      `准入门槛：${value.gate}`,
      `原入口：${value.actionLabel} / ${value.actionPage}`,
    ],
    allowed: ['生成接入检查', '补 ADR / runbook', '列预算策略', '人工确认排队'],
    blocked: ['自动调用外部 API', '自动跑 Gemini/LLM', '无预算批量抓取', '自动部署生产'],
    suggestedFix: `先完成接入前检查：${value.budgetPolicy}`,
    owner: categoryFromFocus(value) === 'market' ? '市场脑 / 数据' : categoryFromFocus(value) === 'agent' ? 'Agent / 智能层' : '产品 / 前端',
    readiness: readinessNumber(value.readiness),
  };
}

function repairToIntelligenceCard(card: RepairCard, approval?: RepairApproval, statusOverride?: IntelligenceCardStatus): IntelligenceCardModel {
  const checklist = integrationChecklistForCard(card);
  return {
    id: card.id,
    type: 'repair',
    priority: card.severity === 'high' ? 'high' : card.severity === 'medium' ? 'medium' : 'low',
    status: repairStatus(card, approval, statusOverride),
    title: card.title,
    summary: card.body,
    entityType: `${categoryLabel(card.category)}修复`,
    freshnessLabel: approvalLabel(approval),
    sourceLabel: card.evidence[0] || 'Repair Center',
    evidence: [
      ...card.evidence.map((item, index) => ({ label: `证据 ${index + 1}`, source: '诊断来源', value: item })),
      { label: '建议动作', source: 'repair_policy', value: card.suggestedFix },
      { label: '允许范围', source: 'allowlist', value: card.allowed.join(' / ') },
      { label: '禁止范围', source: 'blocklist', value: card.blocked.join(' / ') },
      { label: '字段契约', source: checklist.title, value: checklist.dataFields.slice(0, 6).join(' / ') },
      { label: '预算规则', source: checklist.title, value: checklist.budgetRules.slice(0, 4).join(' / ') },
      { label: '失败状态', source: checklist.title, value: checklist.failureStates.slice(0, 5).join(' / ') },
      { label: '人工确认项', source: checklist.title, value: checklist.manualConfirmations.slice(0, 4).join(' / ') },
      { label: '负责人', source: 'owner', value: card.owner },
      { label: '人工确认', source: 'local state', value: approvalLabel(approval) },
    ],
    actions: [
      { label: '查看证据', kind: 'primary' },
      { label: '生成本地提案', kind: approval?.status === 'confirmed' ? 'disabled' : 'secondary' },
      { label: '人工确认排队', kind: approval?.status === 'confirmed' ? 'disabled' : 'secondary' },
      { label: '忽略本轮', kind: approval?.status === 'dismissed' ? 'disabled' : 'secondary' },
    ],
    metadata: {
      category: card.category,
      severity: card.severity,
      readiness: card.readiness,
      approval_status: approval?.status,
      approval_updated_at: approval?.updatedAt,
    },
  };
}

function repairCardsFromState(agents: VkpiAgentInboxItem[], market: Row, apiToken?: string): RepairCard[] {
  const summary = asRecord(market.summary);
  const missingBrief = !agents.some((item) => item.agent_id === 'brief' && item.status === 'active');
  const missingRecommendation = !agents.some((item) => item.agent_id === 'recommendation' && item.status === 'active');
  const missingMarket = numberValue(summary.signals_loaded) <= 0;
  const activeAgents = agents.filter((item) => item.status === 'active').length;
  const warningAgents = agents.filter((item) => item.status === 'warning' || item.passed === false).length;
  const next: RepairCard[] = [
    {
      id: 'repair-dashboard-intelligence-layer',
      category: 'ui',
      severity: 'high',
      title: '管理主控智能层需要持续收敛',
      body: 'Dashboard 已出现智能层入口，但仍需要把趋势、竞品、平台建议、Agent 状态继续压成老板可读的行动摘要。',
      evidence: ['DashboardPremium.tsx', 'dashboard/copilot-brief', 'dashboard/agents-status'],
      allowed: ['前端展示', '中文文案', '只读 drawer', '本地状态'],
      blocked: ['自动外抓', '自动 LLM 调用', '生产部署'],
      suggestedFix: '继续把智能层模块化，所有判断必须带证据和准备度。',
      owner: '产品 / 前端',
      readiness: 68,
    },
    {
      id: 'repair-employee-action-loop',
      category: 'ui',
      severity: 'medium',
      title: '员工工作台动作闭环需要落表前先跑顺',
      body: '员工行动卡已支持本地接受、延后、完成；下一步才是后端 audit 和真实任务表。',
      evidence: ['CommandCenter.tsx', 'intelligenceActionFeedback.ts', 'ProjectDetailView.tsx'],
      allowed: ['本地反馈', '任务 draft', '页面跳转', '人工确认'],
      blocked: ['自动创建真实任务', '自动修改项目阶段'],
      suggestedFix: '先观察本地工作流，再设计 audit 表和任务写库。',
      owner: '产品 / 工作流',
      readiness: 62,
    },
  ];

  if (!apiToken) {
    next.push({
      id: 'repair-no-token',
      category: 'ops',
      severity: 'medium',
      title: '当前没有登录 token，诊断只能使用本地兜底',
      body: 'Repair Center 需要真实 API 才能判断 Agent、市场信号和推荐 backlog。',
      evidence: ['frontend runtime', 'apiToken missing'],
      allowed: ['显示空态', '提示登录', '保留只读策略'],
      blocked: ['伪造线上健康', '自动触发同步'],
      suggestedFix: '登录后重新打开修复中心读取真实诊断。',
      owner: '运维 / 管理员',
      readiness: 30,
    });
  }

  if (missingBrief) {
    next.push({
      id: 'repair-brief-empty',
      category: 'agent',
      severity: 'medium',
      title: 'Brief Agent 输出为空或没有可执行候选',
      body: '老板首页不能显示英文工程空态，需要用推荐、风险、Agent、KOL 池状态做中文业务兜底。',
      evidence: ['dashboard/copilot-brief', 'Brief Agent v0'],
      allowed: ['兜底文案', '证据说明', '进入智能中心'],
      blocked: ['自动生成无证据建议', '批量 LLM'],
      suggestedFix: '继续用已有信号拼业务 brief；LLM summary 后续走预算 ledger。',
      owner: 'Agent / 智能层',
      readiness: 48,
    });
  }

  if (missingRecommendation) {
    next.push({
      id: 'repair-recommendation-empty',
      category: 'agent',
      severity: 'medium',
      title: 'Recommendation Agent 暂无可用推荐动作',
      body: '推荐为空时应显示待反馈 backlog、KOL 池状态或下一步采集条件，不能让员工只靠搜索添加。',
      evidence: ['dashboard/tasks', 'dashboard/agents/inbox', 'recommendation feedback backlog'],
      allowed: ['只读诊断', '反馈 draft', '候选发现入口'],
      blocked: ['自动联系 KOL', '批量深扫'],
      suggestedFix: '先补推荐反馈和 KOL 发现入口，再做真实推荐写库。',
      owner: '推荐 / KOL 池',
      readiness: 44,
    });
  }

  if (missingMarket) {
    next.push({
      id: 'repair-market-empty',
      category: 'market',
      severity: 'low',
      title: 'Market Radar v0 外部趋势源尚未接入',
      body: '当前只读聚合既有品牌/竞品信号；Google、Reddit、News、Trends 需要按预算和审计小口接入。',
      evidence: ['market-intelligence/v0', 'brand-signals'],
      allowed: ['显示接入状态', '展示已有信号', '生成接入计划'],
      blocked: ['无预算外抓', '无审计采集', '全网批量抓取'],
      suggestedFix: '先做外部源接入 ADR，再启用小口采集和成本 ledger。',
      owner: '市场脑 / 数据',
      readiness: 26,
    });
  }

  if (warningAgents > 0 || (agents.length > 0 && activeAgents < agents.length)) {
    next.push({
      id: 'repair-agent-health',
      category: 'agent',
      severity: warningAgents ? 'high' : 'medium',
      title: 'Agent 在线状态需要复核',
      body: `${activeAgents}/${agents.length} 个 Agent active，${warningAgents} 个 warning。当前只展示状态，不自动重跑。`,
      evidence: ['dashboard/agents/inbox', 'dashboard/agents-status'],
      allowed: ['只读告警', '人工确认', '生成复核任务'],
      blocked: ['自动重跑 Agent', '自动写生产配置'],
      suggestedFix: '把 warning Agent 逐个进入 evidence drawer，看 artifact 和失败原因。',
      owner: 'Agent / 运维',
      readiness: warningAgents ? 54 : 42,
    });
  }

  return next;
}

function RepairProposalPanel({
  card,
  source,
  approval,
  record,
  serverDryRun,
  serverCancelDryRun,
  dryRunMessage,
  cancelDryRunMessage,
  copyMessage,
  onDraft,
  onConfirm,
  onDismiss,
  onCopyRecord,
}: {
  card?: IntelligenceCardModel;
  source?: RepairCard;
  approval?: RepairApproval;
  record?: RepairExecutionRecord;
  serverDryRun?: VkpiRepairProposalDryRunResponse | null;
  serverCancelDryRun?: VkpiRepairProposalCancelResponse | null;
  dryRunMessage?: string;
  cancelDryRunMessage?: string;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onDraft: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onDismiss: () => void;
  onCopyRecord: () => void;
}) {
  if (!card || !source) {
    return (
      <div className="vkpi-repair-proposal">
        <b>修复提案</b>
        <p>选择一条问题后，系统会生成只读修复建议。不会自动执行 patch。</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const proposal = dryRunProposalFromCard(source, approval);
  const exportPayload = record ? buildRepairExecutionExportPayload(source, record, proposal) : null;
  const exportJson = exportPayload ? JSON.stringify(exportPayload, null, 2) : '';
  const serverProposal = serverDryRun?.proposal;
  const auditPreview = serverDryRun?.auditPreview || serverDryRun?.audit_preview;
  const serverPersisted = Boolean(serverDryRun?.persisted);
  const serverDryRunFlag = Boolean(serverDryRun?.dryRun ?? serverDryRun?.dry_run);
  const persistencePreview = repairPersistencePreviewFrom(serverDryRun);
  const cancelPersistencePreview = repairPersistencePreviewFrom(serverCancelDryRun);
  const cancelPersisted = Boolean(serverCancelDryRun?.persisted);
  const cancelReason = String(serverCancelDryRun?.cancelReason || serverCancelDryRun?.cancel_reason || '');
  const cancelStatus = String(serverCancelDryRun?.nextStatus || serverCancelDryRun?.next_status || '');

  return (
    <div className="vkpi-repair-proposal">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-proposal__head">
        <div>
          <b>修复提案</b>
          <span>{approvalLabel(approval)} · 准备度 {source.readiness}%</span>
        </div>
        <em>{categoryLabel(source.category)}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-dryrun-card">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-dryrun-card__head">
          <div>
            <strong>{proposal.proposalId}</strong>
            <span>{proposal.statusLabel}</span>
          </div>
          <em>{approval?.updatedAt ? formatTime(approval.updatedAt) : '未生成'}</em>
        </div>
        <p>{proposal.objective}</p>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-dryrun-grid">
          <section>
            <b>范围</b>
            {proposal.scope.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <b>执行草案</b>
            {proposal.patchPlan.map((item, index) => <span key={item}>{index + 1}. {item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <b>验收</b>
            {proposal.acceptance.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <b>回滚</b>
            {proposal.rollback.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
        </div>
        <small>{proposal.decisionGate}</small>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__head">
          <div>
            <b>本地执行记录</b>
            <span>{record ? `${record.recordId} · ${approvalLabel({ status: record.status, updatedAt: record.updatedAt })}` : '点击生成本地提案后创建，不写库。'}</span>
          </div>
          <em>{record ? formatTime(record.updatedAt) : '未创建'}</em>
        </div>
        {record ? (
          <>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__grid">
              <span><strong>proposal_id</strong>{record.proposalId}</span>
              <span><strong>card_id</strong>{record.cardId}</span>
              <span><strong>字段契约</strong>{record.fieldContractSummary}</span>
              <span><strong>预算规则</strong>{record.budgetSummary}</span>
              <span><strong>证据回链</strong>{record.evidenceSummary}</span>
              <span><strong>失败状态</strong>{record.failureStateSummary}</span>
              <span><strong>人工确认</strong>{record.manualConfirmationSummary}</span>
              <span><strong>未来写入</strong>{record.nextWriteTargets.join(' / ')}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__actions">
              <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyRecord}>复制 JSON 草案</button>
              {copyMessage ? <span role="status">{copyMessage}</span> : null}
            </div>
            <details className="vkpi-repair-execution-json">
              <summary>查看本地 JSON 草案</summary>
              <pre>{exportJson}</pre>
            </details>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p>当前只有预览草案。生成后会记录 proposal id、字段契约、证据回链、预算规则、失败状态和未来写入目标。</p>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-flow">
        {['诊断问题', '生成提案', '人工确认', '后续执行'].map((step, index) => (
          <span className={index === 0 || approval ? 'is-active' : ''} key={step}>
            <i>{index + 1}</i>
            {step}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-policy-grid">
        <section>
          <strong>允许</strong>
          {source.allowed.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section className="is-blocked">
          <strong>禁止</strong>
          {source.blocked.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__head">
          <div>
            <b>后端 dry-run</b>
            <span>{serverDryRun ? '已返回预检，不写库、不创建任务。' : '点击生成本地提案后尝试请求后端 dry-run。'}</span>
          </div>
          <em>{serverDryRunFlag ? 'dryRun=true' : '未请求'}</em>
        </div>
        {serverDryRun ? (
          <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__grid">
            <span><strong>proposal_id</strong>{String(serverProposal?.proposalId || serverProposal?.proposal_id || '-')}</span>
            <span><strong>persisted</strong>{String(serverPersisted)}</span>
            <span><strong>audit action</strong>{String(auditPreview?.action || '-')}</span>
            <span><strong>blocked</strong>{(serverDryRun.blockedActions || serverDryRun.blocked_actions || []).join(' / ') || '-'}</span>
            <span><strong>guardrails</strong>{(serverDryRun.guardrails || []).join(' / ') || '-'}</span>
            <span><strong>next</strong>{String(serverDryRun.nextStep || serverDryRun.next_step || '-')}</span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p>后端 dry-run 只用于验证提案契约和审计预览；不会落库、不会调用 provider、不会生成真实任务。</p>
        )}
        <RepairPersistencePreviewBlock preview={persistencePreview} title="proposal 落库预览" />
        {dryRunMessage ? <small role="status">{dryRunMessage}</small> : null}
      </div>
      {(serverCancelDryRun || cancelDryRunMessage) ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record">
          <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__head">
            <div>
              <b>取消 dry-run</b>
              <span>{serverCancelDryRun ? `next=${cancelStatus || 'cancelled'} · persisted=${String(cancelPersisted)}` : '忽略本轮后会请求后端 cancel dry-run。'}</span>
            </div>
            <em>{serverCancelDryRun ? 'dryRun=true' : '未请求'}</em>
          </div>
          {serverCancelDryRun ? (
            <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-record__grid">
              <span><strong>proposal_id</strong>{String(serverCancelDryRun.proposalId || serverCancelDryRun.proposal_id || '-')}</span>
              <span><strong>previous</strong>{String(serverCancelDryRun.previousStatus || serverCancelDryRun.previous_status || '-')}</span>
              <span><strong>next</strong>{cancelStatus || '-'}</span>
              <span><strong>reason</strong>{cancelReason || '-'}</span>
              <span><strong>audit action</strong>{String((serverCancelDryRun.auditPreview || serverCancelDryRun.audit_preview)?.action || '-')}</span>
              <span><strong>blocked</strong>{(serverCancelDryRun.blockedActions || serverCancelDryRun.blocked_actions || []).join(' / ') || '-'}</span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p>取消 dry-run 只用于关闭本轮修复提案预览；不会删除记录、不会写 audit、不会创建任务。</p>
          )}
          <RepairPersistencePreviewBlock preview={cancelPersistencePreview} title="cancel 落库预览" />
          {cancelDryRunMessage ? <small role="status">{cancelDryRunMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <p>{source.suggestedFix}</p>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-proposal__actions">
        <button className="vkpi-button" type="button" onClick={onDraft}>生成本地提案</button>
        <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--primary" type="button" onClick={onConfirm}>人工确认排队</button>
        <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onDismiss}>忽略本轮</button>
      </div>
      <small>当前只写页面本地状态；不会生成真实 patch、不会触发 provider、不会部署。</small>
    </div>
  );
}

function RepairExecutionQueuePanel({
  items,
  copyMessage,
  storageMeta,
  storageMessage,
  onCopyQueue,
  onRestoreQueue,
  onClearQueue,
  onSelectRecord,
}: {
  items: RepairExecutionQueueItem[];
  copyMessage?: string;
  storageMeta?: RepairExecutionStorageMeta | null;
  storageMessage?: string;
  onCopyQueue: () => void;
  onRestoreQueue: () => void;
  onClearQueue: () => void;
  onSelectRecord: (cardId: string) => void;
}) {
  const confirmedCount = items.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'confirmed').length;
  const dismissedCount = items.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'dismissed').length;
  const draftedCount = items.filter((item) => item.record.status === 'drafted').length;

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue" aria-label="本地提案队列">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__head">
        <div>
          <span>Local proposal queue</span>
          <b>本地提案队列</b>
          <p>集中查看本页生成的修复草案；复制出的 JSON 只用于人工复核和后续接 API 设计。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__actions">
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" disabled={!items.length} type="button" onClick={onCopyQueue}>复制队列 JSON</button>
          <button className="vkpi-button" disabled={!storageMeta?.count} type="button" onClick={onRestoreQueue}>恢复上次队列</button>
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" disabled={!items.length && !storageMeta?.count} type="button" onClick={onClearQueue}>清空本地队列</button>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
          {storageMessage ? <small role="status">{storageMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__storage">
        <span><strong>浏览器本地保存</strong>{storageMeta?.count ? `${compact(storageMeta.count)} 条 · ${formatTime(storageMeta.updatedAt)}` : '暂无可恢复队列'}</span>
        <span><strong>保存范围</strong>只保存 proposal record，不保存 token、provider 结果或数据库状态。</span>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__stats">
        <span><b>{compact(items.length)}</b><small>本地记录</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(confirmedCount)}</b><small>人工确认</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(draftedCount)}</b><small>提案草稿</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(dismissedCount)}</b><small>本轮忽略</small></span>
      </div>
      {items.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__list">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <button
              className={`vkpi-repair-execution-queue__item is-${item.record.status}`}
              key={item.record.recordId}
              onClick={() => onSelectRecord(item.record.cardId)}
              type="button"
            >
              <span>{approvalLabel({ status: item.record.status, updatedAt: item.record.updatedAt })}</span>
              <b>{item.record.title}</b>
              <small>{item.record.proposalId} · {categoryLabel(item.category)} · {severityLabel(item.severity)}</small>
              <em>{item.owner} · 准备度 {item.readiness}% · {formatTime(item.record.updatedAt)}</em>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-execution-queue__empty">还没有本地提案。先选择左侧问题，点击“生成本地提案”或“人工确认排队”。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairImplementationHandoffLogPanel({
  entries,
  message,
  serverHandoff,
  dryRunMessage,
  serverFutureWrite,
  futureWriteDryRunMessage,
  mappingCopyMessage,
  onClear,
  onCopyFutureMapping,
  onSelectRecord,
}: {
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  message?: string;
  serverHandoff?: VkpiRepairHandoffDryRunResponse | null;
  dryRunMessage?: string;
  serverFutureWrite?: VkpiRepairFutureWritePreviewResponse | null;
  futureWriteDryRunMessage?: string;
  mappingCopyMessage?: string;
  onClear: () => void;
  onCopyFutureMapping: () => void;
  onSelectRecord: (cardId: string) => void;
}) {
  const latest = entries[0];
  const futureWritePreview = latest ? buildRepairHandoffFutureWritePreview(latest) : null;
  const serverEntry = asRecord(serverHandoff?.handoffEntry || serverHandoff?.handoff_entry);
  const serverLogId = String(serverEntry.logId || serverEntry.log_id || '');
  const serverPersisted = Boolean(serverHandoff?.persisted);
  const serverFutureWriteMode = String(serverFutureWrite?.writeMode || serverFutureWrite?.write_mode || '');
  const serverFutureWritePersisted = Boolean(serverFutureWrite?.persisted);
  const persistencePreview = repairPersistencePreviewFrom(serverHandoff);
  const latestReview = latest
    ? repairImplementationReviewSummary(latest, latest.payload.implementation, latest.preflightPassed)
    : null;

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log" aria-label="实现包交接历史">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__head">
        <div>
          <span>Developer handoff log</span>
          <b>实现包交接历史</b>
          <p>只记录已经复制过的实现包，用作后续接 audit_action 或开发任务表的本地占位。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__actions">
          <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" disabled={!entries.length} type="button" onClick={onClear}>清空交接历史</button>
          {message ? <small role="status">{message}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__stats">
        <span><b>{compact(entries.length)}</b><small>交接包</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(entries.filter((item) => item.preflightPassed).length)}</b><small>Preflight passed</small></span>
        <span><b>{latest ? formatTime(latest.copiedAt) : '-'}</b><small>最近复制</small></span>
      </div>
      {latest && latestReview ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-review" aria-label="最近实现包人工 review 摘要">
          <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-review__head">
            <div>
              <b>最近实现包 Review</b>
              <span>{latest.packageId} · {latest.title}</span>
            </div>
            <em className={`is-${latestReview.riskTone}`}>{latestReview.riskLabel}</em>
          </div>
          <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-review__metrics">
            <span><b>{latest.candidateFiles.length}</b><small>影响文件</small></span>
            <span><b>{latest.validationCommands.length}</b><small>验收命令</small></span>
            <span><b>{latest.payload.implementation.rollbackBoundary.length}</b><small>回滚边界</small></span>
            <span><b>{latestReview.migrationNeeded ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>迁移 Review</small></span>
            <span><b>{latestReview.confirmationLabel}</b><small>{latestReview.confirmationOwner}</small></span>
          </div>
          <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-review__lists">
            <section>
              <strong>影响文件</strong>
              {latest.candidateFiles.slice(0, 4).map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
            </section>
            <section>
              <strong>验收命令</strong>
              {latest.validationCommands.slice(0, 4).map((item) => <code key={item}>{item}</code>)}
            </section>
            <section>
              <strong>下一步 Gate</strong>
              <span>{latestReview.preflightLabel}</span>
              <span>{latestReview.migrationLabel}</span>
              <span>{latestReview.confirmationLabel}</span>
            </section>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {(serverHandoff || dryRunMessage) ? (
        <>
          <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-mapping" aria-label="后端交接 dry-run 回执">
            <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-mapping__head">
              <div>
                <b>后端 handoff dry-run</b>
                <span>{serverHandoff ? `${serverLogId || 'handoff preview'} · persisted=${String(serverPersisted)}` : '尚未请求后端 handoff dry-run'}</span>
              </div>
              {dryRunMessage ? <small role="status">{dryRunMessage}</small> : null}
            </div>
          </div>
          <RepairPersistencePreviewBlock preview={persistencePreview} title="handoff log 落库预览" />
        </>
      ) : null}
      {futureWritePreview ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-mapping" aria-label="未来写入映射预览">
          <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-mapping__head">
            <div>
              <b>未来写入映射预览</b>
              <span>
                {serverFutureWrite
                  ? `后端校验 ${serverFutureWriteMode || 'preview_only'} · persisted=${String(serverFutureWritePersisted)}`
                  : '只读字段草案，不写 audit、不建任务、不写 evidence。'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-mapping__actions">
              <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={onCopyFutureMapping}>复制映射 JSON</button>
              {futureWriteDryRunMessage ? <small role="status">{futureWriteDryRunMessage}</small> : null}
              {mappingCopyMessage ? <small role="status">{mappingCopyMessage}</small> : null}
            </div>
          </div>
          <section>
            <strong>audit_action</strong>
            {futureWritePreview.auditAction.map((item) => (
              <span key={item.field}><b>{item.field}</b>{item.value}</span>
            ))}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>开发任务</strong>
            {futureWritePreview.developerTask.map((item) => (
              <span key={item.field}><b>{item.field}</b>{item.value}</span>
            ))}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>Evidence refs</strong>
            {futureWritePreview.evidenceRefs.map((item) => (
              <span key={item.field}><b>{item.field}</b>{item.value}</span>
            ))}
          </section>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {entries.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__list">
          {entries.slice(0, 6).map((entry) => (
            <button
              className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__item"
              key={entry.logId}
              onClick={() => onSelectRecord(entry.cardId)}
              type="button"
            >
              <span>{entry.packageId}</span>
              <b>{entry.title}</b>
              <small>{categoryLabel(entry.category)} · {severityLabel(entry.severity)} · {entry.owner}</small>
              <em>{entry.candidateFiles.length} 个文件 · {entry.validationCommands.length} 条验收 · {formatTime(entry.copiedAt)}</em>
              <i>
                {repairImplementationReviewSummary(entry, entry.payload.implementation, entry.preflightPassed).migrationLabel}
              </i>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-handoff-log__empty">还没有复制过实现包。完成五项执行前检查后，点击“复制实现包 JSON”会写入这里。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairControlledExecutionQueuePanel({
  entries,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyEvidencePackage,
  onSelectRecord,
}: {
  entries: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[];
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyEvidencePackage: (item: RepairControlledExecutionItem) => void;
  onSelectRecord: (cardId: string) => void;
}) {
  const items = entries.map((entry) => repairControlledExecutionStatus(entry));
  const counts = items.reduce<Record<RepairControlledExecutionStatus, number>>(
    (acc, item) => {
      acc[item.status] += 1;
      return acc;
    },
    { pending_review: 0, confirmed: 0, needs_evidence: 0, blocked: 0 },
  );

  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue" aria-label="受控执行队列">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__head">
        <div>
          <span>Controlled execution queue</span>
          <b>受控执行队列</b>
          <p>这里仅把实现包排成下一轮候选，不执行 patch、不写库、不建任务。所有包必须先过人工 review 和准入 gate。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__actions">
          <em>0 自动执行</em>
          {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__stats">
        <span><b>{compact(items.length)}</b><small>候选包</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(counts.pending_review)}</b><small>待 review</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(counts.confirmed)}</b><small>已确认</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(counts.needs_evidence)}</b><small>需补证据</small></span>
        <span><b>{compact(counts.blocked)}</b><small>禁止执行</small></span>
      </div>

      <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__gates">
        <span><strong>准入 1</strong>backup-first + diff review</span>
        <span><strong>准入 2</strong>migration / 写入契约 review</span>
        <span><strong>准入 3</strong>验证命令明确且可回滚</span>
        <span><strong>仍禁止</strong>provider / LLM / Gemini / deploy 自动执行</span>
      </div>

      {items.length ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__list">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <article
              className={`vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__item is-${item.status}`}
              key={item.entry.logId}
            >
              <div>
                <span>{item.statusLabel}</span>
                <em>{item.review.riskLabel}</em>
              </div>
              <b>{item.entry.title}</b>
              <small>{item.entry.packageId} · {categoryLabel(item.entry.category)} · {severityLabel(item.entry.severity)} · {item.entry.owner}</small>
              <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__facts">
                <span><strong>影响文件</strong>{compact(item.entry.candidateFiles.length)}</span>
                <span><strong>验收命令</strong>{compact(item.entry.validationCommands.length)}</span>
                <span><strong>迁移 Review</strong>{item.review.migrationNeeded ? '需要' : '不需要'}</span>
                <span><strong>Preflight</strong>{item.review.preflightLabel}</span>
              </div>
              <p>{item.rationale}</p>
              <i>{item.nextGate}</i>
              <div className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__item-actions">
                <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" type="button" onClick={() => onSelectRecord(item.entry.cardId)}>查看</button>
                <button className="vkpi-button" type="button" onClick={() => onCopyEvidencePackage(item)}>复制证据包 JSON</button>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="vkpi-repair-controlled-queue__empty">还没有实现包候选。先完成本地提案、preflight，然后复制实现包 JSON，才会进入这里。</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairCapabilityStatusStrip({
  executionRecordCount,
  handoffLogCount,
  hasApiToken,
}: RepairCapabilityStripProps) {
  return (
    <section className="vkpi-repair-capability-strip" aria-label="Repair Center v0 能力状态">
      <article>
        <span>当前能力</span>
        <b>本地闭环已可用</b>
        <p>可生成提案、执行前检查、实现包 JSON、handoff log 和未来写入映射。</p>
        <small>{compact(executionRecordCount)} 条本地提案 · {compact(handoffLogCount)} 条交接历史</small>
      </article>
      <article className="is-blocked">
        <span>仍未接后端</span>
        <b>只读 / 本地状态</b>
        <p>尚未写 `audit_action`、未建开发任务、未写 evidence refs，也不触发 provider。</p>
        <small>{hasApiToken ? '当前可读真实诊断，但写入仍关闭' : '本地诊断模式，未读取线上 API'}</small>
      </article>
      <article className="is-next">
        <span>下一步真实 API</span>
        <b>先设计写入契约</b>
        <p>`repair_proposals`、`audit_action`、`task draft`、`evidence_refs` 四条链路分开接。</p>
        <small>必须 backup-first · migration review · 人工确认</small>
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPreflightPanel({
  record,
  state,
  serverPreflight,
  preflightMessage,
  onToggle,
}: {
  record?: RepairExecutionRecord;
  state?: RepairPreflightState;
  serverPreflight?: VkpiRepairPreflightResponse | null;
  preflightMessage?: string;
  onToggle: (key: RepairPreflightKey) => void;
}) {
  if (!record) {
    return (
      <section className="vkpi-repair-preflight">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-preflight__head">
          <div>
            <span>Preflight checklist</span>
            <b>执行前检查</b>
            <p>先生成本地提案，再逐项确认字段、预算、证据、失败状态和人工审批。</p>
          </div>
          <em>未创建</em>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const current = state || defaultRepairPreflightState();
  const items = repairPreflightItems(record);
  const doneCount = repairPreflightDoneCount(current);
  const isReady = repairPreflightComplete(current);
  const serverGateOpen = Boolean(serverPreflight?.gateOpen ?? serverPreflight?.gate_open);
  const serverPreflightRow = asRecord(serverPreflight?.preflight);
  const serverGateStatus = String(serverPreflightRow.gateStatus || serverPreflightRow.gate_status || (serverGateOpen ? 'ready' : 'not_synced'));
  const serverCompletedCount = numberValue(serverPreflightRow.completedCount || serverPreflightRow.completed_count);
  const persistencePreview = repairPersistencePreviewFrom(serverPreflight);

  return (
    <section className={`vkpi-repair-preflight${isReady ? ' is-ready' : ''}`} aria-label="执行前检查">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-preflight__head">
        <div>
          <span>Preflight checklist</span>
          <b>执行前检查</b>
          <p>只有五项全部确认后，才允许把这条本地提案推进到真实实现设计。</p>
        </div>
        <em>{isReady ? '可进入真实实现' : `${doneCount}/5 已确认`}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-preflight__items">
        {items.map((item) => (
          <label className={current[item.key] ? 'is-checked' : ''} key={item.key}>
            <input
              checked={current[item.key]}
              onChange={() => onToggle(item.key)}
              type="checkbox"
            />
            <span>
              <strong>{item.label}</strong>
              {item.summary}
            </span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-preflight__gate">
        <b>{isReady ? 'Gate open' : 'Gate closed'}</b>
        <span>{isReady ? '可以进入受控实现流程；仍需要 backup-first、代码 review 和验收。' : '未全部勾选前，只能保留为本地草案，不能执行真实修复。'}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-preflight__gate">
        <b>{serverGateOpen ? 'Backend gate ready' : 'Backend gate dry-run'}</b>
        <span>
          {serverPreflight
            ? `后端 dry-run：${serverCompletedCount}/5 · ${serverGateStatus} · persisted=${String(Boolean(serverPreflight.persisted))}`
            : '勾选检查项后会同步给后端 dry-run 校验；不会写库或创建任务。'}
        </span>
        {preflightMessage ? <small role="status">{preflightMessage}</small> : null}
      </div>
      <RepairPersistencePreviewBlock preview={persistencePreview} title="preflight 落库预览" />
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairImplementationPreviewPanel({
  source,
  approval,
  record,
  preflightState,
  serverPackage,
  packageMessage,
  copyMessage,
  onCopyPackage,
}: {
  source?: RepairCard;
  approval?: RepairApproval;
  record?: RepairExecutionRecord;
  preflightState?: RepairPreflightState;
  serverPackage?: VkpiRepairImplementationPackageResponse | null;
  packageMessage?: string;
  copyMessage?: string;
  onCopyPackage: () => void;
}) {
  if (!source || !record) {
    return (
      <section className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__head">
          <div>
            <span>Implementation package</span>
            <b>只读实现包预览</b>
            <p>生成本地提案并完成执行前检查后，这里会显示候选文件、patch 顺序、验收命令和回滚边界。</p>
          </div>
          <em>未生成</em>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const ready = repairPreflightComplete(preflightState);
  const doneCount = repairPreflightDoneCount(preflightState);
  const preview = buildRepairImplementationPreview(source, record, approval);
  const reviewSummary = repairImplementationReviewSummary(source, preview, ready);
  const serverPayload = asRecord(serverPackage?.implementationPackage || serverPackage?.implementation_package);
  const serverPackageId = String(serverPayload.packageId || serverPayload.package_id || '');
  const serverGateOpen = Boolean(serverPackage?.gateOpen ?? serverPackage?.gate_open);
  const serverPersisted = Boolean(serverPackage?.persisted);
  const persistencePreview = repairPersistencePreviewFrom(serverPackage);

  return (
    <section className={`vkpi-repair-implementation-preview${ready ? ' is-ready' : ''}`} aria-label="只读实现包预览">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__head">
        <div>
          <span>Implementation package</span>
          <b>只读实现包预览</b>
          <p>{ready ? '检查项已完成，可以把这条提案带入下一轮受控实现。' : `还有 ${5 - doneCount} 项检查未确认，当前只能预览不能执行。`}</p>
        </div>
        <em>{ready ? preview.packageId : `${doneCount}/5 blocked`}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__status">
        <span><strong>状态</strong>{ready ? preview.statusLabel : '等待执行前检查全部通过'}</span>
        <span><strong>边界</strong>只读预览，不自动 patch、不写库、不部署。</span>
      </div>
      {(serverPackage || packageMessage) ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__status">
          <span>
            <strong>后端 package dry-run</strong>
            {serverPackage
              ? `${serverPackageId || 'package preview'} · gate ${serverGateOpen ? 'ready' : 'blocked'} · persisted=${String(serverPersisted)}`
              : '尚未请求后端 implementation package dry-run'}
          </span>
          {packageMessage ? <span><strong>回执</strong>{packageMessage}</span> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <RepairPersistencePreviewBlock preview={persistencePreview} title="implementation package 落库预览" />
      <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__actions">
        <button className="vkpi-button vkpi-button--ghost" disabled={!ready} type="button" onClick={onCopyPackage}>复制实现包 JSON</button>
        {copyMessage ? <small role="status">{copyMessage}</small> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-review" aria-label="人工 review 交接卡">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-review__head">
          <div>
            <span>Human review package</span>
            <b>人工 Review 交接卡</b>
            <p>把这条修复包进入下一轮前必须看的信息压成一张卡：影响文件、验收命令、风险、回滚、迁移和确认方。</p>
          </div>
          <em className={`is-${reviewSummary.riskTone}`}>{reviewSummary.riskLabel}</em>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-review__metrics">
          <span><b>{preview.candidateFiles.length}</b><small>影响文件</small></span>
          <span><b>{preview.validationCommands.length}</b><small>验收命令</small></span>
          <span><b>{preview.rollbackBoundary.length}</b><small>回滚边界</small></span>
          <span><b>{reviewSummary.migrationNeeded ? '是' : '否'}</b><small>迁移 Review</small></span>
          <span><b>{reviewSummary.preflightLabel}</b><small>执行前检查</small></span>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-review__grid">
          <section>
            <strong>影响文件</strong>
            {preview.candidateFiles.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>验收命令</strong>
            {preview.validationCommands.map((item) => <code key={item}>{item}</code>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>回滚边界</strong>
            {preview.rollbackBoundary.slice(0, 5).map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>审批 / 确认</strong>
            <span>{reviewSummary.migrationLabel}</span>
            <span>{reviewSummary.confirmationLabel}</span>
            <span>Owner: {reviewSummary.confirmationOwner}</span>
            <span>{ready ? '可以复制实现包进入人工 review' : '未完成 preflight，不能进入执行'}</span>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
      {ready ? (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__grid">
          <section>
            <strong>候选文件</strong>
            {preview.candidateFiles.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>Patch 顺序</strong>
            {preview.patchOrder.map((item, index) => <span key={item}>{index + 1}. {item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>验收命令 / 输出</strong>
            {preview.validationCommands.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section>
            <strong>回滚边界</strong>
            {preview.rollbackBoundary.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
          <section className="is-blocked">
            <strong>仍然禁止</strong>
            {preview.blockedActions.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
          </section>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="vkpi-repair-implementation-preview__locked">
          <b>实现包已锁定</b>
          <span>先完成字段契约、预算、证据、失败状态、人工确认五项检查，再进入真实实现设计。</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function RepairPackagePanel({
  source,
  approval,
}: {
  source?: RepairCard;
  approval?: RepairApproval;
}) {
  if (!source) {
    return (
      <div className="vkpi-repair-package">
        <b>修复包详情</b>
        <p>选择一条修复项后，会显示涉及文件、执行步骤、风险控制和验收方式。</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const detail = repairPackageFromCard(source, approval);
  return (
    <div className="vkpi-repair-package">
      <div className="vkpi-repair-package__head">
        <div>
          <b>修复包详情</b>
          <span>{detail.scope}</span>
        </div>
        <em>{source.id}</em>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-package__grid">
        <section>
          <strong>涉及文件 / 入口</strong>
          {detail.targetFiles.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>执行顺序</strong>
          {detail.executionSteps.map((item, index) => <span key={item}>{index + 1}. {item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>风险控制</strong>
          {detail.risks.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>验收方式</strong>
          {detail.validation.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-checklist">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-checklist__head">
          <b>{detail.checklist.title}</b>
          <span>字段、预算、证据、失败状态和人工确认必须先闭环。</span>
        </div>
        <section>
          <strong>数据字段</strong>
          {detail.checklist.dataFields.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>预算规则</strong>
          {detail.checklist.budgetRules.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>证据回链</strong>
          {detail.checklist.evidenceBacklinks.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section>
          <strong>失败状态</strong>
          {detail.checklist.failureStates.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
        <section className="is-confirm">
          <strong>人工确认</strong>
          {detail.checklist.manualConfirmations.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
        </section>
      </div>
      <div className="vkpi-repair-package__nogos">
        <strong>本轮禁止动作</strong>
        {detail.noGo.map((item) => <span key={item}>{item}</span>)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function RepairCenterPage({ apiToken, viewMode }: WorkspacePageProps) {
  const [agents, setAgents] = useState<VkpiAgentInboxItem[]>([]);
  const [market, setMarket] = useState<Row>({});
  const [apiRepair, setApiRepair] = useState<VkpiRepairProposalsResponse | null>(null);
  const [closureStatus, setClosureStatus] = useState<VkpiRepairClosureStatusResponse | null>(null);
  const [persistenceReadiness, setPersistenceReadiness] = useState<VkpiRepairPersistenceReadinessResponse | null>(null);
  const [persistenceBoundary, setPersistenceBoundary] = useState<VkpiRepairPersistenceBoundaryResponse | null>(null);
  const [persistenceApprovalEnvelope, setPersistenceApprovalEnvelope] = useState<VkpiRepairPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeResponse | null>(null);
  const [persistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage, setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage] = useState('');
  const [persistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading, setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading] = useState(false);
  const [migrationPreflightManifest, setMigrationPreflightManifest] = useState<VkpiRepairMigrationPreflightManifestResponse | null>(null);
  const [migrationDraftPackage, setMigrationDraftPackage] = useState<VkpiRepairMigrationDraftPackageResponse | null>(null);
  const [auditWritePreview, setAuditWritePreview] = useState<VkpiRepairAuditWritePreviewResponse | null>(null);
  const [taskDraftPreview, setTaskDraftPreview] = useState<VkpiRepairTaskDraftPreviewResponse | null>(null);
  const [evidenceRefPreview, setEvidenceRefPreview] = useState<VkpiRepairEvidenceRefPreviewResponse | null>(null);
  const [roadmapFocus, setRoadmapFocus] = useState<RepairRoadmapFocusPayload | null>(null);
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<RepairCategoryFilter>('all');
  const [queueFilter, setQueueFilter] = useState<RepairQueueFilter>('all');
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState('');
  const [evidenceDrawer, setEvidenceDrawer] = useState<IntelligenceCardModel | null>(null);
  const [statusOverrides, setStatusOverrides] = useState<Record<string, IntelligenceCardStatus>>({});
  const [approvals, setApprovals] = useState<Record<string, RepairApproval>>({});
  const [executionRecords, setExecutionRecords] = useState<Record<string, RepairExecutionRecord>>({});
  const [preflightChecks, setPreflightChecks] = useState<Record<string, RepairPreflightState>>({});
  const [serverPreflightDryRuns, setServerPreflightDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairPreflightResponse>>({});
  const [preflightMessages, setPreflightMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [serverImplementationDryRuns, setServerImplementationDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairImplementationPackageResponse>>({});
  const [implementationDryRunMessages, setImplementationDryRunMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [serverHandoffDryRuns, setServerHandoffDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairHandoffDryRunResponse>>({});
  const [handoffDryRunMessages, setHandoffDryRunMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [serverFutureWriteDryRuns, setServerFutureWriteDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairFutureWritePreviewResponse>>({});
  const [futureWriteDryRunMessages, setFutureWriteDryRunMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [serverCancelDryRuns, setServerCancelDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairProposalCancelResponse>>({});
  const [cancelDryRunMessages, setCancelDryRunMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [serverDryRuns, setServerDryRuns] = useState<Record<string, VkpiRepairProposalDryRunResponse>>({});
  const [dryRunMessages, setDryRunMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [persistenceRejectReceipts, setPersistenceRejectReceipts] = useState<Record<string, RepairPersistenceRejectedReceipt>>({});
  const [feedbackDrafts, setFeedbackDrafts] = useState<Record<string, IntelligenceFeedbackDraft>>({});
  const [taskDrafts, setTaskDrafts] = useState<Record<string, IntelligenceTaskDraft>>({});
  const [recordCopyMessage, setRecordCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [queueCopyMessage, setQueueCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [implementationCopyMessage, setImplementationCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [handoffLogMessage, setHandoffLogMessage] = useState('');
  const [futureMappingCopyMessage, setFutureMappingCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [executionEvidenceCopyMessage, setExecutionEvidenceCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [preExecutionRunbookCopyMessage, setPreExecutionRunbookCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [v0ClosureReportCopyMessage, setV0ClosureReportCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [v0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage, setV0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [backendContractCopyMessage, setBackendContractCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [executionApprovalCopyMessage, setExecutionApprovalCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [finalHandoffManifestCopyMessage, setFinalHandoffManifestCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [previewCloseoutCopyMessage, setPreviewCloseoutCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [firstSliceCandidateCopyMessage, setFirstSliceCandidateCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [schemaDesignCopyMessage, setSchemaDesignCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [migrationReviewCopyMessage, setMigrationReviewCopyMessage] = useState('');
  const [implementationHandoffLog, setImplementationHandoffLog] = useState<RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry[]>([]);
  const [storageMessage, setStorageMessage] = useState('');
  const [storedQueueMeta, setStoredQueueMeta] = useState<RepairExecutionStorageMeta | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const focus = readRepairRoadmapFocus();
    const stored = readRepairExecutionStorage();
    setImplementationHandoffLog(readRepairImplementationHandoffLog());
    setStoredQueueMeta(stored?.count ? { count: stored.count, updatedAt: stored.updatedAt } : null);
    if (focus) {
      clearRepairRoadmapFocus();
      setRoadmapFocus(focus);
      setSelectedId(`repair-roadmap-${focus.key}`);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    if (!apiToken) {
      setAgents([]);
      setMarket({});
      setApiRepair(null);
      setClosureStatus(null);
      setPersistenceReadiness(null);
      setPersistenceBoundary(null);
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelope(null);
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage('');
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading(false);
      setMigrationPreflightManifest(null);
      setMigrationDraftPackage(null);
      setAuditWritePreview(null);
      setTaskDraftPreview(null);
      setEvidenceRefPreview(null);
      setError('当前前端没有登录 token；修复中心只展示本地诊断，不读取线上 API。');
      return () => { alive = false; };
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    Promise.allSettled([
      getDashboardAgentsInbox(apiToken, { limit: 30 }),
      getMarketIntelligenceV0(apiToken, 120),
      getRepairProposals(apiToken, { category: 'all', limit: 20 }),
      getRepairClosureStatus(apiToken),
      getRepairPersistenceReadiness(apiToken),
      getRepairPersistenceBoundary(apiToken),
      getRepairMigrationPreflightManifest(apiToken),
      getRepairMigrationDraftPackage(apiToken),
      getRepairAuditWritePreview(apiToken),
      getRepairTaskDraftPreview(apiToken),
      getRepairEvidenceRefPreview(apiToken),
    ])
      .then(([agentResult, marketResult, repairResult, closureStatusResult, readinessResult, boundaryResult, migrationManifestResult, migrationDraftResult, auditPreviewResult, taskDraftResult, evidenceRefResult]) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        if (agentResult.status === 'fulfilled') setAgents(agentResult.value.items || []);
        if (marketResult.status === 'fulfilled') setMarket(marketResult.value);
        if (repairResult.status === 'fulfilled') setApiRepair(repairResult.value);
        if (repairResult.status === 'rejected') setApiRepair(null);
        if (closureStatusResult.status === 'fulfilled') setClosureStatus(closureStatusResult.value);
        if (closureStatusResult.status === 'rejected') setClosureStatus(null);
        if (readinessResult.status === 'fulfilled') setPersistenceReadiness(readinessResult.value);
        if (readinessResult.status === 'rejected') setPersistenceReadiness(null);
        if (boundaryResult.status === 'fulfilled') setPersistenceBoundary(boundaryResult.value);
        if (boundaryResult.status === 'rejected') setPersistenceBoundary(null);
        if (migrationManifestResult.status === 'fulfilled') setMigrationPreflightManifest(migrationManifestResult.value);
        if (migrationManifestResult.status === 'rejected') setMigrationPreflightManifest(null);
        if (migrationDraftResult.status === 'fulfilled') setMigrationDraftPackage(migrationDraftResult.value);
        if (migrationDraftResult.status === 'rejected') setMigrationDraftPackage(null);
        if (auditPreviewResult.status === 'fulfilled') setAuditWritePreview(auditPreviewResult.value);
        if (auditPreviewResult.status === 'rejected') setAuditWritePreview(null);
        if (taskDraftResult.status === 'fulfilled') setTaskDraftPreview(taskDraftResult.value);
        if (taskDraftResult.status === 'rejected') setTaskDraftPreview(null);
        if (evidenceRefResult.status === 'fulfilled') setEvidenceRefPreview(evidenceRefResult.value);
        if (evidenceRefResult.status === 'rejected') setEvidenceRefPreview(null);
        if (agentResult.status === 'rejected' || marketResult.status === 'rejected' || repairResult.status === 'rejected' || closureStatusResult.status === 'rejected' || readinessResult.status === 'rejected' || boundaryResult.status === 'rejected' || migrationManifestResult.status === 'rejected' || migrationDraftResult.status === 'rejected' || auditPreviewResult.status === 'rejected' || taskDraftResult.status === 'rejected' || evidenceRefResult.status === 'rejected') {
          setError('部分诊断数据读取失败；Repair Center 仍保持只读，不会自动重跑。');
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [apiToken]);

  const repairCards = useMemo(() => {
    const backendCards = repairCardsFromBackendResponse(apiRepair);
    const base = repairCardsFromState(agents, market, apiToken);
    const merged = backendCards.length ? [...backendCards, ...base] : base;
    return roadmapFocus ? [repairCardFromRoadmapFocus(roadmapFocus), ...merged] : merged;
  }, [agents, apiRepair, apiToken, market, roadmapFocus]);

  const categoryCounts = useMemo(
    () => repairCategoryFilters.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.key === 'all' ? repairCards.length : repairCards.filter((card) => card.category === item.key).length;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<RepairCategoryFilter, number>),
    [repairCards],
  );

  const queueCounts = useMemo(
    () => repairQueueFilters.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = repairCards.filter((card) => matchesQueueFilter(card, approvals[card.id], statusOverrides[card.id], item.key)).length;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<RepairQueueFilter, number>),
    [approvals, repairCards, statusOverrides],
  );

  const visibleRepairCards = useMemo(
    () => repairCards.filter((card) => {
      const categoryMatched = categoryFilter === 'all' || card.category === categoryFilter;
      const queueMatched = matchesQueueFilter(card, approvals[card.id], statusOverrides[card.id], queueFilter);
      return categoryMatched && queueMatched;
    }),
    [approvals, categoryFilter, queueFilter, repairCards, statusOverrides],
  );

  const cards = useMemo(
    () => visibleRepairCards.map((card) => repairToIntelligenceCard(card, approvals[card.id], statusOverrides[card.id])),
    [approvals, statusOverrides, visibleRepairCards],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (cards.length === 0) {
      if (selectedId) setSelectedId('');
      return;
    }
    if (!cards.some((card) => card.id === selectedId)) setSelectedId(cards[0].id);
  }, [cards, selectedId]);

  const selected = useMemo(() => cards.find((card) => card.id === selectedId) || cards[0], [cards, selectedId]);
  const selectedSource = useMemo(() => repairCards.find((card) => card.id === selected?.id), [repairCards, selected?.id]);
  const selectedExecutionRecord = selected ? executionRecords[selected.id] : undefined;
  const selectedPreflightState = selected ? preflightChecks[selected.id] : undefined;
  const selectedServerPreflight = selected ? serverPreflightDryRuns[selected.id] : null;
  const selectedPreflightMessage = selected ? preflightMessages[selected.id] : '';
  const selectedServerImplementation = selected ? serverImplementationDryRuns[selected.id] : null;
  const selectedImplementationDryRunMessage = selected ? implementationDryRunMessages[selected.id] : '';
  const selectedServerDryRun = selected ? serverDryRuns[selected.id] : null;
  const selectedDryRunMessage = selected ? dryRunMessages[selected.id] : '';
  const selectedServerCancelDryRun = selected ? serverCancelDryRuns[selected.id] : null;
  const selectedCancelDryRunMessage = selected ? cancelDryRunMessages[selected.id] : '';
  const selectedFeedbackDraft = selected ? feedbackDrafts[selected.id] : null;
  const selectedTaskDraft = selected ? taskDrafts[selected.id] : null;
  const executionQueueItems = useMemo(() => Object.values(executionRecords)
    .map((record) => {
      const source = repairCards.find((card) => card.id === record.cardId);
      if (!source) return null;
      const proposal = dryRunProposalFromCard(source, approvals[source.id] || { status: record.status, updatedAt: record.updatedAt });
      return {
        record,
        payload: buildRepairExecutionExportPayload(source, record, proposal),
        category: source.category,
        severity: source.severity,
        owner: source.owner,
        readiness: source.readiness,
      };
    })
    .filter((item): item is RepairExecutionQueueItem => Boolean(item))
    .sort((a, b) => b.record.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.record.updatedAt)), [approvals, executionRecords, repairCards]);
  const highCount = repairCards.filter((card) => card.severity === 'high').length;
  const confirmedCount = Object.values(approvals).filter((approval) => approval.status === 'confirmed').length;
  const executionRecordCount = Object.keys(executionRecords).length;
  const persistenceRejectList = useMemo(
    () => Object.values(persistenceRejectReceipts).sort((a, b) => b.occurredAt.localeCompare(a.occurredAt)),
    [persistenceRejectReceipts],
  );
  const blockedActionCount = repairCards.reduce((sum, card) => sum + card.blocked.length, 0);
  const backendRepairCount = Array.isArray(apiRepair?.proposals) ? apiRepair.proposals.length : 0;
  const backendRepairSource = String(apiRepair?.source || apiRepair?.mode || '');
  const backendRepairIsFixture = Boolean(apiRepair?.isFixture ?? apiRepair?.is_fixture);
  const latestHandoffEntry = implementationHandoffLog[0] || null;
  const latestServerHandoff = latestHandoffEntry ? serverHandoffDryRuns[latestHandoffEntry.logId] : null;
  const latestHandoffDryRunMessage = latestHandoffEntry ? handoffDryRunMessages[latestHandoffEntry.logId] : '';
  const latestServerFutureWrite = latestHandoffEntry ? serverFutureWriteDryRuns[latestHandoffEntry.logId] : null;
  const latestFutureWriteDryRunMessage = latestHandoffEntry ? futureWriteDryRunMessages[latestHandoffEntry.logId] : '';

  useEffect(() => {
    setRecordCopyMessage('');
    setImplementationCopyMessage('');
    setHandoffLogMessage('');
    setFutureMappingCopyMessage('');
  }, [selectedId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setQueueCopyMessage('');
  }, [executionRecordCount]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!executionRecordCount) return;
    const stored = writeRepairExecutionStorage(executionRecords);
    setStoredQueueMeta(stored?.count ? { count: stored.count, updatedAt: stored.updatedAt } : null);
  }, [executionRecordCount, executionRecords]);

  const setApproval = useCallback((cardId: string, status: RepairApprovalStatus) => {
    setApprovals((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: { status, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }));
  }, []);

  const setExecutionRecord = useCallback((card: RepairCard, status: RepairApprovalStatus) => {
    setExecutionRecords((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [card.id]: buildRepairExecutionRecord(card, status, current[card.id]),
    }));
    setPreflightChecks((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [card.id]: current[card.id] || defaultRepairPreflightState(),
    }));
  }, []);

  const recordPersistenceReject = useCallback((
    error: unknown,
    scope: RepairRejectScope,
    targetId: string,
    title: string,
  ): RepairPersistenceRejectedReceipt | null => {
    const receipt = repairPersistenceRejectedFromError(error, scope, targetId, title);
    if (!receipt) return null;
    setPersistenceRejectReceipts((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [receipt.id]: receipt,
    }));
    return receipt;
  }, []);

  const requestPreflightDryRun = useCallback(async (cardId: string, record: RepairExecutionRecord, state: RepairPreflightState) => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setPreflightMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '本地 preflight 已更新；未登录，未请求后端 dry-run。' }));
      return;
    }
    setPreflightMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '正在同步后端 preflight dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await updateRepairPreflightDryRun(apiToken, record.proposalId, buildRepairPreflightPayload(state));
      setServerPreflightDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: response }));
      const gateOpen = Boolean(response.gateOpen ?? response.gate_open);
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      setPreflightMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: persisted
          ? '后端 preflight 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。'
          : gateOpen
            ? '后端 dry-run 确认：5/5 gate open，但仍未写库。'
            : '后端 dry-run 已同步：gate 仍关闭，未写库。',
      }));
    } catch (preflightError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(preflightError, 'preflight', record.proposalId, record.title);
      setPreflightMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : preflightError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 preflight dry-run 失败，本地检查已保留：${preflightError.message}`
          : '后端 preflight dry-run 失败，本地检查已保留。',
      }));
    }
  }, [apiToken, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const togglePreflightCheck = useCallback((cardId: string, key: RepairPreflightKey) => {
    const record = executionRecords[cardId];
    let nextStateForServer: RepairPreflightState | null = null;
    setPreflightChecks((current) => {
      const existing = current[cardId] || defaultRepairPreflightState();
      const nextState: RepairPreflightState = {
        ...existing,
        [key]: !existing[key],
      };
      nextStateForServer = nextState;
      return {
        ...current,
        [cardId]: nextState,
      };
    });
    if (record && nextStateForServer) void requestPreflightDryRun(cardId, record, nextStateForServer);
  }, [executionRecords, requestPreflightDryRun]);

  const updateCardStatus = useCallback((status: IntelligenceCardStatus, card: IntelligenceCardModel) => {
    setStatusOverrides((current) => ({ ...current, [card.id]: status }));
    setFeedbackDrafts((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [card.id]: buildIntelligenceFeedbackDraft(card, status, { pagePath: 'repairCenter' }),
    }));
    setTaskDrafts((current) => {
      const next = { ...current };
      if (status === 'accepted') {
        next[card.id] = buildIntelligenceTaskDraft(card, { pagePath: 'repairCenter' });
      } else {
        delete next[card.id];
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const requestProposalDryRun = useCallback(async (cardId: string, source: RepairCard) => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '本地提案已创建；未登录，未请求后端 dry-run。' }));
      return;
    }
    setDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '本地提案已创建；正在请求后端 dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await createRepairProposalDryRun(apiToken, buildRepairProposalCreatePayload(source));
      setServerDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: response }));
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      setDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: persisted ? '后端 dry-run 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。' : '后端 dry-run 已返回：未写库、未创建任务、未执行修复。',
      }));
    } catch (dryRunError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(dryRunError, 'proposal', repairProposalId(cardId), source.title);
      setDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : dryRunError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 dry-run 失败，本地提案已保留：${dryRunError.message}`
          : '后端 dry-run 失败，本地提案已保留。',
      }));
    }
  }, [apiToken, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const requestProposalCancelDryRun = useCallback(async (cardId: string, source: RepairCard, previousApproval?: RepairApproval) => {
    const proposalId = executionRecords[cardId]?.proposalId || repairProposalId(cardId);
    const cancelReason = `忽略本轮：${source.title}`;
    if (!apiToken) {
      setCancelDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '本地已忽略；未登录，未请求后端 cancel dry-run。' }));
      return;
    }
    setCancelDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '正在请求后端 cancel dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await cancelRepairProposalDryRun(apiToken, proposalId, {
        cancelReason,
        previousStatus: repairProposalStatusFromApproval(previousApproval),
        cardId,
        title: source.title,
      });
      setServerCancelDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: response }));
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      setCancelDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: persisted
          ? '后端 cancel dry-run 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。'
          : '后端 cancel dry-run 已返回：未写库、未写 audit、未创建任务。',
      }));
    } catch (cancelError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(cancelError, 'cancel', proposalId, source.title);
      setCancelDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : cancelError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 cancel dry-run 失败，本地忽略已保留：${cancelError.message}`
          : '后端 cancel dry-run 失败，本地忽略已保留。',
      }));
    }
  }, [apiToken, executionRecords, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const requestImplementationPackageDryRun = useCallback(async (
    cardId: string,
    source: RepairCard,
    record: RepairExecutionRecord,
    preflightState: RepairPreflightState,
  ) => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setImplementationDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '本地实现包已生成；未登录，未请求后端 package dry-run。' }));
      return null;
    }
    setImplementationDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: '正在请求后端 implementation package dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await createRepairImplementationPackageDryRun(
        apiToken,
        record.proposalId,
        buildRepairImplementationPackagePayload(source, record, preflightState, approvals[source.id]),
      );
      setServerImplementationDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [cardId]: response }));
      const gateOpen = Boolean(response.gateOpen ?? response.gate_open);
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      setImplementationDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: persisted
          ? '后端 package dry-run 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。'
          : gateOpen
            ? '后端 package dry-run 已返回：gate ready，但仍未写库、未执行。'
            : '后端 package dry-run 已返回：gate blocked，未写库、未执行。',
      }));
      return response;
    } catch (packageError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(packageError, 'implementation', record.proposalId, source.title);
      setImplementationDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [cardId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : packageError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 package dry-run 失败，本地实现包已保留：${packageError.message}`
          : '后端 package dry-run 失败，本地实现包已保留。',
      }));
      return null;
    }
  }, [apiToken, approvals, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const requestHandoffDryRun = useCallback(async (entry: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry) => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setHandoffDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: '本地交接历史已写入；未登录，未请求后端 handoff dry-run。' }));
      return null;
    }
    setHandoffDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: '正在请求后端 handoff dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await createRepairHandoffDryRun(apiToken, entry.packageId, {
        payload: entry.payload as unknown as Row,
        copiedAt: entry.copiedAt,
      });
      setServerHandoffDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: response }));
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      setHandoffDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [entry.logId]: persisted
          ? '后端 handoff dry-run 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。'
          : '后端 handoff dry-run 已返回：未写库、未写 audit、未建任务。',
      }));
      return response;
    } catch (handoffError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(handoffError, 'handoff', entry.logId, entry.title);
      setHandoffDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [entry.logId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : handoffError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 handoff dry-run 失败，本地交接历史已保留：${handoffError.message}`
          : '后端 handoff dry-run 失败，本地交接历史已保留。',
      }));
      return null;
    }
  }, [apiToken, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const requestFutureWritePreviewDryRun = useCallback(async (entry: RepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry) => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setFutureWriteDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: '本地映射已生成；未登录，未请求后端 future-write dry-run。' }));
      return null;
    }
    setFutureWriteDryRunMessages((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: '正在请求后端 future-write dry-run...' }));
    try {
      const response = await createRepairFutureWritePreviewDryRun(apiToken, entry.logId, {
        handoffEntry: entry as unknown as Row,
      });
      setServerFutureWriteDryRuns((current) => ({ ...current, [entry.logId]: response }));
      const persisted = Boolean(response.persisted);
      const writeMode = String(response.writeMode || response.write_mode || 'preview_only');
      setFutureWriteDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [entry.logId]: persisted
          ? '后端 future-write dry-run 返回异常：persisted=true，请停止后续执行。'
          : `后端 future-write dry-run 已返回：${writeMode}，未写 audit、未建任务、未写 evidence。`,
      }));
      return response;
    } catch (futureWriteError) {
      const receipt = recordPersistenceReject(futureWriteError, 'future_write', entry.logId, entry.title);
      setFutureWriteDryRunMessages((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [entry.logId]: receipt
          ? `真实保存被阻止：${receipt.reason} · writeDb=${String(receipt.writeDb)}`
          : futureWriteError instanceof Error
            ? `后端 future-write dry-run 失败，本地映射已保留：${futureWriteError.message}`
          : '后端 future-write dry-run 失败，本地映射已保留。',
      }));
      return null;
    }
