  }, [apiToken, recordPersistenceReject]);

  const handleProposalAction = useCallback((status: RepairApprovalStatus) => {
    if (!selected || !selectedSource) return;
    const previousApproval = approvals[selected.id];
    setApproval(selected.id, status);
    setExecutionRecord(selectedSource, status);
    if (status === 'drafted') void requestProposalDryRun(selected.id, selectedSource);
    if (status === 'confirmed') updateCardStatus('accepted', selected);
    if (status === 'dismissed') {
      updateCardStatus('rejected', selected);
      void requestProposalCancelDryRun(selected.id, selectedSource, previousApproval);
    }
  }, [approvals, requestProposalCancelDryRun, requestProposalDryRun, selected, selectedSource, setApproval, setExecutionRecord, updateCardStatus]);

  const handleCopyExecutionRecord = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedSource || !selectedExecutionRecord) {
      setRecordCopyMessage('请先生成本地提案。');
      return;
    }
    const proposal = dryRunProposalFromCard(selectedSource, approvals[selectedSource.id]);
    const payload = buildRepairExecutionExportPayload(selectedSource, selectedExecutionRecord, proposal);
    try {
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setRecordCopyMessage('JSON 草案已复制。');
    } catch (copyError) {
      setRecordCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请展开 JSON 后手动复制。');
    }
  }, [approvals, selectedExecutionRecord, selectedSource]);

  const handleCopyExecutionQueue = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!executionQueueItems.length) {
      setQueueCopyMessage('当前没有本地提案可复制。');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairExecutionQueueExportPayload(executionQueueItems);
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setQueueCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(executionQueueItems.length)} 条本地提案。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setQueueCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请逐条展开 JSON 后手动复制。');
    }
  }, [executionQueueItems]);

  const handleCopyImplementationPackage = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedSource || !selectedExecutionRecord) {
      setImplementationCopyMessage('请先生成本地提案。');
      return;
    }
    const currentPreflight = selectedPreflightState || defaultRepairPreflightState();
    if (!repairPreflightComplete(currentPreflight)) {
      setImplementationCopyMessage('执行前检查未完成，不能复制实现包。');
      return;
    }
    try {
      await requestImplementationPackageDryRun(
        selectedSource.id,
        selectedSource,
        selectedExecutionRecord,
        currentPreflight,
      );
      const payload = buildRepairImplementationExportPayload(
        selectedSource,
        selectedExecutionRecord,
        currentPreflight,
        approvals[selectedSource.id],
      );
      const entry = buildRepairImplementationHandoffLogEntry(payload);
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setImplementationCopyMessage('实现包 JSON 已复制。');
      setImplementationHandoffLog((current) => {
        const next = [entry, ...current.filter((item) => item.packageId !== entry.packageId)].slice(0, 30);
        writeRepairImplementationHandoffLog(next);
        return next;
      });
      setHandoffLogMessage('已写入本地交接历史。');
      void requestHandoffDryRun(entry);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setImplementationCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [approvals, requestHandoffDryRun, requestImplementationPackageDryRun, selectedExecutionRecord, selectedPreflightState, selectedSource]);

  const handleClearImplementationHandoffLog = useCallback(() => {
    clearRepairImplementationHandoffLog();
    setImplementationHandoffLog([]);
    setServerHandoffDryRuns({});
    setHandoffDryRunMessages({});
    setServerFutureWriteDryRuns({});
    setFutureWriteDryRunMessages({});
    setHandoffLogMessage('交接历史已清空。');
    setFutureMappingCopyMessage('');
    setExecutionEvidenceCopyMessage('');
    setPreExecutionRunbookCopyMessage('');
    setV0ClosureReportCopyMessage('');
    setV0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage('');
    setBackendContractCopyMessage('');
    setExecutionApprovalCopyMessage('');
    setFinalHandoffManifestCopyMessage('');
    setPreviewCloseoutCopyMessage('');
    setFirstSliceCandidateCopyMessage('');
    setSchemaDesignCopyMessage('');
  }, []);

  const handleCopyFutureWriteMapping = useCallback(async () => {
    const latest = implementationHandoffLog[0];
    if (!latest) {
      setFutureMappingCopyMessage('没有可复制的映射。');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairFutureWriteMappingExportPayload(latest);
      await requestFutureWritePreviewDryRun(latest);
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setFutureMappingCopyMessage('映射 JSON 已复制。');
    } catch (copyError) {
      setFutureMappingCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [implementationHandoffLog, requestFutureWritePreviewDryRun]);

  const handleCopyExecutionEvidencePackage = useCallback(async (item: RepairControlledExecutionItem) => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairExecutionEvidencePackage(item);
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setExecutionEvidenceCopyMessage(`${item.entry.packageId} 证据包已复制；仍不执行、不写库。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setExecutionEvidenceCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, []);

  const handleCopyPreExecutionRunbookPackage = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairPreExecutionStatePackage({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setPreExecutionRunbookCopyMessage(`已复制 ${payload.decision === 'go_candidate' ? 'GO-CANDIDATE' : 'NO-GO'} 状态包；仍不执行、不写库。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setPreExecutionRunbookCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyV0ClosureReport = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairV0ClosureReport({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setV0ClosureReportCopyMessage(`已复制 ${payload.decision === 'closed_no_write' ? 'V0 CLOSED' : 'NO-GO'} 闭环报告；仍不执行、不写库。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setV0ClosureReportCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyV0AcceptanceGapReport = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairV0AcceptanceGapReport({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setV0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage(`已复制 ${payload.decision === 'go_candidate' ? 'GO-CANDIDATE' : 'NO-WRITE'} 缺口报告；仍不执行、不写库。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setV0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyBackendPersistenceContract = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairBackendPersistenceContract({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setBackendContractCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(payload.summary.fieldCount)} 个字段的后端契约；writeMode=${payload.writeMode}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setBackendContractCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyExecutionApprovalPackage = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairExecutionApprovalPackage({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setExecutionApprovalCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(payload.summary.stepCount)} 项审批清单；decision=${payload.decision}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setExecutionApprovalCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyFinalHandoffManifest = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairFinalHandoffManifest({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setFinalHandoffManifestCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(payload.summary.roundCount)} 轮最终 Manifest；decision=${payload.decision}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setFinalHandoffManifestCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyPreviewCloseoutReport = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairPreviewCloseoutReport({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setPreviewCloseoutCopyMessage(`已复制收口报告；decision=${payload.decision}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setPreviewCloseoutCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyFirstExecutionSliceCandidate = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidate({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setFirstSliceCandidateCopyMessage(`已复制第一切片候选；decision=${payload.decision}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setFirstSliceCandidateCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopySchemaMigrationDesign = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairSchemaMigrationDesign({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setSchemaDesignCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(payload.summary.tableCount)} 张表的 schema 设计；writeMode=${payload.writeMode}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setSchemaDesignCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handleCopyMigrationReviewPacket = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const payload = buildRepairMigrationReviewPacket({
        closureStatus,
        readiness: persistenceReadiness,
        entries: implementationHandoffLog,
        executionQueueItems,
        hasApiToken: Boolean(apiToken),
        repairCandidateCount: repairCards.length,
      });
      await copyTextToClipboard(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      setMigrationReviewCopyMessage(`已复制 ${compact(payload.summary.upStatementCount)} 条 up SQL 草案；writeMode=${payload.writeMode}。`);
    } catch (copyError) {
      setMigrationReviewCopyMessage(copyError instanceof Error ? copyError.message : '复制失败，请稍后重试。');
    }
  }, [apiToken, closureStatus, executionQueueItems, implementationHandoffLog, persistenceReadiness, repairCards.length]);

  const handlePreviewPersistenceApprovalEnvelope = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!apiToken) {
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage('未登录 token，不能请求后端审批 envelope dry-run。');
      return;
    }
    setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading(true);
    setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage('正在校验审批 envelope dry-run...');
    try {
      const response = await previewRepairPersistenceApprovalEnvelope(apiToken, {
        targetRoute: '/api/admin/vkpi/repair/proposals?persist=true',
        requestedPersist: true,
      });
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelope(response);
      const missing = numberValue(response.missingCount ?? response.missing_count);
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage(`dry-run 已返回：缺失 ${compact(missing)} 项审批，writeAllowed=false。`);
    } catch (approvalError) {
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelope(null);
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage(approvalError instanceof Error ? approvalError.message : '审批 envelope dry-run 失败；仍保持不写库。');
    } finally {
      setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading(false);
    }
  }, [apiToken]);

  const handleRestoreExecutionQueue = useCallback(() => {
    const stored = readRepairExecutionStorage();
    if (!stored?.count) {
      setStorageMessage('没有可恢复的本地队列。');
      return;
    }
    setExecutionRecords(stored.records);
    setApprovals(buildRepairApprovalsFromRecords(stored.records));
    setStatusOverrides(buildRepairStatusOverridesFromRecords(stored.records));
    setPreflightChecks(Object.keys(stored.records).reduce((acc, cardId) => {
      acc[cardId] = defaultRepairPreflightState();
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, RepairPreflightState>));
    setStoredQueueMeta({ count: stored.count, updatedAt: stored.updatedAt });
    setStorageMessage(`已恢复 ${compact(stored.count)} 条本地提案。`);
    const firstRecord = Object.values(stored.records).sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt))[0];
    if (firstRecord) {
      setCategoryFilter('all');
      setQueueFilter('all');
      setSelectedId(firstRecord.cardId);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleClearExecutionQueue = useCallback(() => {
    clearRepairExecutionStorage();
    setExecutionRecords({});
    setPreflightChecks({});
    setServerPreflightDryRuns({});
    setPreflightMessages({});
    setServerImplementationDryRuns({});
    setImplementationDryRunMessages({});
    setServerHandoffDryRuns({});
    setHandoffDryRunMessages({});
    setServerFutureWriteDryRuns({});
    setFutureWriteDryRunMessages({});
    setServerCancelDryRuns({});
    setCancelDryRunMessages({});
    setServerDryRuns({});
    setDryRunMessages({});
    setPersistenceRejectReceipts({});
    setApprovals({});
    setStatusOverrides({});
    setFeedbackDrafts({});
    setTaskDrafts({});
    setStoredQueueMeta(null);
    setRecordCopyMessage('');
    setQueueCopyMessage('');
    setImplementationCopyMessage('');
    setFutureMappingCopyMessage('');
    setExecutionEvidenceCopyMessage('');
    setPreExecutionRunbookCopyMessage('');
    setV0ClosureReportCopyMessage('');
    setV0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage('');
    setBackendContractCopyMessage('');
    setExecutionApprovalCopyMessage('');
    setFinalHandoffManifestCopyMessage('');
    setPreviewCloseoutCopyMessage('');
    setFirstSliceCandidateCopyMessage('');
    setSchemaDesignCopyMessage('');
    setMigrationReviewCopyMessage('');
    setPersistenceApprovalEnvelope(null);
    setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage('');
    setPersistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading(false);
    setStorageMessage('本地队列已清空。');
  }, []);

  const handleAction = useCallback((action: IntelligenceAction, card: IntelligenceCardModel) => {
    if (action.kind === 'disabled') return;
    if (action.label.includes('证据')) {
      setEvidenceDrawer(card);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(card.id);
    const source = repairCards.find((item) => item.id === card.id);
    if (action.label.includes('本地提案')) {
      setApproval(card.id, 'drafted');
      if (source) setExecutionRecord(source, 'drafted');
      if (source) void requestProposalDryRun(card.id, source);
    }
    if (action.label.includes('人工确认')) {
      setApproval(card.id, 'confirmed');
      if (source) setExecutionRecord(source, 'confirmed');
      updateCardStatus('accepted', card);
    }
    if (action.label.includes('忽略')) {
      const previousApproval = approvals[card.id];
      setApproval(card.id, 'dismissed');
      if (source) setExecutionRecord(source, 'dismissed');
      updateCardStatus('rejected', card);
      if (source) void requestProposalCancelDryRun(card.id, source, previousApproval);
    }
  }, [approvals, repairCards, requestProposalCancelDryRun, requestProposalDryRun, setApproval, setExecutionRecord, updateCardStatus]);

  return (
    <div className="vkpi-agents-shell vkpi-repair-center">
      <section className="vkpi-agents-hero vkpi-repair-hero">
        <div>
          <span>V2 Repair Center · 只读自动化</span>
          <h1>自动化修复控制台</h1>
          <p>把问题、证据、建议动作和人工确认放在一个页面；当前阶段只生成本地提案，不自动改生产。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-agents-hero__meta">
          <b>0</b>
          <small>自动生产变更</small>
        </div>
      </section>

      <RepairCapabilityStatusStrip
        executionRecordCount={executionRecordCount}
        handoffLogCount={implementationHandoffLog.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
      />

      <RepairPersistenceModeBanner
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
      />

      <RepairPersistenceRejectPanel
        receipts={persistenceRejectList}
        onClear={() => setPersistenceRejectReceipts({})}
      />

      <RepairEndpointContractPanel />

      <RepairAcceptanceCheckpointPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
      />

      <RepairMinimumExecutionReadinessPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
      />

      <RepairPreExecutionRunbookPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={preExecutionRunbookCopyMessage}
        onCopyPackage={handleCopyPreExecutionRunbookPackage}
      />

      <RepairV0ClosureReportPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={v0ClosureReportCopyMessage}
        onCopyReport={handleCopyV0ClosureReport}
      />

      <RepairV0AcceptanceGapPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={v0AcceptanceGapCopyMessage}
        onCopyReport={handleCopyV0AcceptanceGapReport}
      />

      <RepairBackendPersistenceContractPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={backendContractCopyMessage}
        onCopyContract={handleCopyBackendPersistenceContract}
      />

      <RepairExecutionApprovalPackagePanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={executionApprovalCopyMessage}
        onCopyApproval={handleCopyExecutionApprovalPackage}
      />

      <RepairFinalHandoffManifestPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={finalHandoffManifestCopyMessage}
        onCopyManifest={handleCopyFinalHandoffManifest}
      />

      <RepairPreviewCloseoutReportPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={previewCloseoutCopyMessage}
        onCopyReport={handleCopyPreviewCloseoutReport}
      />

      <RepairFirstExecutionSliceCandidatePanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={firstSliceCandidateCopyMessage}
        onCopyCandidate={handleCopyFirstExecutionSliceCandidate}
      />

      <RepairSchemaMigrationDesignPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={schemaDesignCopyMessage}
        onCopyDesign={handleCopySchemaMigrationDesign}
      />

      <RepairMigrationReviewPacketPanel
        closureStatus={closureStatus}
        readiness={persistenceReadiness}
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        executionQueueItems={executionQueueItems}
        repairCandidateCount={repairCards.length}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        copyMessage={migrationReviewCopyMessage}
        onCopyPacket={handleCopyMigrationReviewPacket}
      />

      <RepairPersistenceBoundaryPanel boundary={persistenceBoundary} />

      <RepairPersistenceApprovalEnvelopePanel
        envelope={persistenceApprovalEnvelope}
        message={persistenceApprovalEnvelopeMessage}
        hasApiToken={Boolean(apiToken)}
        loading={persistenceApprovalEnvelopeLoading}
        onDryRun={handlePreviewPersistenceApprovalEnvelope}
      />

      <RepairMigrationPreflightManifestPanel manifest={migrationPreflightManifest} />

      <RepairMigrationDraftPackagePanel draftPackage={migrationDraftPackage} />

      <RepairClosureStatusPanel status={closureStatus} />

      <RepairPersistenceReadinessPanel readiness={persistenceReadiness} />

      <RepairAuditWritePreviewPanel preview={auditWritePreview} />

      <RepairTaskDraftPreviewPanel preview={taskDraftPreview} />

      <RepairEvidenceRefPreviewPanel preview={evidenceRefPreview} />

      <section className="vkpi-repair-metrics" aria-label="修复中心状态">
        <article><span>问题总数</span><b>{compact(repairCards.length)}</b><small>{loading ? '读取诊断中' : `当前显示 ${compact(cards.length)} 条`}</small></article>
        <article><span>高优先级</span><b>{compact(highCount)}</b><small>需要先看证据</small></article>
        <article><span>本地记录</span><b>{compact(executionRecordCount)}</b><small>{compact(confirmedCount)} 条已确认</small></article>
        <article><span>禁止动作</span><b>{compact(blockedActionCount)}</b><small>provider / 迁移 / 部署受控</small></article>
      </section>

      <section className="vkpi-repair-guard">
        <div>
          <b>执行边界</b>
          <span>允许 UI、文案、只读诊断和本地 draft；禁止自动同步、批量 LLM/Gemini、迁移、权限变更和生产部署。</span>
        </div>
        <em>{apiToken ? '可读真实诊断' : '本地诊断模式'}</em>
      </section>

      <section className="vkpi-repair-guard">
        <div>
          <b>后端提案源</b>
          <span>
            {backendRepairCount > 0
              ? `已读取 ${compact(backendRepairCount)} 条只读提案，来源 ${backendRepairSource || 'repair endpoint'}；当前仍不写库、不执行修复。`
              : '未读到后端 Repair proposal，页面继续使用本地诊断卡片。'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <em>{backendRepairCount > 0 ? (backendRepairIsFixture ? 'Fixture 只读' : 'API 只读') : 'Fallback'}</em>
      </section>

      {roadmapFocus ? (
        <section className="vkpi-repair-guard vkpi-repair-guard--focus">
          <div>
            <b>来自未来智能层路线图</b>
            <span>{roadmapFocus.title} · {roadmapFocus.statusLabel} · 当前只生成接入前检查，不运行 Google / Reddit / Gemini / LLM。</span>
          </div>
          <em>{roadmapFocus.readiness}</em>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {error ? <div className="vkpi-inline-message is-warning">{error}</div> : null}

      <RepairExecutionQueuePanel
        items={executionQueueItems}
        copyMessage={queueCopyMessage}
        storageMeta={storedQueueMeta}
        storageMessage={storageMessage}
        onCopyQueue={handleCopyExecutionQueue}
        onRestoreQueue={handleRestoreExecutionQueue}
        onClearQueue={handleClearExecutionQueue}
        onSelectRecord={(cardId) => {
          setCategoryFilter('all');
          setQueueFilter('all');
          setSelectedId(cardId);
        }}
      />

      <RepairImplementationHandoffLogPanel
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        message={handoffLogMessage}
        serverHandoff={latestServerHandoff}
        dryRunMessage={latestHandoffDryRunMessage}
        serverFutureWrite={latestServerFutureWrite}
        futureWriteDryRunMessage={latestFutureWriteDryRunMessage}
        mappingCopyMessage={futureMappingCopyMessage}
        onClear={handleClearImplementationHandoffLog}
        onCopyFutureMapping={handleCopyFutureWriteMapping}
        onSelectRecord={(cardId) => {
          setCategoryFilter('all');
          setQueueFilter('all');
          setSelectedId(cardId);
        }}
      />

      <RepairControlledExecutionQueuePanel
        entries={implementationHandoffLog}
        copyMessage={executionEvidenceCopyMessage}
        onCopyEvidencePackage={handleCopyExecutionEvidencePackage}
        onSelectRecord={(cardId) => {
          setCategoryFilter('all');
          setQueueFilter('all');
          setSelectedId(cardId);
        }}
      />

      <section className="vkpi-repair-queue-tools" aria-label="修复队列筛选">
        <div className="vkpi-repair-filter-group">
          <strong>缺口类型</strong>
          <div>
            {repairCategoryFilters.map((item) => (
              <button
                className={`vkpi-repair-filter-button${categoryFilter === item.key ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
                key={item.key}
                onClick={() => setCategoryFilter(item.key)}
                type="button"
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                <b>{compact(categoryCounts[item.key] || 0)}</b>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="vkpi-repair-filter-group">
          <strong>处理状态</strong>
          <div>
            {repairQueueFilters.map((item) => (
              <button
                className={`vkpi-repair-filter-button${queueFilter === item.key ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
                key={item.key}
                onClick={() => setQueueFilter(item.key)}
                type="button"
              >
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                <b>{compact(queueCounts[item.key] || 0)}</b>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="vkpi-agents-grid">
        <div className="vkpi-agents-list">
          {cards.length > 0 ? (
            cards.map((card) => (
              <IntelligenceCard
                card={card}
                key={card.id}
                selected={selected?.id === card.id}
                onSelect={(next) => setSelectedId(next.id)}
              />
            ))
          ) : (
            <div className="vkpi-repair-queue-empty">
              <b>当前筛选下没有修复项</b>
              <span>切换缺口类型或处理状态查看其他待办；本页不会自动执行任何修复。</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <IntelligenceDetailPanel
          card={selected}
          eyebrow="修复策略"
          emptyTitle="执行边界"
          emptySummary="Repair Center v0 只允许诊断、提案和人工确认。"
          metaItems={[
            { label: 'Provider', value: '禁止自动调用' },
            { label: 'Gemini', value: '禁止批量运行' },
            { label: '迁移', value: '必须人工审批' },
            { label: '部署', value: '必须备份优先' },
          ]}
          extraSlot={(
            <>
              <RepairProposalPanel
                card={selected}
                source={selectedSource}
                approval={selected ? approvals[selected.id] : undefined}
                record={selectedExecutionRecord}
                serverDryRun={selectedServerDryRun}
                serverCancelDryRun={selectedServerCancelDryRun}
                dryRunMessage={selectedDryRunMessage}
                cancelDryRunMessage={selectedCancelDryRunMessage}
                copyMessage={recordCopyMessage}
                onDraft={() => handleProposalAction('drafted')}
                onConfirm={() => handleProposalAction('confirmed')}
                onDismiss={() => handleProposalAction('dismissed')}
                onCopyRecord={handleCopyExecutionRecord}
              />
              <RepairPreflightPanel
                record={selectedExecutionRecord}
                state={selectedPreflightState}
                serverPreflight={selectedServerPreflight}
                preflightMessage={selectedPreflightMessage}
                onToggle={(key) => {
                  if (selected) togglePreflightCheck(selected.id, key);
                }}
              />
              <RepairImplementationPreviewPanel
                source={selectedSource}
                approval={selected ? approvals[selected.id] : undefined}
                record={selectedExecutionRecord}
                preflightState={selectedPreflightState}
                serverPackage={selectedServerImplementation}
                packageMessage={selectedImplementationDryRunMessage}
                copyMessage={implementationCopyMessage}
                onCopyPackage={handleCopyImplementationPackage}
              />
              <RepairPackagePanel
                source={selectedSource}
                approval={selected ? approvals[selected.id] : undefined}
              />
              <IntelligenceActionStrip
                card={selected}
                status={selected ? statusOverrides[selected.id] || selected.status : undefined}
                statusHint="本页只记录本地处理状态；不会自动执行修复。"
                onStatusChange={updateCardStatus}
              />
              <IntelligenceFeedbackDraftPanel draft={selectedFeedbackDraft} />
              <IntelligenceTaskDraftPanel draft={selectedTaskDraft} viewMode={viewMode} />
            </>
          )}
          footerNote="Repair Center 是自动化修复的前置控制台：先看证据、再生成提案、最后人工确认；真正执行另走受控流程。"
          onAction={handleAction}
        />
      </section>

      {evidenceDrawer ? (
        <IntelligenceEvidenceDrawer
          card={evidenceDrawer}
          onClose={() => setEvidenceDrawer(null)}
          footerNote="Repair Center 的证据只用于人工确认修复范围；dry-run patch 接入前不会自动执行任何变更。"
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export default RepairCenterPage;
